Future<void> main() async {
  WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
  if (!kIsWeb) {
    await SystemChrome.setPreferredOrientations([DeviceOrientation.portraitUp]);
  }
  SystemChrome.setSystemUIOverlayStyle(const SystemUiOverlayStyle(
    statusBarColor: Colors.transparent,
    statusBarIconBrightness: Brightness.light,
    systemNavigationBarColor: midnight,
    systemNavigationBarIconBrightness: Brightness.light,
  ));
  try {
    await Firebase.initializeApp(
      options: DefaultFirebaseOptions.currentPlatform,
    );
    AuthService.instance.init();
  } catch (e) {
    debugPrint('Firebase unavailable: $e');
  }
  await GameAudio.instance.initialize();
  runApp(const ScamouflageApp());
}

class ScamouflageApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const ScamouflageApp({super.key});
  @override
  State<ScamouflageApp> createState() => _ScamouflageAppState();
}

class _ScamouflageAppState extends State<ScamouflageApp> with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  late final GameEngine game;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    game = GameEngine();
    game.load().then((_) => CloudSaveService.instance.attach(game));
  }

  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    GameAudio.instance.appVisible(state == AppLifecycleState.resumed);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    game.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => MaterialApp(
        title: 'Scamouflage: Trust or Bust',
        debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
        theme: ThemeData(
          useMaterial3: true,
          brightness: Brightness.dark,
          scaffoldBackgroundColor: midnight,
          colorScheme: const ColorScheme.dark(
            primary: mustard,
            secondary: mint,
            surface: deskBlue,
            onPrimary: coal,
            onSurface: parchment,
          ),
          textTheme: GoogleFonts.spaceGroteskTextTheme(ThemeData.dark().textTheme),
        ),
        home: GameScreens(game: game),
      );
}
n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\controllers\arcade_session.dart =====nimport 'dart:math';
import '../services/arcade_generator.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// One play session, independent of Flutter widgets. v5 GameEngine is the
/// only permanent career-XP store. Tests use deterministic seeds.
class ArcadeSession {
  final ArcadeDifficulty difficulty;
  final ArcadeMode? selectedMode;
  final bool firstDay;
  final int? roundLimit;
  final void Function(int) awardCareerXp;
  final int Function() careerXp;
  final void Function(String)? onCaseGenerated;
  final Set<String> history;
  final ArcadeGenerator generator;
  final Set<String> signatures = <String>{};
  final List<ArcadeMode> recentModes = [];
  static const firstDayModes = [
    ArcadeMode.trust, ArcadeMode.impostor, ArcadeMode.scamSlam,
    ArcadeMode.inbox, ArcadeMode.comments,
  ];

  late ArcadeCase current;
  int hearts;
  int score = 0;
  int round = 0;
  int successful = 0;
  int streak = 0;
  int bestStreak = 0;
  int lastEarnedXp = 0;
  int lastParts = 0;
  bool lastUsedDecisive = false;
  bool finished = false;
  bool waitingForNext = false;
  bool? lastCorrect;
  List<int> lastAnswer = const [];

  ArcadeSession({
    required this.difficulty,
    required this.selectedMode,
    required this.awardCareerXp,
    int Function()? careerXp,
    this.roundLimit,
    this.firstDay = false,
    this.onCaseGenerated,
    Set<String> recentHistory = const {},
    int? seed,
  }) : hearts = difficulty.startingHearts,
       careerXp = careerXp ?? (() => 0),
       generator = ArcadeGenerator(seed ?? DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch),
       history = Set<String>.from(recentHistory) {
    _deal();
  }

  ArcadeMode _chooseMode() {
    if (firstDay) return firstDayModes[round.clamp(0, 4).toInt()];
    if (selectedMode != null) return selectedMode!;
    final unlocked = ArcadeMode.values
        .where((mode) => careerXp() >= mode.unlockXp).toList();
    final candidates = unlocked.where((mode) =>
        recentModes.isEmpty || mode != recentModes.last).toList();
    final pool = candidates.isEmpty ? unlocked : candidates;
    final fresh = pool.where((mode) => !recentModes.contains(mode)).toList();
    return generator.pick(fresh.isNotEmpty ? fresh : pool);
  }

  void _deal() {
    final kind = _chooseMode();
    // Recent families are drawn from both saved and current-run history.
    // Avoiding a family blocks the same underlying task despite new names or
    // amounts. Limit exclusion to keep short modes (Rumor's five puzzles)
    // playable once each archetype has been seen.
    final recent = [...history, ...signatures].reversed.toList();
    final blockedFamilies = <String>{};
    final familyPrefix = switch (kind) {
      ArcadeMode.trust => 'trust:',
      ArcadeMode.impostor => 'impostor:',
      ArcadeMode.scamSlam => 'chat:',
      ArcadeMode.rumor => 'rumor:',
      ArcadeMode.inbox => 'inbox:',
      ArcadeMode.comments => 'thread:',
      ArcadeMode.clip => 'clip:',
    };
    for (final signature in recent) {
      if (signature.startsWith(familyPrefix)) {
        blockedFamilies.add(ArcadeGenerator.familyOfSignature(signature));
        if (blockedFamilies.length >= min(4, ArcadeGenerator.scenarioCount(kind) - 1)) {
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    current = generator.generate(kind, firstDay ? ArcadeDifficulty.easy : difficulty,
      exclude: {...history, ...signatures},
      excludeFamilies: blockedFamilies, tutorial: firstDay);
    signatures.add(current.signature);
    onCaseGenerated?.call(current.signature);
    recentModes.add(kind);
    if (recentModes.length > 4) recentModes.removeAt(0);
  }

  int get totalAnswered => round;
  int get totalCases => firstDay ? firstDayModes.length : roundLimit ?? 999;

  /// Single-choice convenience.
  bool answer(int optionIndex, {bool inspected = false}) {
    if (optionIndex < 0 || optionIndex >= current.choices.length &&
        current.choices.isNotEmpty) {
      throw RangeError.index(optionIndex, current.choices);
    }
    return submit([optionIndex], usedDecisive: inspected);
  }

  /// Scores an answer in the current mode's own format (see
  /// [ArcadeCase.correct]). One answer per case; XP never decreases, errors
  /// cost a heart. The first day is forgiving so a beginner is never locked
  /// out. Inbox Sprint earns partial XP but any wrong swipe costs a heart.
  bool submit(List<int> answer, {bool usedDecisive = false}) {
    if (finished || waitingForNext) return lastCorrect ?? false;
    final c = current;
    final parts = c.totalParts;
    final right = c.matches(answer);
    final correct = c.isBatch ? right == parts : c.isCorrect(answer);
    lastAnswer = List.unmodifiable(answer);
    lastCorrect = correct;
    lastParts = right;
    lastUsedDecisive = usedDecisive &&
        (c.mode == ArcadeMode.trust || c.mode == ArcadeMode.clip);
    lastEarnedXp = 0;
    waitingForNext = true;
    round++;
    final full = 35 + difficulty.bonusXp + (usedDecisive && (c.mode == ArcadeMode.trust || c.mode == ArcadeMode.clip)
          ? 15 : 0) +
        min(streak, 4) * 3;
    var gained = 0;
    if (correct) {
      gained = c.isBatch ? full + 10 : full;
      successful++;
      streak++;
      bestStreak = max(bestStreak, streak);
    } else {
      if (c.isBatch && right > 0) gained = (full * right / parts / 2).round();
      streak = 0;
      hearts = firstDay ? max(1, hearts - 1) : max(0, hearts - 1);
    }
    if (gained > 0) {
      score += gained;
      lastEarnedXp = gained;
      awardCareerXp(gained);
    }
    if ((firstDay && round >= firstDayModes.length) ||
        (!firstDay && (hearts == 0 || (roundLimit != null && round >= roundLimit!)))) {
      finished = true;
    }
    return correct;
  }

  void next() {
    if (finished || !waitingForNext) return;
    waitingForNext = false;
    lastAnswer = const [];
    lastCorrect = null;
    lastEarnedXp = 0;
    lastUsedDecisive = false;
    _deal();
  }

  void endEarly() => finished = true;
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\controllers\engine.dart =====nimport 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import '../services/generator.dart';
import '../models/models.dart';
import '../models/player_progression.dart';

/// Game state is shared by Android, iOS and web. No networking is required.
class GameEngine extends ChangeNotifier {
  bool ready = false;
  bool tutorialFinished = false;
  bool arcadeIntroFinished = false;
  bool haptics = true;
  int unlockedShift = 1;
  int careerXp = 0;
  String equippedTitleId = CareerProgression.ranks.first.id;
  String equippedBadgeId = CareerProgression.badges.first.id;
  String equippedFrameId = CareerProgression.frames.first.id;
  String equippedMascotNameId = CareerProgression.mascotNames.first.id;
  String equippedMascotColorId = CareerProgression.mascotColors.first.id;
  bool mascotIntroduced = false;
  final List<int> _pendingLevelUps = [];
  String get mascotName => CareerProgression.mascotNameById(equippedMascotNameId)?.name ?? 'ChameLeo';
  int? takeLevelUp() {
    if (_pendingLevelUps.isEmpty) return null;
    final highest = _pendingLevelUps.last;
    _pendingLevelUps.clear();
    return highest;
  }
  Future<void> introduceMascot() async {
    mascotIntroduced = true;
    notifyListeners();
    await _save();
  }
  bool equipMascotName(String id) {
    final choice = CareerProgression.mascotNameById(id);
    if (choice == null || choice.xp > careerXp) return false;
    equippedMascotNameId = id;
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
    return true;
  }
  bool equipMascotColor(String id) {
    final choice = CareerProgression.mascotColorById(id);
    if (choice == null || choice.xp > careerXp) return false;
    equippedMascotColorId = id;
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
    return true;
  }
  CareerRank get careerRank => CareerProgression.rankAt(careerXp);
  CareerRank? get nextCareerRank => CareerProgression.nextRank(careerXp);
  double get careerLevelProgress => CareerProgression.levelProgress(careerXp);
  String get equippedTitle =>
      CareerProgression.rankById(equippedTitleId)?.title ?? careerRank.title;

  bool equipCareerTitle(String id) {
    final choice = CareerProgression.rankById(id);
    if (choice == null || choice.xp > careerXp) return false;
    equippedTitleId = id;
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
    return true;
  }

  bool equipBadge(String id) {
    final choice = CareerProgression.badgeById(id);
    if (choice == null || choice.xp > careerXp) return false;
    equippedBadgeId = id;
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
    return true;
  }

  bool equipFrame(String id) {
    final choice = CareerProgression.frameById(id);
    if (choice == null || choice.xp > careerXp) return false;
    equippedFrameId = id;
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
    return true;
  }
  int hintsUsed = 0;
  /// One free starter hint, then one new hint for each 50 career XP earned.
  int get hintsAvailable => max<int>(0, 1 + careerXp ~/ 50 - hintsUsed);

  bool spendHint() {
    if (hintsAvailable == 0) return false;
    hintsUsed++;
    _save();
    notifyListeners();
    return true;
  }
  /// Maintain current earned rank automatically unless the player explicitly
  /// equipped an earlier title for style. Existing Career Shifts remain separate.
  void _earnCareerXp(int points) {
    if (points <= 0) return;
    final oldRankId = careerRank.id;
    final oldLevel = careerRank.level;
    careerXp += points;
    for (var level = oldLevel + 1; level <= careerRank.level; level++) {
      _pendingLevelUps.add(level);
    }
    if (equippedTitleId == oldRankId) {
      equippedTitleId = careerRank.id;
    }
  }

  int safety = 80;
  int fairness = 80;
  int completedShifts = 0;
  int shiftNumber = 1;
  int shiftXp = 0;
  int correctCount = 0;
  int mistakes = 0;
  int currentIndex = 0;
  int runSeed = 0;
  int credits = 6;
  int tutorialStep = 0;
  Mode mode = Mode.career;
  String modifier = 'SOURCE SWAP';
  List<PlayCase> queue = [];
  final Set<String> revealed = {};
  final Map<String,EvidenceTag> tags = {};
  final List<ReviewResult> results = [];
  Set<String> recent = {};
  final List<Map<String,dynamic>> callbacks = [];
  final Map<String,int> principlesSeen = {};
  final Map<String,int> principlesCorrect = {};
  /// Recent arcade archetypes, kept across sessions to reduce repetition.
  final List<String> arcadeHistory = [];
  bool verifiedSource = false;
  bool finished = false;
  ReviewResult? pendingResult;

  PlayCase get currentCase => queue[currentIndex];
  bool get inShift => queue.isNotEmpty && !finished;
  bool get canPlayEndless => unlockedShift >= 7;
  bool get isTraining => mode == Mode.training;
  double get accuracy => results.isEmpty ? 1 : correctCount / results.length;
  String get grade {
    if (accuracy >= .92 && safety >= 65 && fairness >= 65) return 'S';
    if (accuracy >= .78 && safety >= 55 && fairness >= 55) return 'A';
    if (accuracy >= .65 && safety >= 45 && fairness >= 45) return 'B';
    return 'C';
  }
  bool get promoted => grade != 'C';

  /// Hook attached by CloudSaveService to debounce background cloud syncs.
  VoidCallback? onSaved;

  /// Lifetime arcade stats synced with the player's cloud save.
  final Map<String, int> stats = {
    'runs': 0,
    'rounds': 0,
    'correct': 0,
    'bestStreak': 0,
    'bestRunScore': 0,
  };

  void recordRun({
    required int rounds,
    required int correct,
    required int bestStreak,
    required int score,
  }) {
    if (rounds <= 0) return;
    stats['runs'] = (stats['runs'] ?? 0) + 1;
    stats['rounds'] = (stats['rounds'] ?? 0) + rounds;
    stats['correct'] = (stats['correct'] ?? 0) + correct;
    stats['bestStreak'] = max(stats['bestStreak'] ?? 0, bestStreak);
    stats['bestRunScore'] = max(stats['bestRunScore'] ?? 0, score);
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toCloud() => {
        'careerXp': careerXp,
        'completedShifts': completedShifts,
        'unlockedShift': unlockedShift,
        'safety': safety,
        'fairness': fairness,
        'hintsUsed': hintsUsed,
        'equipped': {
          'title': equippedTitleId,
          'badge': equippedBadgeId,
          'frame': equippedFrameId,
          'mascotName': equippedMascotNameId,
          'mascotColor': equippedMascotColorId,
        },
        'flags': {
          'tutorialFinished': tutorialFinished,
          'arcadeIntroFinished': arcadeIntroFinished,
          'mascotIntroduced': mascotIntroduced,
        },
        'stats': Map<String, int>.from(stats),
      };

  /// Cosmetics unlock by XP. When the account's XP is lower than what this
  /// device had (for example the server corrected an invalid value, or the
  /// device switched accounts), fall back to the default for any equipped
  /// item that is no longer unlocked.
  void _resetLockedCosmetics() {
    bool locked(int? unlockXp) => (unlockXp ?? 999999) > careerXp;
    if (locked(CareerProgression.rankById(equippedTitleId)?.xp)) {
      equippedTitleId = careerRank.id;
    }
    if (locked(CareerProgression.badgeById(equippedBadgeId)?.xp)) {
      equippedBadgeId = CareerProgression.badges.first.id;
    }
    if (locked(CareerProgression.frameById(equippedFrameId)?.xp)) {
      equippedFrameId = CareerProgression.frames.first.id;
    }
    if (locked(CareerProgression.mascotNameById(equippedMascotNameId)?.xp)) {
      equippedMascotNameId = CareerProgression.mascotNames.first.id;
    }
    if (locked(CareerProgression.mascotColorById(equippedMascotColorId)?.xp)) {
      equippedMascotColorId = CareerProgression.mascotColors.first.id;
    }
  }

  Future<void> applyCloud(Map<String, dynamic> data) async {
    int readInt(dynamic v, int fallback) => v is num ? v.toInt() : fallback;

    careerXp = max(0, readInt(data['careerXp'], careerXp));
    completedShifts = max(0, readInt(data['completedShifts'], completedShifts));
    unlockedShift = readInt(data['unlockedShift'], unlockedShift).clamp(1, 7).toInt();
    safety = readInt(data['safety'], safety).clamp(0, 100).toInt();
    fairness = readInt(data['fairness'], fairness).clamp(0, 100).toInt();
    hintsUsed = max(0, readInt(data['hintsUsed'], hintsUsed));

    final eq = data['equipped'];
    if (eq is Map) {
      final title = eq['title']?.toString();
      if (title != null && (CareerProgression.rankById(title)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp) {
        equippedTitleId = title;
      }
      final badge = eq['badge']?.toString();
      if (badge != null && (CareerProgression.badgeById(badge)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp) {
        equippedBadgeId = badge;
      }
      final frame = eq['frame']?.toString();
      if (frame != null && (CareerProgression.frameById(frame)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp) {
        equippedFrameId = frame;
      }
      final mName = eq['mascotName']?.toString();
      if (mName != null && (CareerProgression.mascotNameById(mName)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp) {
        equippedMascotNameId = mName;
      }
      final mColor = eq['mascotColor']?.toString();
      if (mColor != null && (CareerProgression.mascotColorById(mColor)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp) {
        equippedMascotColorId = mColor;
      }
    }

    _resetLockedCosmetics();

    final fl = data['flags'];
    if (fl is Map) {
      if (fl['tutorialFinished'] is bool) {
        tutorialFinished = fl['tutorialFinished'] as bool;
      }
      if (fl['arcadeIntroFinished'] is bool) {
        arcadeIntroFinished = fl['arcadeIntroFinished'] as bool;
      }
      if (fl['mascotIntroduced'] is bool) {
        mascotIntroduced = fl['mascotIntroduced'] as bool;
      }
    }

    final st = data['stats'];
    if (st is Map) {
      for (final key in stats.keys.toList()) {
        final v = st[key];
        if (v is num) stats[key] = max(0, v.toInt());
      }
    }

    notifyListeners();
    await _save(notifyCloud: false);
  }

  Future<void> load() async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      unlockedShift = (prefs.getInt('level_v4') ?? prefs.getInt('level_v3') ?? 1).clamp(1,7).toInt();
      careerXp = prefs.getInt('xp_v4') ?? prefs.getInt('xp_v3') ?? 0;
      final savedTitle = prefs.getString('cosmetic_title_v1') ?? CareerProgression.ranks.first.id;
      final savedBadge = prefs.getString('cosmetic_badge_v1') ?? CareerProgression.badges.first.id;
      final savedFrame = prefs.getString('cosmetic_frame_v1') ?? CareerProgression.frames.first.id;
      final savedMascotName = prefs.getString('mascot_name_v12') ?? 'chameleo';
      final savedMascotColor = prefs.getString('mascot_color_v12') ?? 'mint';
      mascotIntroduced = prefs.getBool('mascot_intro_v12') ?? false;
      equippedTitleId = (CareerProgression.rankById(savedTitle)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp
          ? savedTitle : CareerProgression.ranks.first.id;
      equippedBadgeId = (CareerProgression.badgeById(savedBadge)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp
          ? savedBadge : CareerProgression.badges.first.id;
      equippedFrameId = (CareerProgression.frameById(savedFrame)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp
          ? savedFrame : CareerProgression.frames.first.id;
      equippedMascotNameId = (CareerProgression.mascotNameById(savedMascotName)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp ? savedMascotName : 'chameleo';
      equippedMascotColorId = (CareerProgression.mascotColorById(savedMascotColor)?.xp ?? 999999) <= careerXp ? savedMascotColor : 'mint';
      hintsUsed = prefs.getInt('hints_used_v8') ?? 0;
      safety = (prefs.getInt('safety_v4') ?? 80).clamp(0,100).toInt();
      fairness = (prefs.getInt('fairness_v4') ?? 80).clamp(0,100).toInt();
      completedShifts = prefs.getInt('shifts_v4') ?? 0;
      tutorialFinished = prefs.getBool('tutorial_v5') ?? false;
      arcadeIntroFinished = prefs.getBool('arcade_intro_v7') ?? false;
      haptics = prefs.getBool('haptics_v4') ?? true;
      recent = (prefs.getStringList('recent_v4') ?? []).toSet();
      arcadeHistory.addAll(prefs.getStringList('arcade_recent_v1') ?? []);
      final stored = prefs.getStringList('callbacks_v4') ?? [];
      callbacks.clear();
      for (final raw in stored) {
        final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
        if (decoded is Map<String,dynamic>) callbacks.add(decoded);
      }
      final rawStats = prefs.getString('stats_v1');
      if (rawStats != null) {
        final decoded = jsonDecode(rawStats);
        if (decoded is Map) {
          for (final key in stats.keys.toList()) {
            final v = decoded[key];
            if (v is num) stats[key] = v.toInt();
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (_) { /* App works without storage on unsupported platforms. */ }
    ready = true;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> _save({bool notifyCloud = true}) async {
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      await prefs.setInt('level_v4',unlockedShift);
      await prefs.setInt('xp_v4',careerXp);
      await prefs.setString('cosmetic_title_v1', equippedTitleId);
      await prefs.setString('cosmetic_badge_v1', equippedBadgeId);
      await prefs.setString('cosmetic_frame_v1', equippedFrameId);
      await prefs.setString('mascot_name_v12', equippedMascotNameId);
      await prefs.setString('mascot_color_v12', equippedMascotColorId);
      await prefs.setBool('mascot_intro_v12', mascotIntroduced);
      await prefs.setInt('hints_used_v8',hintsUsed);
      await prefs.setInt('safety_v4',safety);
      await prefs.setInt('fairness_v4',fairness);
      await prefs.setInt('shifts_v4',completedShifts);
      await prefs.setBool('tutorial_v5',tutorialFinished);
      await prefs.setBool('arcade_intro_v7',arcadeIntroFinished);
      await prefs.setBool('haptics_v4',haptics);
      await prefs.setStringList('recent_v4',recent.take(80).toList());
      await prefs.setStringList('arcade_recent_v1',arcadeHistory);
      await prefs.setStringList('callbacks_v4',callbacks.map(jsonEncode).toList());
      await prefs.setString('stats_v1', jsonEncode(stats));
      if (notifyCloud) onSaved?.call();
    } catch (_) { }
  }
  void _haptic() { if (haptics) HapticFeedback.selectionClick(); }

  /// Two short interactive training cases: a fraudulent delivery message and
  /// an authentic bookshop payment receipt. No question-and-answer lecture.
  void startTutorial() {
    final spam = patterns.firstWhere((p) => p.kind == CaseKind.sms && p.verdict == Verdict.reject);
    final receipt = patterns.firstWhere((p) => p.kind == CaseKind.sms && p.verdict == Verdict.approve && p.body.contains('₱'));
    final generator = CaseGenerator(42);
    queue = [generator.build(spam,'training-spam'),generator.build(receipt,'training-receipt')];
    mode = Mode.training;
    shiftNumber = 0;
    modifier = 'FIRST DAY';
    _resetShift();
    credits = 3;
    tutorialStep = 0;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void startShift(int requested, {bool endless = false}) {
    if (endless && !canPlayEndless) return;
    if (!endless && (requested < 1 || requested > 6 || requested > unlockedShift)) return;
    _haptic();
    mode = endless ? Mode.endless : Mode.career;
    shiftNumber = endless ? 7 : requested;
    runSeed = DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch ^ Random().nextInt(0x7fffffff);
    final followups = <PlayCase>[];
    if (callbacks.isNotEmpty) {
      final prior = callbacks.removeAt(0);
      final id = (prior['archetype'] ?? '').toString();
      final index = int.tryParse(id.split(':').last) ?? 0;
      if (index >= 0 && index < patterns.length) {
        final former = patterns[index];
        final original = CaseGenerator(runSeed + 1).build(former,'followup:$runSeed');
        final wasAppeal = prior['type'] == 'appeal';
        followups.add(PlayCase(
          id: 'callback:$runSeed', archetypeId: 'callback:$runSeed',
          kind: CaseKind.report,
          correct: wasAppeal ? Verdict.approve : Verdict.reject,
          topic: original.topic,
          author: 'Tambayan Appeals Desk',
          handle: '@tambayanappeals',
          headline: wasAppeal ? 'A previous removal was appealed' : 'A user reported a prior approval',
          body: wasAppeal
              ? 'The creator of a previously removed ${original.topic.toLowerCase()} post requests a review.'
              : 'A community member reports harm after an earlier ${original.topic.toLowerCase()} post stayed online.',
          artifact: '📁 LINKED CASE', secondary: 'Earlier decision is on record',
          timestamp: 'today', accountAge: '3 years ago', ageDays: 1095,
          followers: 12500, likes: 0, reports: 1, verified: true,
          rationale: wasAppeal
              ? 'The earlier content was legitimate. Reverse the wrongful removal.'
              : 'The earlier content was deceptive. Remove it to prevent further harm.',
          variant: 'followup:$runSeed', tier: 2,
          clues: [
            const Hotspot('identity','PREVIOUS CASE','A reviewer has documented the previous decision.',Icons.history_rounded),
            const Hotspot('context','NEW REPORT','New information prompted the appeal.',Icons.flag_outlined),
            Hotspot('source','REVIEW THE SOURCE',
              wasAppeal ? 'The full original post was legitimate and complied with the rules.' : 'Independent evidence confirms deceptive conduct.',
              Icons.fact_check_outlined, verification:true, strength:3,
              signal:wasAppeal?EvidenceSignal.supporting:EvidenceSignal.suspicious),
          ],
        ));
      }
    }
    final bundle = CaseGenerator(runSeed).generate(
      shiftNumber, seed:runSeed, endless:endless,
      count:7, recent:recent, callbacks:followups,
      safety:safety, fairness:fairness,
    );
    queue = bundle.cases;
    recent.addAll(bundle.signatures);
    if (recent.length > 80) recent = recent.skip(recent.length - 80).toSet();
    modifier = bundle.modifier;
    _resetShift();
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
  }

  void _resetShift() {
    shiftXp = 0;
    correctCount = 0;
    mistakes = 0;
    currentIndex = 0;
    pendingResult = null;
    finished = false;
    credits = 6;
    results.clear();
    revealed.clear();
    tags.clear();
    verifiedSource = false;
  }

  Hotspot? inspect(String key) {
    if (!inShift || pendingResult != null) return null;
    final clues = currentCase.clues.where((h) => h.id == key && !h.verification);
    if (clues.isEmpty) return null;
    revealed.add(key);
    if (isTraining && currentIndex == 0 && tutorialStep == 0) tutorialStep = 1;
    _haptic();
    notifyListeners();
    return clues.first;
  }

  void tagEvidence(String key) {
    if (!revealed.contains(key) || pendingResult != null) return;
    tags[key] = (tags[key] ?? EvidenceTag.unset).next;
    // Evidence classification is optional: beginners first learn to inspect and verify.
    _haptic();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Hotspot? verify() {
    if (!inShift || pendingResult != null || verifiedSource) return null;
    final candidate = currentCase.clues.where((h) => h.verification);
    if (candidate.isEmpty) return null;
    verifiedSource = true;
    revealed.add(candidate.first.id);
    if (isTraining && currentIndex == 0 && tutorialStep <= 1) tutorialStep = 2;
    _haptic();
    notifyListeners();
    return candidate.first;
  }

  ReviewResult? decide(Verdict decision) {
    if (!inShift || pendingResult != null) return null;
    // Training demonstrates a decision after evidence, without forcing a
    // particular verdict or showing its answer beforehand.
    if (isTraining && currentIndex == 0 && tutorialStep < 2) return null;
    final item = currentCase;
    final right = decision == item.correct;
    int matching = 0;
    for (final clue in item.clues) {
      final tag = tags[clue.id];
      if (tag == null || tag == EvidenceTag.unset) continue;
      final correctTag = switch (clue.signal) {
        EvidenceSignal.suspicious => EvidenceTag.suspicious,
        EvidenceSignal.supporting => EvidenceTag.supporting,
        EvidenceSignal.ambiguous => EvidenceTag.unsure,
        EvidenceSignal.neutral => EvidenceTag.unsure,
      };
      if (tag == correctTag) matching++;
      principlesSeen.update(clue.principle,(value)=>value+1,ifAbsent:()=>1);
      if (tag == correctTag) {
        principlesCorrect.update(clue.principle,(value)=>value+1,ifAbsent:()=>1);
      }
    }
    final int points = right
        ? (item.tier == 1 ? 20 : item.tier == 2 ? 30 : 45) +
            min<int>(9, matching * 3)
        : item.correct == Verdict.reject ? -40 : -25;
    shiftXp += points;
    if (right) {
      correctCount++;
      _earnCareerXp(points);
      safety = min(100,safety+1);
      fairness = min(100,fairness+1);
    } else {
      mistakes++;
      if (item.correct == Verdict.reject) {
        safety = max(0,safety-10);
      } else {
        fairness = max(0,fairness-8);
      }
      if (!isTraining && !item.id.startsWith('callback')) {
        callbacks.add({
          'type': item.correct == Verdict.approve ? 'appeal' : 'victim',
          'archetype': item.archetypeId,
        });
        if (callbacks.length > 8) callbacks.removeAt(0);
      }
    }
    final result = ReviewResult(item,decision,points,right,matching,
      verifiedSource, Map<String,EvidenceTag>.from(tags), Set<String>.from(revealed));
    results.add(result);
    pendingResult = result;
    _haptic();
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
    return result;
  }

  void next() {
    if (pendingResult == null) return;
    pendingResult = null;
    if (currentIndex >= queue.length - 1) {
      finished = true;
      if (isTraining) {
        tutorialFinished = true;
        tutorialStep = 5;
      } else {
        completedShifts++;
        // Completing a numbered career shift always unlocks the next in order.
        // The grade affects the report, not the player's ability to continue.
        if (mode == Mode.career && shiftNumber == unlockedShift && unlockedShift <= 6) {
          unlockedShift++;
        }
      }
      _save();
    } else {
      currentIndex++;
      revealed.clear();
      tags.clear();
      verifiedSource = false;
      if (isTraining) tutorialStep = 3;
    }
    _haptic();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Arcade onboarding is separate from the classic moderator training.
  /// First-day training unlocks the Arcade Hub; XP unlocks advanced modes.
  bool get arcadeUnlocked => arcadeIntroFinished;

  bool arcadeModeUnlocked(int threshold) => arcadeIntroFinished && careerXp >= threshold;

  Future<void> finishArcadeIntro() async {
    arcadeIntroFinished = true;
    notifyListeners();
    await _save();
  }

  Future<void> replayArcadeIntro() async {
    arcadeIntroFinished = false;
    notifyListeners();
    await _save();
  }


  void recordArcadeCase(String signature) {
    arcadeHistory.remove(signature);
    arcadeHistory.add(signature);
    if (arcadeHistory.length > 15) arcadeHistory.removeAt(0);
    _save();
  }

  void awardArcadeXp(int amount) {
    if (amount <= 0) return;
    _earnCareerXp(amount);
    notifyListeners();
    _save();
  }

  Future<void> skipTutorial() async {
    tutorialFinished = true;
    tutorialStep = 5;
    notifyListeners();
    await _save();
  }
  Future<void> resetTutorial() async {
    tutorialFinished = false;
    tutorialStep = 0;
    notifyListeners();
    await _save();
  }
  Future<void> setHaptics(bool value) async {
    haptics = value;
    notifyListeners();
    await _save();
  }
}
n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\controllers\inbox_motion.dart =====nimport 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Pure, testable swipe rules for Inbox Sprint.
/// 0 = SPAM (left), 1 = KEEP (right), null = return to centre.
int? inboxSwipeVerdict({
  required double displacement,
  required double velocity,
  required double viewportWidth,
}) {
  final threshold = (viewportWidth * .23).clamp(72.0, 126.0);
  if (displacement.abs() >= threshold) {
    return displacement < 0 ? 0 : 1;
  }
  // A flick must already have moved the card in the flick's direction.
  // This prevents a vertical scroll or accidental touch from sorting mail.
  if (displacement.abs() >= 24 &&
      velocity.abs() >= 650 &&
      displacement * velocity > 0) {
    return displacement < 0 ? 0 : 1;
  }
  return null;
}

/// Gradual, pale whole-card colour feedback while following a finger.
/// Never colours the card when it is centred; avoids floating verdict stamps.
Color inboxSwipeTint({
  required Color base,
  required double displacement,
  required double viewportWidth,
}) {
  if (displacement == 0) return base;
  final distance = math.max(100.0, viewportWidth * .34);
  final fraction = (displacement.abs() / distance).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
  final eased = Curves.easeOutCubic.transform(fraction);
  final tint = displacement < 0
      ? const Color(0xFFFFCFD5)
      : const Color(0xFFC9F2DB);
  return Color.lerp(base, tint, eased * .84)!;
}

Color inboxSwipeBorder({
  required Color base,
  required double displacement,
  required double viewportWidth,
}) {
  if (displacement == 0) return base;
  final fraction = (displacement.abs() / math.max(100.0, viewportWidth * .34))
      .clamp(0.0, 1.0);
  final tint = displacement < 0
      ? const Color(0xFFC9495B)
      : const Color(0xFF278259);
  return Color.lerp(base, tint, fraction * .7)!;
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\controllers\navigation_controller.dart =====n/// Application navigation rules. This controller does not build widgets.
/// UI widgets ask it whether a transition is allowed and then rebuild.
enum GameView { home, arcade, career, profile, desk, summary }

class NavigationController {
  GameView current = GameView.home;

  static const menuTabs = <GameView>{
    GameView.home, GameView.arcade, GameView.career, GameView.profile,
  };

  /// The bottom tabs must never interrupt an active Arcade challenge.
  bool openTab(GameView tab, {required bool arcadePlaying}) {
    if (arcadePlaying || !menuTabs.contains(tab)) return false;
    current = tab;
    return true;
  }

  bool get onMenu => menuTabs.contains(current);

  void enterCareerChallenge() => current = GameView.desk;

  void showCareerSummary() => current = GameView.summary;

  void returnHome() => current = GameView.home;
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\models\arcade_models.dart =====n/// Arcade game rules and data. This layer is UI-independent (pure Dart) so it
/// can be unit tested without Flutter.
enum ArcadeMode { trust, impostor, scamSlam, rumor, inbox, comments, clip }

enum ArcadeDifficulty { easy, medium, hard }

/// How a rumor puzzle is played. Each task uses a different control.
enum RumorTask { order, spotDiff, outdated, independent, wordEdit }

/// How useful an inspection result is. Never shown to the player; used for
/// XP bonuses, hints and generator validation.
enum Relevance { decisive, support, decoy }

extension ArcadeModeInfo on ArcadeMode {
  String get title => switch (this) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => 'TRUST OR BUST',
    ArcadeMode.impostor => 'IMPOSTOR HUNT',
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => 'SCAM SLAM',
    ArcadeMode.rumor => 'RUMOR RADAR',
    ArcadeMode.inbox => 'INBOX SPRINT',
    ArcadeMode.comments => 'COMMENT CHAOS',
    ArcadeMode.clip => 'CLIP CHECK',
  };
  String get subtitle => switch (this) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => 'Inspect a viral post, then keep or remove',
    ArcadeMode.impostor => 'Cross-check two accounts with the directory',
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => 'Chat back before the scammer wins',
    ArcadeMode.rumor => 'Rebuild chains, spot edits, find real proof',
    ArcadeMode.inbox => 'Swipe a whole inbox: spam or keep',
    ArcadeMode.comments => 'Flag only the comments that break rules',
    ArcadeMode.clip => 'Scrub the timeline: viral cut vs full video',
  };

  /// One short line shown during play.
  String get how => switch (this) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => 'Tap checks, then decide.',
    ArcadeMode.impostor => 'Open both. Compare with the directory.',
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => 'Read the chat. Pick your reply.',
    ArcadeMode.rumor => 'Solve the puzzle, then lock it in.',
    ArcadeMode.inbox => 'Swipe left = spam, right = keep.',
    ArcadeMode.comments => 'Tap rule-breaking comments.',
    ArcadeMode.clip => 'Slide the timeline. Compare cuts.',
  };
  String get emoji => switch (this) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => '📣', ArcadeMode.impostor => '🕵️',
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => '💬', ArcadeMode.rumor => '🧩',
    ArcadeMode.inbox => '✉️', ArcadeMode.comments => '👑',
    ArcadeMode.clip => '🎬',
  };
  int get unlockXp => switch (this) {
    ArcadeMode.trust || ArcadeMode.impostor || ArcadeMode.scamSlam => 0,
    ArcadeMode.inbox => 300,
    ArcadeMode.comments => 600,
    ArcadeMode.rumor => 900,
    ArcadeMode.clip => 1200,
  };
}

extension ArcadeDifficultyInfo on ArcadeDifficulty {
  String get title => switch (this) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => 'EASY',
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 'MEDIUM',
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 'HARD',
  };
  int get startingHearts => switch (this) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => 5,
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 4,
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 3,
  };
  int get bonusXp => index * 10;

  /// How many of the five inspection tools a player may open on one case.
  int get scanBudget => switch (this) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => 5,
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 4,
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 3,
  };

  /// Seconds per email in Inbox Sprint; null means untimed.
  int? get emailSeconds => switch (this) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => null,
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 14,
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 9,
  };
}

}

/// One inspection result (a "tool" the player can open on a case).
class Finding {

  final Relevance relevance;
  const Finding(this.id, this.label, this.emoji, this.text, this.relevance);
}

class ProfileView {
  final String name, handle, bio, link;
  final String? label;
  final bool badge;
  final List<String> recent;
  final int ageDays, followers;
  const ProfileView({
    required this.name, required this.handle, required this.bio,
    required this.ageDays, required this.followers,
    this.link = 'no link', this.label, this.badge = false,
    this.recent = const [],
  });
  String get ageLabel => ageDays < 7 ? '$ageDays days old' :
      ageDays < 365 ? '${(ageDays / 30).round()} months old' :
      '${(ageDays / 365).round()} yrs old';
}

class EmailItem {
  final String fromName, fromAddress, replyTo, subject, body;
  final String linkLabel, linkTarget, attachment;
  /// The sorting truth: true = spam/phishing, false = legitimate.
  final bool spam;
  final String why;
  const EmailItem({
    required this.fromName, required this.fromAddress, required this.subject,
    required this.body, required this.spam, required this.why,
    this.replyTo = '', this.linkLabel = '', this.linkTarget = '',
    this.attachment = '',
  });
}

  /// True when this comment really breaks the community rules.
  final bool violates;
  const CommentView(this.handle, this.body,
      {this.context = '', this.violates = false, this.reason = ''});
}

  final String title, body, meta;
  const RumorCard(this.title, this.body, [this.meta = '']);
}

class ClipBeat {
  /// Seconds into the original video.
  final int second;
  final String scene, viralLine, fullLine;
  final bool inViral;
  const ClipBeat(this.second, this.scene, this.viralLine, this.fullLine,
      {this.inViral = true});
}

/// A complete scenario. Truth is generated first (see [deceptive] and
/// [correct]); visible wording and clues are derived from it.
/// Fictional people, brands and `.example` domains only.
class ArcadeCase {
  final String signature, scenario;
  final ArcadeMode mode;
  final String title, author, handle, text, subtext, lesson, hint, visual;
  final bool deceptive;

  /// The correct answer in the mode's own format:
  ///  single choice: [index]; comments: sorted flagged indices (may be empty);
  ///  rumor order: card indices in the right order; word edit: sorted token
  ///  indices; inbox: per email 0 = spam, 1 = keep.
  final List<int> correct;

  // Post Autopsy / Clip Check
  final String imageTopic, imageCaption, reactions;
  final List<Finding> tools;

  // Impostor Hunt
  final ProfileView? leftProfile, rightProfile;
  final List<String> directory;

  // Scam Slam
  final List<String> chatIn, activity;
  final String linkDomain;
  final List<ArcadeOption> choices;
  final Map<int, List<String>> followUps;

  // Rumor Radar
  final RumorTask? rumorTask;
  final List<RumorCard> cards;
  final List<String> tokens;

  // Inbox Sprint
  final List<EmailItem> emails;

  // Comment Chaos
  final List<CommentView> comments;

  // Clip Check
  final List<ClipBeat> beats;

  const ArcadeCase({
    required this.signature, required this.scenario, required this.mode,
    required this.title, required this.lesson, required this.hint,
    required this.correct, required this.deceptive,
    this.author = '', this.handle = '', this.text = '', this.subtext = '',
    this.visual = '', this.imageTopic = 'Government', this.imageCaption = '',
    this.reactions = '', this.tools = const [],
    this.leftProfile, this.rightProfile, this.directory = const [],
    this.chatIn = const [], this.activity = const [], this.linkDomain = '',
    this.choices = const [], this.followUps = const {},
    this.rumorTask, this.cards = const [], this.tokens = const [],
    this.emails = const [], this.comments = const [], this.beats = const [],
  });

  /// Single-choice convenience.
  int get correctChoice => correct.isEmpty ? -1 : correct.first;

  bool get isBatch => mode == ArcadeMode.inbox;

  bool get _sortsAnswer => mode == ArcadeMode.comments ||
      (mode == ArcadeMode.rumor && rumorTask == RumorTask.wordEdit);

  /// Number of sub-answers that match the truth (Inbox Sprint is partial).
  int matches(List<int> answer) {
    if (mode == ArcadeMode.inbox) {
      var n = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < correct.length; i++) {
        if (i < answer.length && answer[i] == correct[i]) n++;
      }
      return n;
    }
    return isCorrect(answer) ? 1 : 0;
  }

  int get totalParts => mode == ArcadeMode.inbox ? correct.length : 1;

  bool isCorrect(List<int> answer) {
    final a = [...answer];
    if (_sortsAnswer) a.sort();
    if (a.length != correct.length) return false;
    for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
      if (a[i] != correct[i]) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  /// The tool ids that actually settle the question.
  List<String> get decisiveIds => tools
      .where((t) => t.relevance == Relevance.decisive)
      .map((t) => t.id).toList();
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\models\models.dart =====nimport 'package:flutter/material.dart';

enum CaseKind { post, profile, sms, email, report, video }
enum Verdict { approve, reject }
enum Mode { career, endless, training }
enum EvidenceSignal { suspicious, supporting, ambiguous, neutral }
enum EvidenceTag { unset, suspicious, supporting, unsure }

extension EvidenceSignalInfo on EvidenceSignal {
  String get name => switch (this) {
    EvidenceSignal.suspicious => 'SUSPICIOUS',
    EvidenceSignal.supporting => 'SUPPORTS',
    EvidenceSignal.ambiguous => 'UNCERTAIN',
    EvidenceSignal.neutral => 'BACKGROUND',
  };
  IconData get icon => switch (this) {
    EvidenceSignal.suspicious => Icons.flag_rounded,
    EvidenceSignal.supporting => Icons.check_circle_rounded,
    EvidenceSignal.ambiguous => Icons.help_outline_rounded,
    EvidenceSignal.neutral => Icons.remove_circle_outline_rounded,
  };
}

extension EvidenceTagInfo on EvidenceTag {
  String get label => switch (this) {
    EvidenceTag.unset => 'MARK',
    EvidenceTag.suspicious => 'FLAG',
    EvidenceTag.supporting => 'BACKS',
    EvidenceTag.unsure => 'UNSURE',
  };
  EvidenceTag get next => switch (this) {
    EvidenceTag.unset => EvidenceTag.suspicious,
    EvidenceTag.suspicious => EvidenceTag.supporting,
    EvidenceTag.supporting => EvidenceTag.unsure,
    EvidenceTag.unsure => EvidenceTag.unset,
  };
}

extension CaseKindInfo on CaseKind {
  String get label => switch (this) {
    CaseKind.post => 'SOCIAL POST', CaseKind.profile => 'ACCOUNT PROFILE',
    CaseKind.sms => 'TEXT MESSAGE', CaseKind.email => 'EMAIL INBOX',
    CaseKind.report => 'USER REPORT', CaseKind.video => 'VIDEO CLIP',
  };
  IconData get icon => switch (this) {
    CaseKind.post => Icons.dynamic_feed_rounded,
    CaseKind.profile => Icons.account_circle_rounded,
    CaseKind.sms => Icons.sms_rounded,
    CaseKind.email => Icons.alternate_email_rounded,
    CaseKind.report => Icons.report_gmailerrorred_rounded,
    CaseKind.video => Icons.smart_display_rounded,
  };
}

class Hotspot {
  final String id;
  final String label;
  final String reveal;
  final IconData icon;
  final EvidenceSignal signal;
  final int strength;
  final bool verification;
  final String principle;
  const Hotspot(
    this.id, this.label, this.reveal, this.icon, {
    this.signal = EvidenceSignal.neutral,
    this.strength = 1,
    this.verification = false,
    this.principle = 'check_the_source',
  });
}

class PlayCase {
  final String id, archetypeId, topic, author, handle, headline, body;
  final String artifact, secondary, timestamp, accountAge, rationale, variant;
  final CaseKind kind;
  final Verdict correct;
  final int ageDays, followers, likes, reports, tier;
  final bool verified;
  final List<Hotspot> clues;
  const PlayCase({
    required this.id, required this.kind, required this.correct,
    required this.topic, required this.author, required this.handle,
    required this.headline, required this.body, required this.artifact,
    required this.secondary, required this.timestamp, required this.accountAge,
    required this.followers, required this.likes, required this.reports,
    required this.verified, required this.clues, required this.rationale,
    required this.variant, this.archetypeId = '', this.ageDays = 30,
    this.tier = 2,
  });
}

class ReviewResult {
  final PlayCase item;
  final Verdict answer;

  final bool correct, verifiedSource;
  final Map<String, EvidenceTag> tags;
  final Set<String> opened;
  const ReviewResult(this.item, this.answer, this.xp, this.correct,
    this.tagsCorrect, this.verifiedSource, this.tags, this.opened);
}

class ShiftInfo {
  final String name, subtitle, hint;
  final IconData icon;

}

    ProfileCollectible('ice', 'Icy Investigator', '❄️', 3800, 'Cool-headed fact checks.'),
    ProfileCollectible('ember', 'Ember Evidence', '🔥', 6000, 'A fiery pursuit of facts.'),
    ProfileCollectible('gold', 'Legendary Gold', '👑', 9500, 'The final glowing disguise.'),
  ];

  static ProfileCollectible? mascotNameById(String id) {
    for (final item in mascotNames) {
      if (item.id == id) return item;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static ProfileCollectible? mascotColorById(String id) {
    for (final item in mascotColors) {
      if (item.id == id) return item;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static CareerRank rankAt(int xp) {
    var current = ranks.first;
    for (final rank in ranks) {
      if (xp < rank.xp) break;
      current = rank;
    }
    return current;
  }

  static CareerRank? nextRank(int xp) {
    for (final rank in ranks) {
      if (xp < rank.xp) return rank;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static double levelProgress(int xp) {
    final current = rankAt(xp);
    final next = nextRank(xp);
    if (next == null) return 1;
    return ((xp - current.xp) / (next.xp - current.xp)).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
  }

  static CareerRank? rankById(String id) {
    for (final rank in ranks) {
      if (rank.id == id) return rank;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static ProfileCollectible? badgeById(String id) {
    for (final item in badges) {
      if (item.id == id) return item;
    }
    return null;
  }

  static ProfileCollectible? frameById(String id) {
    for (final item in frames) {
      if (item.id == id) return item;
    }
    return null;
  }
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_common.dart =====nimport 'dart:math';

/// Thin wrapper around [Random] with the helpers every mode generator uses.
class Gen {
  final Random r;
  Gen(this.r);

  T pick<T>(List<T> items) => items[r.nextInt(items.length)];
  bool chance(double p) => r.nextDouble() < p;
  int between(int low, int high) => low + r.nextInt(high - low + 1);
  int id() => 100 + r.nextInt(900);
  List<T> shuffled<T>(Iterable<T> items) => items.toList()..shuffle(r);

  /// Picks [n] distinct items.
  List<T> sample<T>(List<T> items, int n) =>
      shuffled(items).take(n).toList();
}

/// Fictional content pools. No real people, parties or live websites.
class Pools {
  Pools._();

  static const intros = ['Uy!', 'Mga besh,', 'FYI lang,', 'Hello po!',
    'Grabe,', 'Pa-share po,', 'Update:'];

/// Replaces `{slot}` markers.
String fillSlots(String s, Map<String, String> slots) {
  var out = s;
  slots.forEach((k, v) => out = out.replaceAll('{$k}', v));
  return out;
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_generator.dart =====nimport 'dart:math';
import 'arcade_common.dart';
import 'arcade_gen_chat.dart';
import 'arcade_gen_clip.dart';
import 'arcade_gen_impostor.dart';
import 'arcade_gen_inbox.dart';
import 'arcade_gen_rumor.dart';
import 'arcade_gen_thread.dart';
import 'arcade_gen_trust.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// Offline, truth-first case composer. For each mode the generator first picks
/// a scenario (and its true outcome), then writes visible wording and clues
/// from that truth. Difficulty changes evidence budget, noise and the number
/// of items, not just the hearts.
class ArcadeGenerator {
  final Random random;
  final Gen gen;
  ArcadeGenerator(int seed)
      : random = Random(seed),
        gen = Gen(Random(seed ^ 0x5bd1e995));

  T pick<T>(List<T> choices) => choices[random.nextInt(choices.length)];

  /// How many distinct scenarios a mode has (before per-case variation).
  static int scenarioCount(ArcadeMode mode) => switch (mode) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => trustScenarioCount(),
    ArcadeMode.impostor => impostorScenarioCount(),
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => chatScenarioCount(),
    ArcadeMode.rumor => rumorScenarioCount(),
    ArcadeMode.inbox => inboxScenarioCount(),
    ArcadeMode.comments => threadScenarioCount(),
    ArcadeMode.clip => clipScenarioCount(),
  };

  /// Whether scenario [i] of [mode] is a "bad" (remove/flag/scam) outcome, or
  /// null when the mode does not have a simple good/bad split.
  static bool? scenarioIsBad(ArcadeMode mode, int i) => switch (mode) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => trustScenarioBad(i),
    ArcadeMode.impostor => impostorScenarioFlagged(i),
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => chatScenarioScam(i),
    ArcadeMode.clip => clipScenarioBad(i),
    _ => null,
  };

  static int _tutorialIndex(ArcadeMode mode) {
    String idOf(int i) => switch (mode) {
      ArcadeMode.trust => trustScenarioId(i),
      ArcadeMode.impostor => impostorScenarioId(i),
      ArcadeMode.scamSlam => chatScenarioId(i),
      _ => '',
    };
    final wanted = switch (mode) {
      ArcadeMode.trust => 'recycled_flood',
      ArcadeMode.impostor => 'lookalike',
      ArcadeMode.scamSlam => 'parcel_fee',
      _ => '',
    };
    if (wanted.isEmpty) return 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < scenarioCount(mode); i++) {
      if (idOf(i) == wanted) return i;
    }
    return 0;
  }

  /// A family is the decision-making archetype, not a new handle or amount.
  /// A player should not see "parcel fee" again immediately just because
  /// the courier name changed.
  static String familyOfSignature(String signature) {
    final parts = signature.split(':');
    if (parts.length < 2) return signature;
    return '${parts[0]}:${parts[1]}';
  }

  static String _familyAt(ArcadeMode mode, int i) => switch (mode) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => 'trust:${trustScenarioId(i)}',
    ArcadeMode.impostor => 'impostor:${impostorScenarioId(i)}',
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => 'chat:${chatScenarioId(i)}',
    ArcadeMode.rumor => 'rumor:${switch (RumorTask.values[i]) {
      RumorTask.spotDiff => 'spot',
      RumorTask.wordEdit => 'words',
      final task => task.name,
    }}',
    ArcadeMode.inbox => 'inbox:batch',
    ArcadeMode.comments => 'thread:${threadScenarioId(i)}',
    ArcadeMode.clip => 'clip:${clipScenarioId(i)}',
  };

  int _chooseScenario(ArcadeMode mode, double badShare,
      {Set<String> excludeFamilies = const {},
      Set<int> triedIndices = const {}}) {
    final count = scenarioCount(mode);
    final all = [for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) i];
    // Inbox generates whole batches rather than selecting a single template,
    // so it is intentionally not subject to family exclusion.
    final unseen = mode == ArcadeMode.inbox ? all : all.where((i) =>
        !excludeFamilies.contains(_familyAt(mode, i))).toList();
    final candidates = unseen.where((i) => !triedIndices.contains(i)).toList();
    final pool = candidates.isEmpty ? all : candidates;
    if (scenarioIsBad(mode, 0) == null) return pick(pool);
    final wantBad = random.nextDouble() < badShare;
    final matched = pool.where((i) => scenarioIsBad(mode, i) == wantBad).toList();
    return pick(matched.isEmpty ? pool : matched);
  }

  ArcadeCase _build(ArcadeMode mode, ArcadeDifficulty d, int i,
      {bool tutorial = false}) => switch (mode) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => buildTrust(gen, d, i),
    ArcadeMode.impostor => buildImpostor(gen, d, i),
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => buildChat(gen, d, i),
    ArcadeMode.rumor => buildRumor(gen, d, i),
    ArcadeMode.inbox => buildInbox(gen, d, i, count: tutorial ? 3 : null),
    ArcadeMode.comments =>
        buildThread(gen, d, i, forceBad: tutorial ? 1 : null),
    ArcadeMode.clip => buildClip(gen, d, i),
  };

  /// Avoid recent *scenario families* first, then exact signatures.
  /// We only fall back to a repeat when the available pool is exhausted.
  ArcadeCase generate(ArcadeMode mode, ArcadeDifficulty difficulty,
      {Set<String> exclude = const {},
      Set<String> excludeFamilies = const {},
      bool tutorial = false}) {
    if (tutorial) {
      return _build(mode, ArcadeDifficulty.easy, _tutorialIndex(mode),
          tutorial: true);
    }
    final badShare = switch (mode) {
      ArcadeMode.scamSlam => .5,
      ArcadeMode.impostor => .55,
      _ => .5,
    };
    // A fixed-template mode can exhaust its entire available pool. Try
    // alternate templates rather than randomly rolling the same one 16 times.
    final tried = <int>{};
    final dynamicBatch = mode == ArcadeMode.inbox;
    ArcadeCase? fallback;
    final limit = dynamicBatch ? 64 : scenarioCount(mode);
    for (var attempt = 0; attempt < limit; attempt++) {
      final index = _chooseScenario(mode, badShare,
          excludeFamilies: excludeFamilies, triedIndices: tried);
      if (!dynamicBatch) tried.add(index);
      final caseData = _build(mode, difficulty, index);
      fallback ??= caseData;
      final family = familyOfSignature(caseData.signature);
      if (!excludeFamilies.contains(family) &&
          !exclude.contains(caseData.signature)) return caseData;
      // In a fixed-template mode, once every template is considered, it is
      // better to fall back than to keep requesting another random roll.
    }
    // When all families are blocked (e.g. all 5 Rumor puzzles have appeared),
    // relax the family rule before abandoning exact-repeat prevention.
    for (var attempt = 0; attempt < (dynamicBatch ? 24 : scenarioCount(mode)); attempt++) {
      final candidate = _build(mode, difficulty,
          _chooseScenario(mode, badShare));
      if (!exclude.contains(candidate.signature)) return candidate;
    }
    return fallback!;
  }
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_gen_chat.dart =====nimport 'arcade_common.dart';

/// Scam Slam. The player sees what they actually did recently ("your phone"),
/// an incoming chat, and the link's real domain. A message is legitimate only
/// when it matches the player's own activity AND asks for nothing the real
/// service would never ask (OTP, PIN, advance fees, off-app payment).
///
/// The correct reply is NOT always "verify": real messages are answered by
/// carrying on normally, so the answer depends on the facts, not the phrasing.
class _Chat {
  final String id, sender, number;
  final bool scam;
  final List<String> incoming;
  final List<String> activity;
  final String link;     // domain shown in the link preview, '' if none
  final String official; // what the real service uses
  final String senderNote;
  final String best;     // correct reply
  final List<String> wrong; // plausible wrong replies (2 used)
  final List<String> afterBest, afterWrong;
  final String lesson, hint;
  const _Chat(this.id, this.scam, this.sender, this.number, this.incoming,
      this.activity, this.link, this.official, this.senderNote, this.best,
      this.wrong, this.afterBest, this.afterWrong, this.lesson, this.hint);
}

int chatScenarioCount() => _chats.length;
String chatScenarioId(int i) => _chats[i].id;
bool chatScenarioScam(int i) => _chats[i].scam;

ArcadeCase buildChat(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d, int index) {
  final c = _chats[index];
  final group = g.pick(Pools.groups);
  final slots = <String, String>{
    'store': g.pick(Pools.stores), 'wallet': g.pick(Pools.wallets),
    'amt': g.pick(['89', '149', '250', '399', '1,200']),
    'group': group, 'id': '${g.id()}',
    'groupDomain': group.toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z]'), ''),
    'name': g.pick(['Santos', 'Reyes', 'Cruz', 'Bautista']),
    'courier': g.pick(Pools.couriers),
    'schoolDomain': g.pick(['northridge', 'kalikasan', 'pagasa']),
  };
  slots['link'] = fillSlots(c.link, slots);
  String f(String s) => fillSlots(s, slots);
  final wrongOrder = g.shuffled([for (var i = 0; i < c.wrong.length; i++) i])
      .take(2).toList();
  final texts = [f(c.best), ...wrongOrder.map((i) => f(c.wrong[i]))];
  final order = g.shuffled([0, 1, 2]);
  final choices = [for (final i in order) ArcadeOption(texts[i])];
  final correctChoice = order.indexOf(0);
  // Each wrong choice has its own follow-up; the right one has a good ending.
  final follow = <int, List<String>>{};
  for (var pos = 0; pos < 3; pos++) {
    final original = order[pos];
    if (original == 0) {
      follow[pos] = c.afterBest.map(f).toList();
    } else {
      final w = wrongOrder[original - 1];
      follow[pos] = [f(c.afterWrong[w.clamp(0, c.afterWrong.length - 1)])];
    }
  }
  final sender = f(c.sender);
  final number = c.number == 'PesoGo' || c.number == 'GROUP-TIX'
      ? (c.number == 'PesoGo' ? slots['wallet']! : '${group.toUpperCase()}-TIX')
      : c.number;
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'chat:${c.id}', scenario: c.id, mode: ArcadeMode.scamSlam,
    title: 'New message', author: sender, handle: number,
    text: 'What do you do?', subtext: f(c.senderNote),
    chatIn: c.incoming.map(f).toList(), activity: c.activity.map(f).toList(),
    linkDomain: slots['link']!, deceptive: c.scam, correct: [correctChoice],
    choices: choices, followUps: follow,
    lesson: f(c.lesson), hint: f(c.hint),
  );
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_gen_clip.dart =====nimport 'arcade_common.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// Clip Check. A clip is a few beats on a timeline. The viral cut keeps some
/// beats; the full video has all of them. Five info chips (upload info, audio,
/// creator, label, full video) give neutral facts; the truth follows from the
/// facts, so an honest trim and a deceptive trim can look alike on the surface.
class _Clip {
  final String id, topic, title, caption, lesson, hint;
  final bool bad;
  final List<ClipBeat> beats;
  final String upload, audio, creator, label;
  final Relevance rUpload, rAudio, rCreator, rLabel, rFull;
  final String full;
  const _Clip(this.id, this.bad, this.topic, this.title, this.caption,
      this.beats, {
    required this.upload, required this.audio, required this.creator,
    required this.label, required this.full,
    required this.lesson, required this.hint,
    this.rUpload = Relevance.support, this.rAudio = Relevance.support,
    this.rCreator = Relevance.decoy, this.rLabel = Relevance.decoy,
    this.rFull = Relevance.decisive,
  });
}
