    audio: 'The audio names a storm that has nothing to do with today\'s weather.',
    creator: 'A page that posts "live" disaster videos.',
    label: 'No date or location is shown in the video.',
    full: 'The original video is labeled with the {y1} storm and the city of Tala.',
    rUpload: Relevance.decisive, rFull: Relevance.decisive, rAudio: Relevance.support,
    lesson: 'Real disaster footage from an earlier storm is being shown as breaking news. Nothing was edited, but the context is false.',
    hint: 'When was the earliest upload of this video?'),
  _Clip('ai_voice_swap', true, 'AI', 'Senator announcement',
    'Sen. Magtanggol: "Ayuda para sa lahat, mag-register sa link."',
    [
      ClipBeat(0, 'Senator at a podium', 'Voice: "Mga kababayan, may ayuda ako!"', 'Voice: "Mga kababayan, mag-ingat po sa baha."'),
      ClipBeat(9, 'Senator gestures', 'Voice: "Mag-register sa link sa ibaba."', 'Voice: "Sundin ang payo ng mga lokal na opisyal."'),
      ClipBeat(20, 'Senator waves', 'Voice: "Salamat po!"', 'Voice: "Salamat po!"'),
    ],
    upload: 'The video is a re-upload of the senator\'s press statement from last week.',
    audio: 'In the viral version, the lip movements don\'t match the words.',
    creator: 'A new page with no other posts.',
    label: 'No label. Nothing says AI or edited.',
    full: 'The senator\'s own page has the original video: it never mentions ayuda or a link.',
    rAudio: Relevance.decisive, rFull: Relevance.decisive, rCreator: Relevance.support,
    lesson: 'The face is real but the voice was swapped. A real person never said these lines, and the link is a trap.',
    hint: 'Do the mouth and the words match? What does the original say?'),
  _Clip('fake_apology', true, 'Music', 'Idol apology clip',
    '{group} leader finally admits she lip-synced the whole concert.',
    [
      ClipBeat(0, 'Leader at a press room', 'Leader: "Aaminin ko na."', 'Leader: "Gusto kong linawin ang usapin."'),
      ClipBeat(7, 'Leader speaks', 'Leader: "Lip sync lahat."', 'Leader: "May isang kanta na pre-recorded. Hindi lahat."'),
      ClipBeat(18, 'Leader bows', 'Leader: "Pasensya na."', 'Leader: "Pasensya sa kalituhan."'),
    ],
    upload: 'The clip is a short piece of a longer press conference.',
    audio: 'The viral version\'s audio skips between sentences.',
    creator: 'A page that posts only drama about idols.',
    label: 'No label.',
    full: 'In the full video, the leader says one song was pre-recorded and the rest was live.',
    rFull: Relevance.decisive, rAudio: Relevance.support,
    lesson: 'Cutting between sentences made a small clarification sound like a confession. Always look for the full statement.',
    hint: 'Are the words in the viral clip in the same order in the full video?'),
  _Clip('unlabeled_ai_ad', true, 'Health', 'Doctor testimonial',
    'Doc Ramon says this pill cures every lung problem. Buy now.',
    [
      ClipBeat(0, 'Doctor in a clinic', 'Doc: "Ito ang lunas sa lahat ng sakit sa baga."', 'Doc: "Maghugas ng kamay para makaiwas sa sakit."'),
      ClipBeat(10, 'Product shot', 'Doc: "Bumili na bago maubos."', 'Doc: "Magpatingin sa doktor kapag may sintomas."'),
      ClipBeat(20, 'Hotline', 'Doc: "Mag-order sa link."', 'Doc: "Salamat sa panonood."'),
    ],
    upload: 'The video uses footage from a {y1} public health talk by the real doctor.',
    audio: 'The voice is smooth but cuts out whenever the camera moves.',
    creator: 'A seller page created last week.',
    label: 'No AI disclosure.',
    full: 'The doctor\'s original video is about hygiene and never mentions any product.',
    rFull: Relevance.decisive, rAudio: Relevance.support, rUpload: Relevance.support,
    lesson: 'A trusted face and voice were used to sell a product the doctor never mentioned. This is an AI-edited endorsement without disclosure.',
    hint: 'What does the doctor\'s own original video talk about?'),
];

int clipScenarioCount() => _clips.length;
String clipScenarioId(int i) => _clips[i].id;
bool clipScenarioBad(int i) => _clips[i].bad;

ArcadeCase buildClip(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d, int index) {
  final c = _clips[index];
  final group = g.pick(Pools.groups);
  final slots = {
    'group': group, 'city': g.pick(Pools.cities), 'queen': g.pick(Pools.queens),
    'y1': '${g.between(2019, 2023)}',
  };
  String f(String s) => fillSlots(s, slots);
  final tools = <Finding>[
    Finding('upload', 'UPLOAD INFO', '📅', f(c.upload), c.rUpload),
    Finding('audio', 'AUDIO', '🎧', f(c.audio), c.rAudio),
    Finding('creator', 'CREATOR', '👤', f(c.creator), c.rCreator),
    Finding('label', 'LABEL', '🏷️', f(c.label), c.rLabel),
    Finding('full', 'FULL VIDEO', '🎬', f(c.full), c.rFull),
  ];
  final beats = [
    for (final b in c.beats)
      ClipBeat(b.second, f(b.scene), f(b.viralLine), f(b.fullLine),
          inViral: b.inViral),
  ];
  final decisive = tools.firstWhere((t) => t.relevance == Relevance.decisive);
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'clip:${c.id}', scenario: c.id, mode: ArcadeMode.clip,
    title: c.title, author: 'Tambayan Reels', handle: '@reels${g.id()}',
    text: f(c.caption), subtext: 'Scrub the timeline and compare both cuts.',
    imageTopic: c.topic, imageCaption: c.title, tools: tools, beats: beats,
    deceptive: c.bad, correct: [c.bad ? 1 : 0],
    choices: const [ArcadeOption('KEEP'), ArcadeOption('REMOVE')],
    lesson: f(c.lesson),
    hint: 'Open ${decisive.emoji} ${decisive.label}. Ask: ${f(c.hint)}',
  );
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_gen_impostor.dart =====nimport 'arcade_common.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// Impostor Hunt. Each story writes a small independent "directory" plus two
/// profiles. Age, followers, verified badges and polish are rolled apart from
/// the truth. The truth comes from one rule: an account is a problem only if
/// it claims authority it was never given, or if an authorized account has
/// been taken over.
class _Org {
  final String name, base, kind;
  const _Org(this.name, this.base, this.kind);
}

const _orgs = <_Org>[
  _Org('Sulit Shop', 'sulitshop', 'shop'),
  _Org('Pag-asa Science High', 'pagasashs', 'school'),
  _Org('Barangay Liwayway Hall', 'brgyliwayway', 'barangay'),
  _Org('Office of Mayor Del Rosario', 'mayordelrosario', 'politician'),
  _Org('BAHAGHARI Official', 'bahaghariofficial', 'group'),
  _Org('PesoGo', 'pesogo', 'wallet'),
  _Org('Kargo Express PH', 'kargoexpressph', 'courier'),
  _Org('Northridge Falcons', 'northridgefalcons', 'sports'),
  _Org('Tambayan Daily', 'tambayandaily', 'news'),
  _Org('LUNARIS Official', 'lunarisofficial', 'group'),
  _Org('Bili-Bili Hub', 'bilibilihub', 'shop'),
  _Org('Kalikasan College', 'kalikasancollege', 'school'),
];

const _benign = <String, List<String>>{
  'shop': ['New stocks tomorrow, order in the app!', 'Salamat sa lahat ng 5-star reviews!', 'Shipping update: orders ship Mon-Sat.'],
  'school': ['Enrollment extended until Friday.', 'Reminder: ID validation sa Room 12.', 'Foundation week schedule is out.'],
  'barangay': ['Clean-up drive sa Sabado, 7AM.', 'Schedule ng bakuna: Martes sa health center.', 'Paalala: ihanda ang ID para sa payout.'],
  'politician': ['Nag-inspect kami ng drainage project ngayong umaga.', 'Salamat sa mga volunteer ng clean-up drive.', 'Public hearing sa Huwebes, bukas sa lahat.'],
  'group': ['Comeback teaser drops tonight!', 'Salamat sa 1M views, mga fans!', 'Fan meeting details sa official website.'],
  'wallet': ['Reminder: never share your OTP with anyone.', 'App update available, download from the store.', 'Our hotline is listed in the app only.'],
  'courier': ['Tracking tip: use the app, not links from texts.', 'Holiday delivery schedule posted.', 'Hubs are closed this Sunday.'],
  'sports': ['Game day Sabado, 3PM!', 'Practice moved to 5PM.', 'Ticket info is on the school page.'],
  'news': ['Weather update, 5PM bulletin.', 'City council session recap.', 'Correction: earlier post misspelled a name.'],
};

const _scams = <String>[
  'Libreng P{amt}! Mag-register dito: {dom}-promo.example',
  'Nanalo ka! I-DM ang OTP mo para ma-claim ang prize.',
  'Para ma-refund, i-send ang card details sa DM.',
  'Magbayad ng P500 para ma-reserve ang slot mo.',
];

class _Spec {
  final List<String> dir;
  final ProfileView a, b;
  final int flagged; // 0 = a, 1 = b, -1 = neither
  final String lesson, hint;
  const _Spec(this.dir, this.a, this.b, this.flagged, this.lesson, this.hint);
}

const _stories = <String>[
  'lookalike', 'hijacked', 'rebrand', 'fan_label', 'parody_label',
  'parody_hidden', 'same_name', 'fake_support', 'two_branches', 'renamed',
  'paid_badge', 'supporters',
];

int impostorScenarioCount() => _stories.length;
String impostorScenarioId(int i) => _stories[i];

/// Stories where exactly one account must be flagged.
const _flaggedStories = {'lookalike', 'hijacked', 'parody_hidden',
  'fake_support', 'renamed', 'paid_badge'};
bool impostorScenarioFlagged(int i) => _flaggedStories.contains(_stories[i]);

String _lookalike(Gen g, String base) {
  if (base.contains('m')) return base.replaceFirst('m', 'rn');
  if (base.contains('l')) return base.replaceFirst('l', '1');
  return g.pick(['${base}_', '${base}ph', '${base}official']);
}

ArcadeCase buildImpostor(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d, int index) {
  final story = _stories[index];
  final hard = d == ArcadeDifficulty.hard;
  final easy = d == ArcadeDifficulty.easy;

  List<_Org> fit(List<String> kinds) =>
      _orgs.where((o) => kinds.contains(o.kind)).toList();
  final org = switch (story) {
    'parody_label' || 'parody_hidden' || 'supporters' =>
      g.pick(fit(['politician'])),
    'fan_label' || 'paid_badge' => g.pick(fit(['group'])),
    'fake_support' => g.pick(fit(['wallet', 'courier', 'shop'])),
    'two_branches' => g.pick(fit(['school', 'sports'])),
    _ => g.pick(_orgs),
  };
  final dom = org.base;
  final benign = _benign[org.kind]!;
  int followers() => g.between(120, 48000);
  int oldAge() => g.between(400, 2600);
  int newAge() => g.between(1, 25);
  final amt = g.pick(['500', '1,000', '5,000']);
  String scam() => fillSlots(g.pick(_scams), {'amt': amt, 'dom': dom});

  ProfileView real({int? age, String? bio, List<String>? recent}) => ProfileView(
    name: org.name, handle: '@${org.base}',
    bio: bio ?? 'Official page of ${org.name}.',
    link: '$dom.example', ageDays: age ?? oldAge(), followers: followers(),
    recent: recent ?? g.sample(benign, 2));

  final _Spec spec = switch (story) {
    'lookalike' => (() {
      final fake = ProfileView(
        name: org.name, handle: '@${_lookalike(g, org.base)}',
        bio: 'Official page. Verified ✔ Fast support!',
        link: '$dom-help.example', ageDays: g.chance(.5) ? newAge() : oldAge(),
        followers: followers(), badge: g.chance(.5),
        recent: easy || g.chance(.5) ? [scam(), benign.first] : [benign.last, benign.first]);
      return _Spec(
        ['${org.name} website lists its only account as @${org.base}.'],
        real(), fake, 1,
        "The handle is one character off from the listed account and isn't linked from the official website. Age, badge and polish don't prove identity.",
        'Compare each handle letter by letter with the directory.');
    })(),
    'hijacked' => (() {
      final hacked = ProfileView(
        name: org.name, handle: '@${org.base}',
        bio: 'Official page. Big giveaway sa pinned post!',
        link: '$dom-winners.example', ageDays: oldAge(), followers: followers(),
        badge: true, recent: [scam(), 'Last month: ${benign.first}']);
      final backup = ProfileView(
        name: '${org.name} (Backup)', handle: '@${org.base}_backup',
        bio: 'Backup page while our main account is recovered.',
        link: '$dom.example', ageDays: g.between(30, 900), followers: g.between(200, 4000),
        recent: ['Our main page was hacked. Ignore links it posts this week.', benign.first]);
      return _Spec(
        ['${org.name} website lists @${org.base} as its main page and @${org.base}_backup as its backup.'],
        hacked, backup, 0,
        "@${org.base} is the real listed account, but it was taken over: a new link, a giveaway, and the owner's backup page warns about it. The right move is to flag the hacked page, not the backup.",
        'Which account is listed, and which one is behaving differently?');
    })(),
    'rebrand' => (() {
      final old = ProfileView(
        name: org.name, handle: '@${org.base}',
        bio: 'We moved! Follow our new handle.', link: '$dom.example',
        ageDays: oldAge(), followers: followers(),
        recent: ['Pinned: "Lumipat na kami sa @${org.base}ph. Same team, same page!"', benign.first]);
      final fresh = ProfileView(
        name: org.name, handle: '@${org.base}ph',
        bio: 'New official handle of ${org.name}.', link: '$dom.example',
        ageDays: newAge(), followers: g.between(80, 1800),
        recent: [benign.first, 'Salamat sa mga sumunod sa amin dito!']);
      return _Spec(
        ['The ${org.name} website lists @${org.base}ph as its new account.'],
        old, fresh, -1,
        "A brand-new account can be the real one. The old page's pinned post and the website both point to the new handle.",
        'Is the new account announced by the old one?');
    })(),
    'fan_label' => (() {
      final fan = ProfileView(
        name: '${org.name.replaceAll(' Official', '')} Fans PH',
        handle: '@${org.base.replaceAll('official', '')}fansph',
        bio: 'Unofficial fan account. Not affiliated.', label: 'Fan account',
        link: 'no link', ageDays: g.between(30, 2000), followers: g.between(2000, 90000),
        recent: ['Fancam compilation 🎥', 'Ranking our favorite MV looks!']);
      return _Spec(
        ['${org.name} agency page lists @${org.base} as its only official account.'],
        real(), fan, -1,
        "A fan account that says it's unofficial isn't impersonating anyone, even with a bigger following.",
        'Does the second account claim to be official?');
    })(),
    'parody_label' => (() {
      final parody = ProfileView(
        name: 'Mayor Del Rosaryo (Parody)',
        handle: '@mayor_delrosaryo', label: 'Parody',
        bio: 'Parody account. Hindi totoong alkalde. Joke lang po.',
        ageDays: g.between(60, 1400), followers: g.between(1000, 30000),
        recent: ['Utos ko: libreng kape tuwing Lunes ☕ (joke)', 'Mga dalubhasa sa traffic: mga pusa.']);
      return _Spec(
        ['The city website lists @${org.base} as the mayor\'s office account.'],
        real(), parody, -1,
        "A clearly labeled parody account isn't pretending to be the real mayor. Mocking officials with a label is allowed.",
        'Does the parody account label itself?');
    })(),
    'parody_hidden' => (() {
      final fake = ProfileView(
        name: org.name, handle: '@${org.base}_pr',
        bio: 'Opisyal na page ng alkalde. Para sa mga balita at anunsyo.',
        ageDays: newAge(), followers: g.between(300, 9000),
        recent: ['Wala akong pakialam kung mamatay kayo sa baha.', 'Libreng ayuda sa mga mag-share ng post na ito!']);
      return _Spec(
        ['The city website lists @${org.base} as the mayor\'s office account.'],
        fake, real(), 0,
        "This account uses the mayor's name and claims to be official, with no parody label. It is not listed anywhere, and it posts offensive claims in the mayor's name.",
        'Does it say "parody" anywhere, or does it claim to be the real office?');
    })(),
    'same_name' => (() {
      final cityA = g.pick(Pools.cities);
      final cityB = g.pick(Pools.cities.where((c) => c != cityA).toList());
      final a = ProfileView(
        name: 'Kapitbahay Bakery', handle: '@kapitbahaybakery_${cityA.replaceAll(' ', '')}'.toLowerCase(),
        bio: 'Pandesal at kape sa $cityA.', link: 'no link',
        ageDays: oldAge(), followers: followers(),
        recent: ['Mainit na pandesal 6AM!', 'Sold out na ang ensaymada 😋']);
      final b = ProfileView(
        name: 'Kapitbahay Bakery', handle: '@kapitbahaybakery_${cityB.replaceAll(' ', '')}'.toLowerCase(),
        bio: 'Small bakery sa $cityB.', link: 'no link',
        ageDays: oldAge(), followers: followers(),
        recent: ['New flavor: ube cheese pandesal.', 'Bukas kami kahit holiday.']);
      return _Spec(
        ['Local shop listings: Kapitbahay Bakery ($cityA) @${a.handle.substring(1)}; Kapitbahay Bakery ($cityB) @${b.handle.substring(1)}.'],
        a, b, -1,
        "Two businesses can share a name in different cities. Neither claims to be the other, and both are listed separately.",
        'Do either of them claim to be the other shop?');
    })(),
    'fake_support' => (() {
      final fake = ProfileView(
        name: '${org.name} Customer Care', handle: '@${org.base}_care_ph',
        bio: '24/7 support. DM us for account fix!',
        ageDays: newAge(), followers: g.between(40, 900),
        recent: ['Replying to a complaint: "Send your OTP in DM so we can fix it."', scam()]);
      return _Spec(
        ['${org.name} says support is handled only inside its app and by @${org.base}.'],
        real(recent: [benign.first, 'We never ask for your OTP.']), fake, 1,
        "This account isn't listed, offers \"support\" in DMs, and asks for an OTP. Genuine support never asks for your OTP.",
        'Where does the company say support happens?');
    })(),
    'two_branches' => (() {
      final main = ProfileView(
        name: '${org.name} (Main)', handle: '@${org.base}_main',
        bio: 'Main campus announcements.', link: '$dom.example',
        ageDays: oldAge(), followers: followers(), recent: g.sample(benign, 2));
      final south = ProfileView(
        name: '${org.name} (Southfield)', handle: '@${org.base}_south',
        bio: 'Southfield branch announcements.', link: '$dom.example/south',
        ageDays: g.between(90, 1800), followers: followers(), recent: g.sample(benign, 2));
      return _Spec(
        ['${org.name} website lists two official pages: @${org.base}_main and @${org.base}_south.'],
        main, south, -1,
        "Both accounts are acknowledged by the organization as official branches. Similar names don't automatically mean one is fake.",
        'Does the directory list more than one official page?');
    })(),
    'renamed' => (() {
      final reused = ProfileView(
        name: org.name, handle: '@${org.base}_deals',
        bio: 'Official ${org.name}. Message us for slots!',
        link: 'no link', ageDays: oldAge(), followers: g.between(3000, 40000),
        recent: ['Name changed 3 days ago from "Mura Gadgets Resell".', scam()]);
      return _Spec(
        ['${org.name} website lists @${org.base} as its page. No other page is mentioned.'],
        reused, real(), 0,
        "An old account with lots of followers was renamed to look official. It isn't listed, and its history shows it was a different business days ago.",
        'Look at the recent activity: what did this page used to be?');
    })(),
    'paid_badge' => (() {
      final badged = ProfileView(
        name: org.name, handle: '@${org.base}_ph',
        bio: 'Official fan club. Join our VIP raffle!',
        link: '$dom-vip.example', badge: true,
        ageDays: newAge(), followers: followers(),
        recent: ['VIP raffle! Send P500 to reserve your slot.', 'Giveaway ends tonight.']);
      return _Spec(
        ['${org.name} agency page lists @${org.base} as its official account. Badges can be bought by anyone.'],
        badged, real(), 0,
        "A paid badge isn't proof. This account isn't listed by the agency, and it asks for money for a raffle slot. The real page has no badge.",
        'Is a badge the same thing as being listed in the directory?');
    })(),
    _ => (() {
      final fans = ProfileView(
        name: 'Kap. Reyes Supporters', handle: '@kapreyes_supporters',
        bio: 'Supporters page, hindi opisyal na account ng kapitan.',
        label: 'Supporters page', ageDays: g.between(30, 900),
        followers: g.between(500, 20000),
        recent: ['Salamat sa lahat ng dumalo sa clean-up drive!', 'Memes tungkol sa traffic 😂']);
      return _Spec(
        ['The barangay site lists @${org.base} as the official page.'],
        fans, real(), -1,
        "A supporters page is allowed to cheer for someone as long as it doesn't claim to be their official account.",
        'Does the supporters page say it is unofficial?');
    })(),
  };

  final flip = g.chance(.5);
  final left = flip ? spec.b : spec.a;
  final right = flip ? spec.a : spec.b;
  final flagged = spec.flagged == -1 ? -1 : (flip ? 1 - spec.flagged : spec.flagged);
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'impostor:$story:${org.base}', scenario: story,
    mode: ArcadeMode.impostor, title: org.name,
    author: 'Tambayan Identity Desk', handle: '@identitychecks',
    text: 'Two accounts use a similar name. Does either one fake an identity?',
    subtext: hard ? 'The directory is below: read it carefully.' : 'Open both profiles, then compare.',
    directory: spec.dir,
    leftProfile: left, rightProfile: right,
    deceptive: flagged != -1,
    correct: [flagged == -1 ? 2 : flagged],
    choices: const [ArcadeOption('FLAG A'), ArcadeOption('FLAG B'), ArcadeOption('BOTH OK')],
    lesson: spec.lesson, hint: spec.hint,
  );
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_gen_inbox.dart =====nimport 'arcade_common.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// Inbox Sprint. One case is a batch of emails the player sorts quickly.
/// Spam always has an objective tell in the sender/link/ask details; legit
/// mail may still look urgent, informal or full of money talk.
class _E {
  final String family, fromName, from, subject, body, why;
  final String reply, linkLabel, link, attach;
  final bool spam;
  const _E(this.family, this.spam, this.fromName, this.from, this.subject,
      this.body, this.why,
      {this.reply = '', this.linkLabel = '', this.link = '', this.attach = ''});
}

const _legit = <_E>[
  _E('scholar_status', false, 'Student Services', 'scholarships@{schoolDom}.example', 'Application received',
    "Hi {name}, we got your scholarship application. You can check the status in the student portal you already use. No action needed by email.",
    "The sender domain is the school's own, and it only points you to the portal you already use."),
  _E('receipt_store', false, '{store}', 'receipts@{storeDom}.example', 'Your receipt: order #R{id}',
    "Salamat sa order mo! Total: ₱{amt}. Naka-attach ang receipt. Makikita rin ito sa Orders ng app.",
    "It matches an order you made, comes from the store's domain, and asks you for nothing.",
    attach: 'receipt-R{id}.pdf'),
  _E('enroll_urgent', false, 'Registrar', 'registrar@{schoolDom}.example', 'Enrollment closes this Friday',
    "Reminder: enrollment closes this Friday, 5PM. Please finish on the portal at {schoolDom}.example. The registrar never asks for your password by email.",
    "It's urgent, but the deadline is real, the link goes to the school's own domain, and it asks for no password.",
    linkLabel: 'Open the portal', link: '{schoolDom}.example/portal'),
  _E('teacher_gmail', false, 'Sir Dennis (Math)', 'sir.dennis.math@mail.example', 'Quiz moved to Thursday',
    "Hi class, na-move ang quiz sa Thursday dahil sa class suspension. Same coverage. Reply here if may tanong.",
    "A teacher you know can use a personal address. It asks for nothing, and the news matches your class group.",
    reply: 'sir.dennis.math@mail.example'),
  _E('wallet_statement', false, '{wallet}', 'no-reply@{walletDom}.example', 'Your monthly statement is ready',
    "Your {wallet} statement for last month is ready. Open the {wallet} app and go to Statements. We will never ask for your PIN or OTP.",
    "It points you to the app instead of a link and warns against sharing your PIN or OTP."),
  _E('event_ticket', false, '{group} Tickets', 'tickets@{groupDom}-tix.example', 'Your e-ticket for Saturday',
    "Your ticket to the {group} fan meeting is attached. Show the QR code at the gate. Don't share it with anyone.",
    "You bought this ticket, it comes from the ticket seller's domain, and it warns you not to share the QR.",
    attach: 'eticket.pdf'),
  _E('club_meet', false, 'Photography Club', 'club@{schoolDom}.example', 'Meeting on Thursday, 4PM',
    "Hi {name}! Club meeting sa Thursday, 4PM, Room 12. Dalhin ang camera kung meron. Walang bayad.",
    "It comes from the school's club address and is free, with no link or code request."),
  _E('refund_done', false, '{store}', 'support@{storeDom}.example', 'Refund approved: R{id}',
    "Your refund of ₱{amt} was approved and will arrive in 5-7 days. You don't need to do anything.",
    "It matches a return you filed, and it asks you for nothing.",
    linkLabel: 'View your return', link: '{storeDom}.example/returns'),
  _E('city_notice', false, 'City Hall', 'notices@city-{cityDom}.example', 'Brgy. payout schedule',
    "Ang payout para sa registered seniors ay sa Sabado, 8AM sa covered court. Valid ID lang ang kailangan.",
    "It comes from the city's domain, matches the barangay's own post, and asks for no link or money."),
  _E('pw_changed', false, '{wallet}', 'security@{walletDom}.example', 'You changed your password',
    "You changed your {wallet} password a minute ago. If this wasn't you, open the app and use Help. We won't ask for your password by email.",
    "You just did this, it has no link, and it tells you to use the app if you didn't."),
];

int inboxScenarioCount() => _legit.length + _spam.length;

EmailItem _make(Gen g, _E e, Map<String, String> s) {
  String f(String x) => fillSlots(x, s);
  return EmailItem(
    fromName: f(e.fromName), fromAddress: f(e.from), subject: f(e.subject),
    body: f(e.body), spam: e.spam, why: f(e.why),
    replyTo: f(e.reply), linkLabel: f(e.linkLabel), linkTarget: f(e.link),
    attachment: f(e.attach),
  );
}

ArcadeCase buildInbox(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d, int index, {int? count}) {
  final n = count ?? switch (d) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => 3,
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 4,
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 5,
  };
  final store = g.pick(Pools.stores);
  final wallet = g.pick(Pools.wallets);
  final group = g.pick(Pools.groups);
  String dom(String s) => s.toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z]'), '');
  final slots = <String, String>{
    'name': g.pick(['Mika', 'Jules', 'Nica', 'Paolo']),
    'store': store, 'storeDom': dom(store), 'wallet': wallet,
    'walletDom': dom(wallet), 'group': group, 'groupDom': dom(group),
    'schoolDom': dom(g.pick(Pools.schools)), 'cityDom': dom(g.pick(Pools.cities)),
    'amt': g.pick(['250', '1,500', '5,000']), 'id': '${g.id()}',
  };
  // Beyond the tutorial, a batch may be all genuine or all spam.
  // This prevents players from using "there must be one of each" to win.
  final mixedTutorial = count != null;
  final extremeChance = d == ArcadeDifficulty.easy ? .12 : .25;
  final spamCount = !mixedTutorial && g.chance(extremeChance)
      ? g.pick([0, n])
      : g.between(1, n - 1);
  final chosenSpam = g.sample(_spam, spamCount);
  final chosenLegit = g.sample(_legit, n - spamCount);
  final mix = g.shuffled([...chosenSpam, ...chosenLegit]);
  final emails = [for (final e in mix) _make(g, e, slots)];
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'inbox:${mix.map((e) => e.family).toList().join(',')}',
    scenario: 'batch', mode: ArcadeMode.inbox,
    title: 'Inbox', author: 'Tambayan Mail',
    text: 'Swipe each email: left = SPAM, right = KEEP.',
    subtext: 'Tap an email to see who really sent it.',
    emails: emails, deceptive: spamCount > 0,
    correct: [for (final e in emails) e.spam ? 0 : 1],
    lesson: 'Spam can look polished and real mail can look rushed. The sender address, where a link really goes, and what the email asks you for are what count.',
    hint: 'Tap the card to flip it. Compare the sender and link with the real service, and ask what it wants from you.',
  );
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_gen_rumor.dart =====nimport 'arcade_common.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// Rumor Radar. Five puzzles, five controls:
///  0 order       drag cards to rebuild who reposted whom
///  1 spotDiff    tap the version that disagrees with the official notice
///  2 outdated    tap the post that would mislead *today* (dates, not age)
///  3 independent tap the one item that confirms the claim on its own
///  4 wordEdit    tap the words that were changed in a viral caption
int rumorScenarioCount() => RumorTask.values.length;
String rumorScenarioId(int i) => RumorTask.values[i].name;

const _escalations = <String, List<String>>{
  'fire': [
    "May sunog sa Brgy. {town}. Dalawang bahay ang apektado, wala pong nasaktan.",
    "Sunog sa Brgy. {town}! Mga 10 bahay na raw ang nasusunog.",
    "SUNOG SA {town}! Halos buong barangay na, may mga patay daw!",
    "Walang lumalabas na balita pero sunog na ang buong lungsod! Magtago na kayo!",
    "BREAKING: Sunog sa buong probinsya, itinatago ng gobyerno!",
  ],
  'flood': [
    "Hanggang bukung-bukong ang baha sa kanto ng Brgy. {town}, passable pa rin.",
    "Hanggang tuhod na ang baha sa Brgy. {town}, mag-ingat po.",
    "Hanggang bewang na ang baha sa buong {town}! Hindi na madaanan!",
    "Lubog na ang buong lungsod, rescue na ang kailangan!",
    "WALANG TUMUTULONG! Lubog ang buong probinsya, nawawala na ang mga tao!",
  ],
  'dengue': [
    "Limang kaso ng dengue ang naitala sa isang paaralan sa Brgy. {town}.",
    "Dengue sa school ng Brgy. {town}, marami na raw ang naospital.",
    "Outbreak ng dengue sa buong {town}, sarado na raw ang mga paaralan!",
    "Dengue emergency sa lungsod, daan-daang patay na daw!",
    "Lockdown na ang buong probinsya dahil sa dengue, itinatago ng gobyerno!",
  ],
  'fare': [
    "Panukala: ₱2 na dagdag-pasahe sa jeep, pag-uusapan pa sa hearing sa Huwebes.",
    "Tataas na raw ang pamasahe ng ₱2 simula Lunes.",
    "Doble na ang pamasahe simula bukas!!",
    "Hindi na makakasakay ang mga estudyante dahil sa taas ng pamasahe, kanselado na ang klase!",
    "Walang jeep na bibiyahe simula Lunes, hindi na raw babalik!",
  ],
  'road': [
    "Isang lane sa highway ang sarado ngayong gabi para sa pag-aayos.",
    "Sarado ang highway ng 3 oras ngayong gabi, humanap ng ibang daan.",
    "SARADO ang highway buong linggo! Wag na kayong dumaan!",
    "Sarado na ang buong NCR at probinsya, lahat ng tulay bagsak na!",
    "Bagsak ang tulay at sarado ang bansa, magtago na!",
  ],
};

const _eyewitness = [
  "Nandito ako mismo sa lugar. Ito ang kuha ko kanina.",
  "Personal kong nakita. Kuha ko ang larawan na ito.",
  "Residente ako dito, ako ang nag-video.",
];

String _h(Gen g) => '@${g.pick(Pools.handlesFirst)}${g.id()}';

ArcadeCase buildRumor(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d, int index) {
  switch (RumorTask.values[index]) {
    case RumorTask.order: return _order(g, d);
    case RumorTask.spotDiff: return _spot(g, d);
    case RumorTask.outdated: return _outdated(g, d);
    case RumorTask.independent: return _independent(g, d);
    case RumorTask.wordEdit: return _word(g, d);
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------- order
ArcadeCase _order(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d) {
  final topic = g.pick(_escalations.keys.toList());
  final levels = _escalations[topic]!;
  final n = switch (d) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => 3,
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 4,
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 5,
  };
  final town = g.pick(Pools.towns);
  final handles = List.generate(n, (_) => _h(g));
  // Chain position 0 is the first-hand post.
  final chain = <RumorCard>[];
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    final body = fillSlots(levels[i], {'town': town});
    final String meta;
    if (i == 0) {
      meta = g.pick(_eyewitness);
    } else if (g.chance(.5)) {
      meta = 'Shared from ${handles[i - 1]}';
    } else {
      meta = "Screenshot of ${handles[i - 1]}'s post";
    }
    chain.add(RumorCard(handles[i], body, meta));
  }
  // Shuffle for display; make sure it isn't already solved.
  var perm = g.shuffled([for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) i]);
  var tries = 0;
  while (_isIdentity(perm) && tries++ < 8) {
    perm = g.shuffled([for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) i]);
  }
  final cards = [for (final i in perm) chain[i]];
  // correct: display indexes in chain order (origin first).
  final correct = [for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) perm.indexOf(i)];
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'rumor:order:$topic:$n', scenario: 'order', mode: ArcadeMode.rumor,
    title: 'Who started it?', rumorTask: RumorTask.order,
    text: 'Use the arrows: first-hand post at the top, newest repost last.',
    subtext: 'Each card says who it copied from.',
    cards: cards, correct: correct, deceptive: true,
    lesson: "Each repost copies the one before it, and the story grows. The post at the top is first-hand, and the later ones add details nobody there confirmed.",
    hint: 'Start with the card that says it saw it personally. Then follow each "Shared from".',
  );
}

bool _isIdentity(List<int> p) {
  for (var i = 0; i < p.length; i++) {
    if (p[i] != i) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- spotDiff
ArcadeCase _spot(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d) {
  final days = ['Sabado', 'Linggo', 'Biyernes', 'Huwebes'];
  final times = ['8:00 AM', '9:30 AM', '1:00 PM', '3:00 PM'];
  final venues = ['covered court ng Brgy. {town}', 'Health Center ng Brgy. {town}',
    'gym ng {school}', 'plaza ng {city}'];
  final fees = ['Libre', 'Libre', 'Libre'];
  final town = g.pick(Pools.towns);
  final slots = {
    'town': town, 'school': g.pick(Pools.schools), 'city': g.pick(Pools.cities),
  };
  final day = g.pick(days), time = g.pick(times);
  final venue = fillSlots(g.pick(venues), slots);
  final fee = g.pick(fees);
  final phone = '0917 ${g.between(100, 999)} ${g.between(1000, 9999)}';
  final event = g.pick(['Libreng medical mission', 'Libreng bakuna',
    'Scholarship briefing', 'Job fair', 'Blood donation drive']);
  final attrs = ['day', 'time', 'venue', 'fee', 'phone'];
  final changed = g.pick(attrs);
  String render(String Function(String a, String real) tweak) {
    String v(String a, String real) => tweak(a, real);
    return '$event: ${v('day', day)}, ${v('time', time)}, ${v('venue', venue)}. '
        '${v('fee', fee)}. Hotline: ${v('phone', phone)}';
  }
  String altered(String a, String real) {
    if (a != changed) return real;
    return switch (a) {
      'day' => g.pick(days.where((x) => x != day).toList()),
      'time' => g.pick(times.where((x) => x != time).toList()),
      'venue' => fillSlots(g.pick(venues.where((x) => fillSlots(x, slots) != venue).toList()), slots),
      'fee' => '₱${g.pick(['50', '150', '300'])} registration',
      _ => '0917 ${g.between(100, 999)} ${g.between(1000, 9999)}',
    };
  }
  final official = render((a, real) => real);
  final faithful = render((a, real) => real);
  final n = d == ArcadeDifficulty.hard ? 4 : 3;
  final wrongOne = render(altered);
  final texts = <String>[wrongOne];
  while (texts.length < n) {
    // Hard mode requires semantic comparison, not simply finding the lone
    // string that isn't byte-for-byte identical to its neighbours.
    texts.add(d == ArcadeDifficulty.hard
        ? g.pick(['Reposted notice: $faithful', 'Forwarded: $faithful',
            'Shared community announcement: $faithful'])
        : faithful);
  }
  final sources = g.shuffled(['Group chat forward', 'Page repost',
    'Screenshot in a story', 'Fan page', 'Barangay board repost']).take(n).toList();
  final perm = g.shuffled([for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) i]);
  final cards = [for (final i in perm) RumorCard('Version ${perm.indexOf(i) + 1}', texts[i], sources[i])];
  final correct = perm.indexOf(0);
  final label = switch (changed) {
    'day' => 'day', 'time' => 'time', 'venue' => 'place',
    'fee' => 'fee', _ => 'hotline',
  };
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'rumor:spot:$changed:$n', scenario: 'spotDiff', mode: ArcadeMode.rumor,
    title: 'Which one was changed?', rumorTask: RumorTask.spotDiff,
    text: 'Which version changes a fact from the official notice? Ignore forwarding labels.',
    subtext: 'OFFICIAL NOTICE: $official',
    cards: cards, correct: [correct], deceptive: true,
    lesson: "Only the $label changed in one version. Small edits to a date, fee or hotline are how scammers redirect people, so compare each detail line by line.",
    hint: 'Read each version line by line against the official notice. What detail is different?',
  );
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------- outdated
ArcadeCase _outdated(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d) {
  final months = ['Oct', 'Nov'];
  final todayDay = g.between(10, 22);
  final todayMonth = months.first;
  String dateAt(int offset) {
    var day = todayDay + offset;
    var month = todayMonth;
    if (day > 31) { day -= 31; month = 'Nov'; }
    if (day < 1) { day += 30; month = 'Sep'; }
    return '$month $day';
  }
  final what = g.pick([
    ('Enrollment deadline', 'Enrollment sa {school} hanggang'),
    ('Promo end', 'Libreng sakay promo hanggang'),
    ('Registration', 'Registration para sa scholarship hanggang'),
    ('Payout', 'Payout para sa registered seniors sa'),
  ]);
  final school = g.pick(Pools.schools);
  String line(int deadlineOffset) =>
      '${fillSlots(what.$2, {'school': school})} ${dateAt(deadlineOffset)}.';
  final n = d == ArcadeDifficulty.easy ? 3 : 4;
  // Stale post can be an old post OR a freshly re-shared old poster.
  final recycled = d == ArcadeDifficulty.easy ? false : g.chance(.6);
  final items = <(RumorCard, bool)>[];
  items.add((RumorCard('Page A', line(-g.between(4, 9)),
      recycled ? 'Posted yesterday' : 'Posted ${g.between(18, 30)} days ago'), true));
  items.add((RumorCard('Page B', line(g.between(6, 14)),
      'Posted ${g.between(20, 35)} days ago'), false)); // old but still valid
  items.add((RumorCard('Page C', line(g.between(10, 20)),
      'Posted ${g.between(1, 3)} days ago'), false));
  if (n == 4) {
    items.add((RumorCard('Page D', line(g.between(3, 9)), 'Posted today'), false));
  }
  final perm = g.shuffled([for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) i]);
  final cards = [for (final i in perm) items[i].$1];
  final correct = perm.indexWhere((i) => items[i].$2);
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'rumor:outdated:${what.$1}:${recycled ? 1 : 0}:$n',
    scenario: 'outdated', mode: ArcadeMode.rumor,
    title: 'What is stale?', rumorTask: RumorTask.outdated,
    text: 'Today is ${dateAt(0)}. Which post would mislead people if you shared it now?',
    subtext: 'Look at the deadline, not just when it was posted.',
    cards: cards, correct: [correct], deceptive: true,
    lesson: "The stale post has a deadline that's already passed. The post date can fool you: an old post can still be valid, and a brand-new post can recycle an old deadline.",
    hint: 'Compare each deadline to today. Ignore how old each post is.',
  );
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------- independent
ArcadeCase _independent(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d) {
  final claim = g.pick([
    'Libreng sakay para sa mga estudyante sa Lunes',
    'Bagong bayad sa e-wallet para sa ayuda',
    'Sarado ang tulay sa Brgy. {town} sa Sabado',
    'Nag-apruba ang konseho ng ordinansa sa single-use plastic',
  ]);
  final town = g.pick(Pools.towns);
  final c = fillSlots(claim, {'town': town});
  final outlet = g.pick(Pools.outlets);
  final real = <RumorCard>[
    RumorCard("City Hall's own notice", 'Signed, dated notice on the official page: "$c".', 'Basis: its own decision'),
    RumorCard('Council meeting record', 'The council secretary\'s posted minutes include: "$c".', 'Basis: the meeting itself'),
    RumorCard('On-scene video', 'Reporter\'s own video with a time stamp and a visible street sign: "$c".', 'Basis: filmed on location'),
  ];
  final fake = <RumorCard>[
    RumorCard('Viral repost', '"$c" with 3.2K shares.', 'Basis: same screenshot as the first post'),
    RumorCard('$outlet article', 'Headline: "$c, netizens say".', 'Basis: cites "a viral post"'),
    const RumorCard('Comment thread', '"Totoo \'yan! Sabi ng kapitbahay ko."', 'Basis: heard from a neighbor'),
    const RumorCard('Fan page copy', 'Same wording as the viral post, posted 2 hours later.', 'Basis: copied the viral post'),
    const RumorCard('"Insider" screenshot', 'A blurry screenshot of a chat, no names visible.', 'Basis: unnamed source'),
  ];
  final n = d == ArcadeDifficulty.easy ? 3 : 4;
  final good = g.pick(real);
  final all = [good, ...g.sample(fake, n - 1)];
  final perm = g.shuffled([for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) i]);
  final cards = [for (final i in perm) all[i]];
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'rumor:independent:${real.indexOf(good)}:${all.skip(1).map((e) => e.title).join('|')}',
    scenario: 'independent', mode: ArcadeMode.rumor,
    title: 'Where is the proof?', rumorTask: RumorTask.independent,
    text: 'Tap the one item that confirms the claim by itself, without copying the viral post.',
    subtext: 'Claim: $c',
    cards: cards, correct: [perm.indexOf(0)], deceptive: true,
    lesson: "Independent proof comes from the people or place that made the decision, or from someone who was physically there. A big outlet that just cites a viral post isn't proof, and neither is the 3.2K shares.",
    hint: 'Read each "Basis" line. Which one does not depend on the viral post?',
  );
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------- wordEdit
class _Quote {
  final String speaker, orig, edit;
  const _Quote(this.speaker, this.orig, this.edit);
}

const _quotes = <_Quote>[
  _Quote('Mayor Santillan', "Hindi pa final ang desisyon, pag-aaralan pa namin sa Lunes.", "[[Final na]] ang desisyon, pag-aaralan pa namin sa Lunes."),
  _Quote('Registrar', "Ang bayad sa enrollment ay ₱500 lang para sa mga scholar.", "Ang bayad sa enrollment ay [[₱5,000]] lang para sa mga scholar."),
  _Quote('Education officer', "Sinuspinde ang klase sa elementarya lamang.", "Sinuspinde ang klase sa [[lahat ng antas]] lamang."),
  _Quote('Organizer', "Kung walang pila, tapos na ang pamimigay bago mag-tanghali.", "[[Dahil]] walang pila, tapos na ang pamimigay bago mag-tanghali."),
  _Quote('Singer', "Hindi ko sinabing pangit ang kanta, kailangan lang ng practice.", "[[Pangit]] ang kanta, kailangan lang ng practice."),
  _Quote('Coach', "Natalo kami ng 2 puntos, pero maganda ang laro ng team.", "Natalo kami ng [[20]] puntos, pero maganda ang laro ng team."),
  _Quote('Event host', "Tuloy ang concert sa Sabado kung walang bagyo.", "Tuloy ang concert sa Sabado [[kahit may]] bagyo."),
  _Quote('Engineer', "Ang proyekto ay 80% tapos na at matatapos sa Enero.", "Ang proyekto ay [[100%]] tapos na at matatapos sa Enero."),
  _Quote('Kapitan', "Humihingi ako ng paumanhin sa mga naabala.", "[[Wala akong pakialam]] sa mga naabala."),
  _Quote('Nurse', "Isa lang ang nasaktan at ligtas na siya ngayon.", "[[Sampu]] ang nasaktan at ligtas na siya ngayon."),
];

ArcadeCase _word(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d) {
  final q = g.pick(_quotes);
  final tokens = <String>[];
  final changed = <int>[];
  var inMark = false;
  for (final raw in q.edit.split(' ')) {
    var t = raw;
    if (t.startsWith('[[')) { inMark = true; t = t.substring(2); }
    final ends = t.endsWith(']]');
    if (ends) t = t.substring(0, t.length - 2);
    if (inMark) changed.add(tokens.length);
    tokens.add(t);
    if (ends) inMark = false;
  }
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'rumor:words:${q.speaker}:${q.orig.length}', scenario: 'wordEdit',
    mode: ArcadeMode.rumor, title: 'Spot the edit', rumorTask: RumorTask.wordEdit,
    text: 'Tap the words in the viral caption that were changed.',
    subtext: 'FULL RECORDING (${q.speaker}): "${q.orig}"',
    tokens: tokens, correct: changed, deceptive: true,
    lesson: "Changing a single word can flip a statement. Always compare a viral quote with the full recording.",
    hint: 'Read the full recording first, then compare it word by word.',
  );
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_gen_thread.dart =====nimport 'arcade_common.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// Comment Chaos. The rule has three parts: criticism is allowed (even harsh),
/// but threats, coordinated harassment / doxxing, scams and hate toward a
/// group of people are not. Zero, one or two comments may break the rules.
class _C {
  final String text, reason, context;
  final bool bad;
  const _C(this.bad, this.text, {this.reason = '', this.context = ''});
}

const _allowed = <_C>[
  _C(false, "Ang pangit ng ending, sayang ang oras ko. Walang kwenta ang writing."),
  _C(false, "Hindi ko bet si {t} dito, bakit ba siya laging ganito 😭 pero okay lang, opinion ko lang."),
  _C(false, "Pwede bang magpaliwanag si {t}? Maraming tanong, kailangan ng sagot."),
  _C(false, "Ang tagal ng sagot ng admin. Nakakainis na, sana may update man lang."),
  _C(false, "Mas magaling pa sa kanila ang kapatid ko, seryoso 😂"),
  _C(false, "May nag-DM sa akin ng \"hahanapin ka namin\". Report n'yo po ang account na 'yon, hindi ako okay.",
      context: 'Quoting a threat in order to report it.'),
  _C(false, "Hindi dapat tinatago ang detalye. Public funds po 'to, maghintay tayo ng sagot."),
  _C(false, "Nakakadismaya, pero mas okay kung pag-usapan natin ang isyu, hindi ang tao."),
  _C(false, "Bumaba ang respeto ko kay {t} after nito. Sana mag-sorry."),
  _C(false, "LOL, ang bastos ng edit pero hindi naman nakakasakit. Funny 😭"),
  _C(false, "Nag-resign na sana si {t} kung may delikadesa. Opinion ko 'yan."),
  _C(false, "Tama na ang hate, mga besh. Okay lang mag-disagree pero respeto naman."),
  _C(false, "Hindi ko sinasabing kasalanan niya, pero responsibilidad niya 'to bilang opisyal."),
  _C(false, "Ang init ng ulo ng lahat dito, pero wala namang threat kaya hayaan na natin."),
  _C(false, "Nakakaasar yung ads sa gitna ng video, sobrang dami 😤"),
  _C(false, "Ayoko sa takbo ng palabas, pero mahal ko pa rin ang mga fans, mababait naman."),
];

const _bad = <_C>[
  _C(true, "Hahanapin ka namin pag-uwi mo, {t}. Mag-ingat ka na lang.",
      reason: 'A threat aimed at a person.'),
  _C(true, "Eto number niya: 0917 000 {n}. Tawagan n'yo hanggang magsorry!",
      reason: 'Sharing private contact details to get people to harass someone.'),
  _C(true, "Mass report natin si {t} hanggang ma-ban. Spam natin ang inbox niya 24/7!",
      reason: 'Organizing a pile-on to harass someone.'),
  _C(true, "DM me para sa ₱5,000 ayuda. I-send lang ang OTP mo para ma-verify.",
      reason: 'A scam: no real program asks for your OTP.'),
  _C(true, "Mga taga-probinsya talaga, lahat magnanakaw. Dapat hindi na pinapapasok dito.",
      reason: 'Hate toward a whole group of people based on where they are from.'),
  _C(true, "Ang taba mo, huwag ka nang magpakita sa school. Walang may gusto sa'yo.",
      reason: 'Targeted body-shaming and harassment.'),
  _C(true, "Dapat saktan na ang mga ganyan. Antayin natin sa labas ng school bukas.",
      reason: 'A call for violence against people.'),
  _C(true, "Eto address ni {t}: Blk 4 Lot 9. Puntahan natin sila ngayong gabi.",
      reason: 'Posting a home address and urging people to go there.'),
  _C(true, "Libre ticket sa concert! I-click ang link sa bio ko at ilagay ang card details.",
      reason: 'A scam trying to collect card details.'),
  _C(true, "Burahin mo na ang post mo o ipapakalat namin ang private photos mo.",
      reason: 'Blackmail: threatening to spread private photos.'),
];

class _Topic {
  final String id, post, target;
  const _Topic(this.id, this.post, this.target);
}

const _topics = <_Topic>[
  _Topic('drag_finale', 'Grand finale ng Drag Supreme PH: sino ang nanalo?', 'Miss Talahib'),
  _Topic('flood_project', 'City Hall: update sa flood wall project sa Brgy. Sinag', 'Mayor Del Rosario'),
  _Topic('ppop_mv', 'Bagong music video ng BAHAGHARI, ano sa tingin n\'yo?', 'BAHAGHARI'),
  _Topic('school_rule', 'Bagong ID at uniform policy sa Northridge University', 'ang admin'),
  _Topic('interu_loss', 'Natalo ang Falcons sa Inter-U Games finals', 'Coach Ramos'),
  _Topic('tickets', 'Sold out na ang tickets ng LUNARIS Manila concert', 'ang ticketing page'),
  _Topic('sc_election', 'Student council election: sino ang iboboto n\'yo?', 'si Mara'),
  _Topic('vlog', 'Viral na vlog ng sikat na food vlogger sa Manila', 'ang vlogger'),
];

int threadScenarioCount() => _topics.length;
String threadScenarioId(int i) => _topics[i].id;

ArcadeCase buildThread(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d, int index, {int? forceBad}) {
  final t = _topics[index];
  final n = switch (d) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => 4,
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 5,
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 6,
  };
  final roll = g.r.nextInt(10);
  var badCount = forceBad ?? (roll < 2 ? 0 : roll < 7 ? 1 : 2);
  // Zero-violation threads are possible at every difficulty: don't reward
  // the habit of always flagging someone. Tutorial still forces one.
  final slots = <String, String>{'t': t.target, 'n': '${g.between(100, 999)}'};
  final pickedBad = g.sample(_bad, badCount);
  // Harsh-but-allowed comments are the point: always include some.
  final pickedGood = g.sample(_allowed, n - badCount);
  final all = g.shuffled([...pickedBad, ...pickedGood]);
  final views = <CommentView>[];
  final flagged = <int>[];
  for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
    final c = all[i];
    if (c.bad) flagged.add(i);
    views.add(CommentView(
      '@${g.pick(Pools.handlesFirst)}${g.id()}', fillSlots(c.text, slots),
      context: c.context.isNotEmpty ? c.context
          : g.chance(.3) ? 'Replying to ${t.target}' : '',
      violates: c.bad, reason: c.reason,
    ));
  }
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'thread:${t.id}:${all.map((e) => e.text.hashCode % 997).join('.')}',
    scenario: t.id, mode: ArcadeMode.comments,
    title: t.post, author: 'Tambayan Thread',
    handle: '${g.between(12, 80)} reported comments',
    text: 'Which comments break the rules? Maybe none.',
    subtext: 'Criticism is allowed. Threats, doxxing, scams and hate are not.',
    comments: views, correct: flagged, deceptive: badCount > 0,
    lesson: badCount == 0
        ? 'No comment here breaks the rules. Some are rude or angry, but criticism and strong opinions are allowed, and reporting a threat is not making one.'
        : 'Strong opinions are allowed. What gets removed: threats, sharing private details, organized harassment, scams and hate toward whole groups.',
    hint: 'Ask of each comment: is it an opinion, or is it threatening, exposing, scamming or targeting someone?',
  );
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\arcade_gen_trust.dart =====nimport 'arcade_common.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';

/// Post Autopsy. Each scenario fixes the truth first, then writes five
/// inspection results. Account details, tone and engagement are rolled
/// independently of the truth, so they never predict the verdict.
class _Post {
  final String id, topic, caption, q, lesson;
  final bool bad;
  final List<String> bodies;
  final String photo, date, source, replies, photoLabel;
  final Relevance rPhoto, rDate, rSource, rReplies;
  final String? account;
  final Relevance rAccount;
  const _Post(this.id, this.bad, this.topic, this.caption, this.bodies, {
    required this.q, required this.lesson,
    required this.photo, required this.date, required this.source,
    required this.replies,
    this.rPhoto = Relevance.support, this.rDate = Relevance.decoy,
    this.rSource = Relevance.decisive, this.rReplies = Relevance.support,
    this.account, this.rAccount = Relevance.decoy,
    this.photoLabel = 'MEDIA TRACE',
  });
}

const _accounts = <String>[
  "Established page, 7 yrs, 120K followers. Mostly news and memes.",
  "New page, 9 days old, 340 followers. Posts only this week.",
  "Personal account, 3 yrs, 800 followers. Real name, school and photos.",

const _hypeHead = ['🚨 ALERT: ', 'BREAKING: ', '📢 PAKI-SHARE: ', 'UPDATE: '];
const _hypeTail = [' SHARE NA!! 🚨', ' Pa-viral naman 🙏', ' Basahin hanggang dulo!!'];
const _calmTail = [' Salamat po.', ' Fyi lang.', ' Ingat tayo lahat.'];

String _titleCase(String w) => w[0] + w.substring(1).toLowerCase();

/// Toned-down version: no shouting, one calm sentence ender.
String _calm(String text) {
  var t = text.replaceAllMapped(RegExp(r'\b[A-Z]{4,}\b'), (m) => _titleCase(m[0]!));
  t = t.replaceAll(RegExp(r'!{2,}'), '.').replaceAll('?!', '?');
  return t.replaceAll(' 😡', '').replaceAll(' 😱', '');
}

String _applyRegister(Gen g, String body) {
  final roll = g.r.nextDouble();
  if (roll < .34) {
    final head = g.chance(.6) ? g.pick(_hypeHead) : '';
    return head + body + g.pick(_hypeTail);
  }
  if (roll < .84) return _calm(body) + g.pick(_calmTail);
  return body;
}

int trustScenarioCount() => _posts.length;
String trustScenarioId(int i) => _posts[i].id;
bool trustScenarioBad(int i) => _posts[i].bad;

ArcadeCase buildTrust(Gen g, ArcadeDifficulty d, int index) {
  final p = _posts[index];
  final town = g.pick(Pools.towns);
  final town2 = g.pick(Pools.towns.where((t) => t != town).toList());
  final slots = <String, String>{
    'town': town, 'town2': town2, 'city': g.pick(Pools.cities),
    'who': g.pick(Pools.officials), 'amt': g.pick(['85', '120', '250', '5,000']),
    'outlet': g.pick(Pools.outlets), 'group': g.pick(Pools.groups),
    'school': g.pick(Pools.schools), 'store': g.pick(Pools.stores),
    'wallet': g.pick(Pools.wallets), 'y1': '${g.between(2019, 2023)}',
    'queen': g.pick(Pools.queens),
  };
  // Register (shouting, plain or calm) is rolled independently of the truth:
  // honest posts can shout, and false ones can sound perfectly calm.
  final body = _applyRegister(g, fillSlots(g.pick(p.bodies), slots));
  // In hard mode, social proof is UNRELIABLE rather than always reversed.
  // Its popularity, confidence and opinion are unrelated to the truth;
  // otherwise players could learn to invert the top comment for easy XP.
  var replies = fillSlots(p.replies, slots);
  if (d == ArcadeDifficulty.hard) {
    final opinion = g.pick([
      "Legit 'to, nakita ko rin sa GC.",
      "Fake 'to sabi ng pinsan ko.",
      'May nagsabi lang, better check the source first.',
      'Mukhang tunay, pero wala pa akong makita na official source.',
      'Hindi enough ang dami ng shares para malaman kung totoo.',
    ]);
    replies += ' Top comment (${g.between(18, 950)} likes): "$opinion"';
  }
  final author = p.id == 'satire_page' ? 'Kalokohan PH'
      : g.pick(['Balita Ngayon', 'Kanto Updates', 'Tambayan Feed', 'Sikat PH',
        'Barkada Bulletin', 'Mika R.', 'Paolo D.']);
  final tools = <Finding>[
    Finding('photo', p.photoLabel, '🖼️', fillSlots(p.photo, slots), p.rPhoto),
    Finding('account', 'ACCOUNT', '👤', p.account ?? g.pick(_accounts), p.rAccount),
    Finding('date', 'DATE CHECK', '🕒', fillSlots(p.date, slots), p.rDate),
    Finding('source', 'ORIGINAL SOURCE', '🔎', fillSlots(p.source, slots), p.rSource),
    Finding('replies', 'REPLIES', '💬', replies, p.rReplies),
  ];
  final decisive = tools.firstWhere((t) => t.relevance == Relevance.decisive);
  return ArcadeCase(
    signature: 'trust:${p.id}', scenario: p.id, mode: ArcadeMode.trust,
    title: 'Trending in Tambayan', author: author,
    handle: '@${author.toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z]'), '')}${g.id()}',
    text: body, subtext: 'Open up to ${d.scanBudget} checks',
    imageTopic: p.topic, imageCaption: p.caption,
    reactions: '${g.between(1, 49)}K views · ${g.between(80, 4000)} shares',
    tools: tools, deceptive: p.bad, correct: [p.bad ? 1 : 0],
    choices: const [ArcadeOption('KEEP'), ArcadeOption('REMOVE')],
    lesson: fillSlots(p.lesson, slots),
    hint: 'Try ${decisive.emoji} ${decisive.label}. Ask: ${fillSlots(p.q, slots)}',
  );
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\auth_service.dart =====nimport 'dart:async';
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:firebase_core/firebase_core.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

/// A message that is safe to show directly to the player.
class AuthException implements Exception {
  final String message;
  const AuthException(this.message);
  @override
  String toString() => message;
}

/// Optional online accounts. The game never requires this: if Firebase is
/// missing or the player is offline, [available] / [signedIn] are simply
/// false and everything keeps working from local storage.
///
/// Firestore layout used here:
///   users/{uid}              -> { uid, username, usernameLower, createdAt }
///   usernames/{usernameLower} -> { uid, createdAt }   (makes names unique)
class AuthService extends ChangeNotifier {
  AuthService._();
  static final AuthService instance = AuthService._();

  /// 3-16 characters: letters, numbers, underscore.
  static final RegExp usernamePattern = RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_]{3,16}$');

  bool available = false; // Firebase initialised successfully
  bool busy = false; // a sign-in / register call is running
  bool profileLoaded = false; // users/{uid} has been read (or created)
  bool profileError = false; // reading users/{uid} failed (offline, etc.)
  User? user;
  String? username;

  bool _registering = false;
  StreamSubscription<User?>? _sub;

  FirebaseAuth get _auth => FirebaseAuth.instance;
  FirebaseFirestore get _db => FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  bool get signedIn => user != null;
  bool get needsUsername => user != null && profileLoaded && username == null;

  /// Call once from main() right after Firebase.initializeApp succeeds.
  void init() {
    if (Firebase.apps.isEmpty) return;
    available = true;
    _sub?.cancel();
    _sub = _auth.authStateChanges().listen(_onAuthChanged);
  }

  Future<void> _onAuthChanged(User? next) async {
    user = next;
    username = null;
    profileLoaded = false;
    profileError = false;
    notifyListeners();
    if (next != null && !_registering) await reloadProfile();
  }

  Future<void> reloadProfile() async {
    final current = user;
    if (current == null) return;
    profileError = false;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final snap = await _db.collection('users').doc(current.uid).get();
      if (user?.uid != current.uid) return; // signed out meanwhile
      username = snap.data()?['username'] as String?;
      profileLoaded = true;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Could not load profile: $e');
      profileError = true;
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- usernames

  String _checkedUsername(String raw) {
    final name = raw.trim();
    if (!usernamePattern.hasMatch(name)) {
      throw const AuthException(
          'Username must be 3–16 characters: letters, numbers or underscore.');
    }
    return name;
  }

  /// Case-insensitive: "Ollie" and "ollie" are the same username.
  Future<bool> isUsernameAvailable(String name) async {
    final trimmed = name.trim();
    if (!usernamePattern.hasMatch(trimmed)) return false;
    final snap =
        await _db.collection('usernames').doc(trimmed.toLowerCase()).get();
    return !snap.exists;
  }

  /// Reserves the username and creates the player record in ONE transaction,
  /// so two players can never end up with the same name.
  Future<void> _claim(User u, String name) async {
    final lower = name.toLowerCase();
    final nameRef = _db.collection('usernames').doc(lower);
    final userRef = _db.collection('users').doc(u.uid);
    await _db.runTransaction((tx) async {
      final existing = await tx.get(nameRef);
      if (existing.exists) {
        throw const AuthException('That username is already taken.');
      }
      tx.set(nameRef, {
        'uid': u.uid,
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
      tx.set(userRef, {
        'uid': u.uid,
        'username': name,
        'usernameLower': lower,
        'createdAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      });
    });
    username = name;
    profileLoaded = true;
    profileError = false;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ actions

  /// E-mail + password account with a unique username.
  Future<void> register({
    required String email,
    required String password,
    required String username,
  }) async {
    final name = _checkedUsername(username);
    await _run(() async {
      if (!await isUsernameAvailable(name)) {
        throw const AuthException('That username is already taken.');
      }
      final cred = await _auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(
          email: email.trim(), password: password);
      try {
        await _claim(cred.user!, name);
      } catch (_) {
        // Roll back so the e-mail is free again and the player can retry.
        await cred.user?.delete();
        rethrow;
      }
    }, registering: true);
  }

  Future<void> signIn({required String email, required String password}) =>
      _run(() async {
        await _auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(
            email: email.trim(), password: password);
      });

  /// Google sign-in. New Google players still have to pick a username
  /// ([needsUsername] becomes true) before they can appear on leaderboards.
  Future<void> signInWithGoogle() => _run(() async {
        final provider = GoogleAuthProvider();
        if (kIsWeb) {
          await _auth.signInWithPopup(provider);
        } else {
          await _auth.signInWithProvider(provider);
        }
      });

  /// For players who signed in with Google and have no username yet.
  Future<void> chooseUsername(String raw) async {
    final name = _checkedUsername(raw);
    final current = user;
    if (current == null) throw const AuthException('Please sign in first.');
    await _run(() async {
      if (!await isUsernameAvailable(name)) {
        throw const AuthException('That username is already taken.');
      }
      await _claim(current, name);
    });
  }

  Future<void> signOut() async {
    if (!available) return;
    await _auth.signOut();
  }

  // ----------------------------------------------------------------- plumbing

  Future<void> _run(Future<void> Function() action,
      {bool registering = false}) async {
    if (!available) {
      throw const AuthException('Online features are not available right now.');
    }
    busy = true;
    _registering = registering;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await action();
    } on AuthException {
      rethrow;
    } on FirebaseAuthException catch (e) {
      throw AuthException(_authMessage(e.code));
    } on FirebaseException catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Firebase error: ${e.code} ${e.message}');
      throw AuthException(e.code == 'unavailable'
          ? 'No connection. Check your internet and try again.'
          : 'Something went wrong (${e.code}). Please try again.');
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      _registering = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  String _authMessage(String code) => switch (code) {
        'invalid-email' => 'That email address does not look right.',
        'user-disabled' => 'This account has been disabled.',
        'user-not-found' ||
        'wrong-password' ||
        'invalid-credential' =>
          'Wrong email or password.',
        'email-already-in-use' =>
          'An account with that email already exists. Try signing in.',
        'weak-password' => 'Password is too weak. Use at least 6 characters.',
        'network-request-failed' =>
          'No connection. Check your internet and try again.',
        'too-many-requests' =>
          'Too many attempts. Please wait a moment and try again.',
        'operation-not-allowed' =>
          'This sign-in method is not enabled in the Firebase console.',
        'popup-closed-by-user' ||
        'cancelled-popup-request' ||
        'canceled' ||
        'web-context-canceled' =>
          'Sign-in was cancelled.',
        _ => 'Sign-in failed ($code). Please try again.',
      };
}
n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\backend_client.dart =====nimport 'dart:convert';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

class BackendException implements Exception {
  final int statusCode;
  final String message;
  const BackendException(this.statusCode, this.message);
  @override
  String toString() => 'BackendException($statusCode): $message';
}

/// Authenticated JSON client for the Node.js/Express backend hosted on Vercel.
///
/// Every request carries the player's Firebase ID token. The backend verifies
/// it with the Firebase Admin SDK, so the uid is never taken from the body.
class BackendClient {
  BackendClient._();
  static final BackendClient instance = BackendClient._();

  /// Override at build time:
  ///   flutter run --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=https://your-project.vercel.app
  /// or edit the defaultValue below.
  static const String _rawBaseUrl = String.fromEnvironment(
    'API_BASE_URL',
    defaultValue: 'https://YOUR-PROJECT.vercel.app',
  );

  static String get baseUrl => _rawBaseUrl.endsWith('/')
      ? _rawBaseUrl.substring(0, _rawBaseUrl.length - 1)
      : _rawBaseUrl;

  /// False until a real backend URL has been configured.
  static bool get isConfigured => !baseUrl.contains('YOUR-PROJECT');

  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> post(
    String path,
    Map<String, dynamic> body, {
    Duration timeout = const Duration(seconds: 25),
  }) async {
    if (!isConfigured) {
      throw const BackendException(0, 'API_BASE_URL is not configured.');
    }
    final user = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser;
    if (user == null) {
      throw const BackendException(401, 'Sign in first.');
    }

    var res = await _send(user, path, body, timeout, forceRefresh: false);
    // An expired or revoked ID token: fetch a fresh one and retry exactly once.
    if (res.statusCode == 401) {
      res = await _send(user, path, body, timeout, forceRefresh: true);
    }

    final decoded = _decode(res.body);
    if (res.statusCode < 200 || res.statusCode >= 300) {
      final msg =
          (decoded['error'] ?? 'Request failed (${res.statusCode})').toString();
      throw BackendException(res.statusCode, msg);
    }
    return decoded;
  }

  Future<http.Response> _send(
    User user,
    String path,
    Map<String, dynamic> body,
    Duration timeout, {
    required bool forceRefresh,
  }) async {
    final token = await user.getIdToken(forceRefresh);
    if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
      throw const BackendException(401, 'Missing auth token.');
    }
    return http
        .post(
          Uri.parse('$baseUrl$path'),
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'Authorization': 'Bearer $token',
          },
          body: jsonEncode(body),
        )
        .timeout(timeout);
  }

  /// Tolerates empty bodies and non-JSON error pages (for example an HTML 502
  /// from the host) instead of crashing with a FormatException.
  Map<String, dynamic> _decode(String raw) {
    if (raw.isEmpty) return <String, dynamic>{};
    try {
      final parsed = jsonDecode(raw);
      if (parsed is Map) {
        return parsed.map((k, v) => MapEntry(k.toString(), v));
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // fall through
    }
    return <String, dynamic>{};
  }
}
n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\cloud_save_service.dart =====nimport 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import '../controllers/engine.dart';
import 'auth_service.dart';
import 'backend_client.dart';

/// Syncs the player's progress (XP, level, unlocked cosmetics, stats) with
/// the Express backend so it follows the account across devices.
class CloudSaveService {
  CloudSaveService._();
  static final CloudSaveService instance = CloudSaveService._();

  static const _linkedUidKey = 'cloud_uid_v1';

  GameEngine? _engine;
  Timer? _debounce;
  bool _syncing = false;
  String? _signInSyncedUid;

  /// Call once, after GameEngine.load() has finished.
  void attach(GameEngine engine) {
    _engine = engine;
    engine.onSaved = _scheduleSync;
    AuthService.instance.addListener(_onAuthChanged);
    _onAuthChanged();
  }

  void _onAuthChanged() {
    final auth = AuthService.instance;
    final uid = auth.user?.uid;
    if (uid == null) {
      _signInSyncedUid = null;
      _debounce?.cancel();
      return;
    }
    if (auth.username != null && _signInSyncedUid != uid) {
      _signInSyncedUid = uid;
      sync();
    }
  }

  /// Called after every local save; waits for 20 quiet seconds, then syncs.
  void _scheduleSync() {
    if (AuthService.instance.username == null) return;
    _debounce?.cancel();
    _debounce = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 20), sync);
  }

  /// Safe to call any time; does nothing when signed out or offline.
  Future<void> sync() async {
    final engine = _engine;
    final auth = AuthService.instance;
    final user = auth.user;
    if (engine == null ||
        user == null ||
        auth.username == null ||
        !auth.available ||
        _syncing) {
      return;
    }
    _syncing = true;
    try {
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      final linked = prefs.getString(_linkedUidKey);
      // Different account on the same device: adopt the server profile instead
      // of pushing the previous player's local progress into the new account.
      final mode = (linked != null && linked != user.uid) ? 'adopt' : 'merge';
      final sentXp = engine.careerXp;

      final data = await BackendClient.instance.post('/api/syncProfile', {
        'mode': mode,
        'profile': engine.toCloud(),
      });

      final profile = data['profile'];
      if (profile is! Map) return;

      if (engine.careerXp != sentXp) {
        _scheduleSync();
        return;
      }
      await engine.applyCloud(
        profile.map((k, v) => MapEntry(k.toString(), v)),
      );
      await prefs.setString(_linkedUidKey, user.uid);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Cloud save skipped: $e');
    } finally {
      _syncing = false;
    }
  }
}

  const MusicScene(this.asset);
