/// Shared audio mixer: one looped music channel and a rotating set of short
/// effect channels (effects can overlap without interrupting the music).
class GameAudio extends ChangeNotifier {
  GameAudio._();
  static final GameAudio instance = GameAudio._();

  static const String _musicKey = 'scamouflage_audio_music_v1';
  static const String _effectsKey = 'scamouflage_audio_effects_v1';
  static const String _volumeKey = 'scamouflage_audio_volume_v1';
  static const String _effectLevelKey = 'scamouflage_audio_effect_level_v2';

  bool musicEnabled = true;
  bool effectsEnabled = true;
  double volume = .60;
  double effectsLevel = .60; // Subtle by default; players can fine-tune.

  bool _activated = false;
  bool _paused = false;
  bool _closed = false;
  SharedPreferences? _preferences;
  AudioPlayer? _musicPlayer;
  final List<AudioPlayer> _effectPlayers = [];
  int _nextEffect = 0;
  MusicScene _desiredScene = MusicScene.menu;
  String? _playingAsset;
  int _requestId = 0;
  Future<void> _transitionQueue = Future<void>.value();

  MusicScene get scene => _desiredScene;
  bool get activated => _activated;

  Future<void> initialize() async {
    try {
      _preferences = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      musicEnabled = _preferences!.getBool(_musicKey) ?? true;
      effectsEnabled = _preferences!.getBool(_effectsKey) ?? true;
      volume = (_preferences!.getDouble(_volumeKey) ?? .60).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
      effectsLevel = (_preferences!.getDouble(_effectLevelKey) ?? .60)
          .clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Scamouflage audio preferences could not load: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Call synchronously inside an onPressed/onTap callback. Safari refuses
  /// autonomous playback before the user interacts with the page.
  void enter(MusicScene next) {
    if (_closed) return;
    final shouldRequest = !_activated || _desiredScene != next ||
        (musicEnabled && volume > 0 && !_paused && _playingAsset != next.asset);
    _activated = true;
    _desiredScene = next;
    if (shouldRequest) _requestMusic();
  }

  void setScene(MusicScene next) {
    if (_closed) return;
    if (_desiredScene == next) return;
    _desiredScene = next;
    if (_activated) _requestMusic();
  }

  void setMusicEnabled(bool enabled) {
    if (_closed) return;
    musicEnabled = enabled;
    if (enabled) _activated = true; // User toggled it on themselves.
    notifyListeners();
    unawaited(_save());
    _requestMusic();
  }

  void setEffectsEnabled(bool enabled) {
    if (_closed) return;
    effectsEnabled = enabled;
    notifyListeners();
    unawaited(_save());
    if (enabled) cue(GameCue.tap);
  }

  void setVolume(double next) {
    if (_closed) return;
    final previous = volume;
    volume = next.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
    notifyListeners();
    unawaited(_save());
    final player = _musicPlayer;
    if ((previous == 0) != (volume == 0)) {
      if (_activated) _requestMusic();
    } else if (player != null) {
      unawaited(_safeSetVolume(player, _musicVolume));
    }
  }

  /// Bright menus; gameplay intentionally sits lower in the audio mix.
  double get _musicVolume => (volume *
      ((_desiredScene == MusicScene.menu || _desiredScene == MusicScene.arcade)
          ? .34 : .25)).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();

  /// Frequent interactions are unobtrusive. Important success / victory
  /// jingles remain noticeable; the player still controls overall SFX level.
  double _effectVolume(GameCue cue) {
    final gain = switch (cue) {
      GameCue.correct || GameCue.victory => 1.10,
      GameCue.unlock => .86,
      GameCue.wrong || GameCue.defeat => .50,
      GameCue.hint => .42,
      GameCue.tap || GameCue.choose => .31,
      GameCue.swipe => .29,
      GameCue.flip || GameCue.inspect || GameCue.snap => .25,
    };
    return (volume * gain * effectsLevel).clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
  }

  void setEffectsLevel(double next) {
    if (_closed) return;
    effectsLevel = next.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble();
    notifyListeners();
    unawaited(_save());
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    try {
      final prefs = _preferences ?? await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      _preferences ??= prefs;
      await prefs.setBool(_musicKey, musicEnabled);
      await prefs.setBool(_effectsKey, effectsEnabled);
      await prefs.setDouble(_volumeKey, volume);
      await prefs.setDouble(_effectLevelKey, effectsLevel);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Unable to save audio preference: $e');
    }
  }

  void _requestMusic() {
    final id = ++_requestId;
    // Serialize changes: two rapid screens cannot race to restart a track.
    _transitionQueue = _transitionQueue.then((_) => _switchMusic(id))
        .catchError((Object error) {
      debugPrint('Audio transition skipped: $error');
    });
  }

  Future<void> _switchMusic(int id) async {
    if (_closed || id != _requestId) return;
    final player = _musicPlayer ??= AudioPlayer();
    final shouldPlay = _activated && !_paused && musicEnabled && volume > 0;
    if (!shouldPlay) {
      _playingAsset = null;
      try { await player.stop(); } catch (e) { debugPrint('$e'); }
      return;
    }
    final asset = _desiredScene.asset;
    if (_playingAsset == asset) {
      await _safeSetVolume(player, _musicVolume);
      return;
    }
    try {
      await player.stop();
      if (id != _requestId || _closed) return;
      await player.setReleaseMode(ReleaseMode.loop);
      await player.setVolume(_musicVolume);
      if (id != _requestId || _closed) return;
      await player.play(AssetSource('audio/music/$asset.mp3'));
      _playingAsset = asset;
    } catch (e) {
      _playingAsset = null;
      // Mobile Safari can block playback until its first user gesture.
      debugPrint('Music was not started (tap a game control to retry): $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _safeSetVolume(AudioPlayer player, double value) async {
    try { await player.setVolume(value); } catch (e) { debugPrint('$e'); }
  }

  /// Fire and forget on interaction. Uses multiple channels so rapid taps,
  /// flips and feedback do not cut each other off.
  void cue(GameCue sound) {
    if (_closed || !effectsEnabled || volume <= 0 || effectsLevel <= 0 || _paused) return;
    unawaited(_playCue(sound));
  }

  Future<void> _playCue(GameCue sound) async {
    try {
      while (_effectPlayers.length < 4) {
        _effectPlayers.add(AudioPlayer());
      }
      final player = _effectPlayers[_nextEffect++ % _effectPlayers.length];
      await player.stop();
      if (_closed || !effectsEnabled || _paused) return;
      await player.setReleaseMode(ReleaseMode.stop);
      await player.setVolume(_effectVolume(sound));
      await player.play(AssetSource('audio/sfx/${sound.asset}.mp3'));
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Sound effect skipped: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Stops background audio when the app/tab is hidden, without changing
  /// saved settings. Re-enters the requested scene on return.
  void appVisible(bool visible) {
    if (_closed || _paused == !visible) return;
    _paused = !visible;
    _requestMusic();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _closed = true;
    ++_requestId;
    final music = _musicPlayer;
    if (music != null) unawaited(music.dispose());
    for (final player in _effectPlayers) {
      unawaited(player.dispose());
    }
    super.dispose();
  }
}

/// A single reusable phone-friendly control panel for Career and Arcade.
class ScamouflageAudioControls extends StatelessWidget {
  const ScamouflageAudioControls({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnimatedBuilder(
    animation: GameAudio.instance,
    builder: (context, _) {
      final audio = GameAudio.instance;
      return Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        SwitchListTile.adaptive(
          contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          secondary: const Icon(Icons.music_note_rounded, color: mustard),
          title: Text('Background music', style: captionText(15)),
          subtitle: Text('Energetic menus, mellow during investigations',
            style: captionText(12, color: parchment)),
          value: audio.musicEnabled,
          activeTrackColor: mustard.withValues(alpha: .45),
          activeThumbColor: mustard,
          onChanged: audio.setMusicEnabled,
        ),
        SwitchListTile.adaptive(
          contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          secondary: const Icon(Icons.graphic_eq_rounded, color: blue),
          title: Text('Sound effects', style: captionText(15)),
          subtitle: Text('Soft clicks and swipes; clear success jingles',
            style: captionText(12, color: parchment)),
          value: audio.effectsEnabled,
          activeTrackColor: mustard.withValues(alpha: .45),
          activeThumbColor: mustard,
          onChanged: audio.setEffectsEnabled,
        ),
        Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(4, 5, 4, 0),
          child: Row(children: [
            const Icon(Icons.volume_up_rounded, color: mint),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(child: Text('Master volume', style: captionText(15))),
            Text('${(audio.volume * 100).round()}%',
              style: typewriter(13, color: mustard)),
          ])),
        Slider(
          value: audio.volume,
          min: 0, max: 1, divisions: 20,
          activeColor: mustard,
          inactiveColor: deskLight,
          onChanged: audio.setVolume,
          onChangeEnd: (_) => audio.cue(GameCue.choose),
        ),
        Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(4, 9, 4, 0),
          child: Row(children: [
            const Icon(Icons.tune_rounded, color: blue),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(child: Text('Sound effect intensity', style: captionText(15))),
            Text('${(audio.effectsLevel * 100).round()}%',
              style: typewriter(13, color: blue)),
          ])),
        Slider(
          value: audio.effectsLevel,
          min: 0, max: 1, divisions: 20,
          activeColor: blue,
          inactiveColor: deskLight,
          onChanged: audio.effectsEnabled ? audio.setEffectsLevel : null,
          onChangeEnd: (_) => audio.cue(GameCue.tap),
        ),
        TextButton.icon(
          onPressed: () {
            audio.enter(audio.scene);
            audio.cue(GameCue.correct);
          },
          icon: const Icon(Icons.play_circle_outline_rounded, color: blue),
          label: Text('TEST SOUND', style: captionText(13, color: paperWhite)),
        ),
      ]);
    },
  );
}

/// All output is fictional and offline. Rules create the ground truth BEFORE
/// text and visible evidence, so a payment, new account or typo is never a
/// standalone automatic reason to reject content.
class Pattern {
  final CaseKind kind;
  final Verdict verdict;
  final String topic, title, body, artifact, secondary;
  final String source, context, identity, lesson;
  const Pattern(this.kind,this.verdict,this.topic,this.title,this.body,
    this.artifact,this.secondary,this.source,this.context,this.identity,this.lesson);
}

Share para mabisto!','🌾 EMPTY LOT PHOTO','Wrong location','The photo was taken in a different barangay two weeks ago; the public inspection report shows the wall in {area} is complete.','Corruption is a real concern, but this picture does not show the place it names.','The uploader cites no document for the claim.','Valid anger needs accurate evidence. A photo of the wrong place is not proof.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.post,Verdict.reject,'Weather','LIVE: baha sa {area} NGAYON','Hanggang bubong na ang baha sa {area} ngayon!! Pa-share para makita ng lahat.','🌊 FLOOD PHOTO','Recycled disaster photo','The photo first appeared years ago during a different storm in another city.','The weather bureau lists no flood warning for {area} today.','The page re-posts dramatic disaster photos often.','Old disaster photos return every storm season and cause needless panic.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.post,Verdict.approve,'Weather','Weather bulletin summary','Ayon sa weather bureau, Signal No. 2 sa ilang lugar ngayong gabi.   Pattern(CaseKind.video,Verdict.reject,'Health','Doctor “testimonial” for a capsule','A well-known fictional physician seems to say a herbal capsule cures every lung illness. A trusted face selling a cure needs a check with the person’s own page.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.video,Verdict.approve,'AI satire','Labeled AI art reel','A reel of AI-generated jeepneys flying over a city, labeled “AI art” by the creator, with the prompt in the caption.','🎨 AI ART','Visible label','The creator labels the work and does not present it as news.','Nobody real is imitated and no event is claimed.','An art account with a clear bio.','AI art is fine when it is clearly presented as art.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.sms,Verdict.reject,'Jobs','Part-time “like and earn” job','Hi {who}! ₱{amount}/day lang sa pag-like ng videos. Magdeposito muna ng ₱1,000 para ma-activate ang account.','💼 ONLINE JOB','Unknown number','No employer lists this offer, and legitimate jobs do not charge you to start.','Easy money plus an upfront deposit is a classic task scam.','The number found you without any application.','Never pay to start a job. Report and block.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.sms,Verdict.approve,'Shopping','Login code you just requested','Your login code is 482 910. Never share it with anyone, not even staff. If you did not request this, ignore it.','🔐 LOGIN CODE','Saved wallet sender','You tapped “Log in” in the app a minute ago and the sender is the saved wallet name.','The code is not requested from you; it is only delivered.','The message warns you not to share the code.','Getting a code you asked for is normal. The danger is giving it to someone else.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.sms,Verdict.reject,'Entertainment','Concert tickets via DM','Last 2 VIP tickets, 50% off! Bayad muna sa e-wallet para ma-reserve, ipapadala ko ang QR.','🎟️ TICKET RESALE','Unknown seller','The official ticketing site says tickets are sold only on its own page.','Several buyers report paying and being blocked.','The seller account is new and reuses the same QR image.','Sold-out concerts attract scammers. Buy only from the official seller.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.profile,Verdict.reject,'Shopping','Support • verified ✔','A newly created page with a paid badge claims to be customer care and asks people to DM their OTP.','🎧 FAKE SUPPORT','About • Created 4 days ago','The company says support is handled only inside its app and its listed account.','The page asks for a one-time code, which real support never needs.','A badge can be bought. It does not prove identity.','A badge is not a directory. Check the company’s own list of accounts.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.profile,Verdict.approve,'Government','New handle announced by the old page','A new account for a barangay office says it replaced the old handle. The old account’s pinned post announces the move.','🏘️ NEW HANDLE','About • Created 5 days ago','The old official page and the city website both link to the new account.','Both accounts point to each other and nothing asks for money.','New does not mean fake when the old official page vouches for it.','A brand-new account can be the real one when trusted sources confirm it.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.report,Verdict.approve,'Entertainment','Fandom roast, not harassment','Reported: “Overrated ang comeback nila at bagsak ang choreo. Pero mahal ko pa rin ang fans, mababait sila.”','🎤 FANDOM OPINION','Complaint: “Hate speech!”','The comment criticizes a music video and nobody’s identity.','There are no threats, slurs or personal details.','An ordinary fan account sharing an opinion.','Disliking a comeback is not harassment, however loud the fandom reacts.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.report,Verdict.reject,'Campus','Doxxing a student','Reported: A comment posts a student’s home address and says “puntahan natin siya ngayong gabi.”','📍 DOXXING','Complaint: “Private info”','The comment shares a private address and urges people to go there.','This exposes a person to physical harm and is not criticism.','The poster organizes others to target one student.','Sharing private details to target someone is never allowed.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.email,Verdict.reject,'Campus','New bank details for tuition','The accounting office says its old bank account is closed and asks you to send tuition to a new account number. Reply-to is a different address.','🏦 PAYMENT CHANGE','Sender looks right, reply-to differs','The school’s portal lists only the original payment channels.','The reply-to address does not match the school domain.','The sender name copies the accounting office.','Changes to payment details by email are a classic scam. Confirm through the school directly.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.email,Verdict.approve,'Campus','Enrollment closes Friday','Reminder from the registrar: enrollment closes this Friday, 5PM. Finish on the school portal you already use. We never ask for your password by email.','⏰ DEADLINE','Registrar address','The school portal lists the same deadline and the link points to the school’s own domain.','It is urgent, but the deadline is real and it asks for no password.','The sender is the registrar’s official address.','Urgent does not mean fake. Check whether the deadline and domain are real.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.video,Verdict.approve,'Government','Short cut, full video linked','A 20-second clip of a council answer, cut from a livestream. The caption links the full video and the question is included.','✂️ SHORT CUT','Full video linked','The full recording says the same thing, with the question and answer intact.','Trimming did not change the meaning and the source is linked.','A local news page that links full recordings.','Short clips are normal. What matters is whether the cut changes the meaning.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.video,Verdict.reject,'Government','“Wala akong pakialam” clip','A clip cuts a mayor’s answer so he seems to say he does not care about flood victims. Look for the full recording.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.post,Verdict.reject,'Election','“Partial count” screenshot','Kap. Reyes lamang daw ng 90%! Dinaya ang kalaban. Screenshot ng bilangan ang nakalakip.','🗳️ RESULTS SCREENSHOT','Old practice run','The screenshot header shows a test run from an earlier election; the election office says no results are out.','Fake results spread fast because people are emotional.','The uploader cites no official release.','Only the election office’s own release counts as results.'),
  Pattern(CaseKind.post,Verdict.reject,'Campus','Dengue: ALL schools closed','BREAKING: lahat ng paaralan sa probinsya, sarado next week dahil sa dengue! Share sa GC!','🦟 DENGUE NOTICE','Wrong scope','The real notice covers one school for two days of cleaning.','Parents of other schools say classes continue.','The caption stretches one notice into a province-wide claim.','Misleading posts often start with something real and stretch its scope.'),];

class ShiftBundle {
  final int seed;
  final List<PlayCase> cases;
  final String modifier;
  final List<String> signatures;
  const ShiftBundle(this.seed, this.cases, this.modifier, this.signatures);
}

/// Truth-first, offline generator. The Pattern verdict is decided before
/// formatting the content and revealing evidence.
class CaseGenerator {
  final Random rng;
  CaseGenerator(int seed) : rng = Random(seed);

  static const names = ['Mika', 'Nica', 'Jules', 'Rafi', 'Sam', 'Kai', 'Ate Chai', 'Kuya Eli', 'Paolo', 'Iya', 'Tita Cora'];
  static const groups = ['Tambayan Updates', 'Barkada Bulletin', 'Campus Chika', 'Kanto Herald', 'Community Circle', 'Bayanihan Board'];
  static const queens = ['Miss Talahib', 'Queen Mariposa', 'Madam Sarsa', 'Diva Pinya', 'Misis Halo-Halo'];
  static const places = ['Malaya', 'Liwayway', 'Gumamela', 'Masigla', 'Maharlika'];
  static const dates = ['tomorrow', 'next Monday', 'this weekend', 'on Friday', 'later today'];
  static const modifiers = ['SOURCE SWAP', 'FANDOM FINALE', 'CAMPUS WEEK', 'COMMUNITY BULLETIN'];

  T choose<T>(List<T> list) => list[rng.nextInt(list.length)];
  int between(int low, int high) => low + rng.nextInt(high - low + 1);

  String _fill(String s, Map<String,String> fields) {
    var result = s;
    for (final e in fields.entries) {
      result = result.replaceAll('{${e.key}}', e.value);
    }
    return result;
  }

  String _neutralHeadline(Pattern p, Map<String,String> fields) {
    final location = fields['area']!;
    final queen = fields['queen']!;
    return switch (p.kind) {
      CaseKind.profile => p.topic == 'Drag Race'
          ? 'About $queen' : p.topic == 'Government'
          ? '$location Community Desk' : 'Campus community page',
      CaseKind.sms => choose(['New message received', 'A message for you', 'Inbox notification']),
      CaseKind.email => choose(['A message about your account', 'New mail received', 'An update for you']),
      CaseKind.report => choose(['User report submitted', 'Community report', 'Reported content']),
      CaseKind.video => choose(['A clip is circulating', 'Trending video', 'Recent upload']),
      CaseKind.post => switch(p.topic) {
        'Drag Race' => '$queen: tonight\'s episode',
        'Government' => 'News from $location?',
        'Campus' => 'Campus update in the group chat',
        'Basketball memorial' => 'Courtside discussion',
        'Weather' => 'Weather talk in the barangay',
        'Election' => 'Election chatter',
        'Health' => 'Health tips going around',
        _ => 'A community post is trending',
      },
    };
  }

  /// The displayed artifact never contains verdict-spoiling labels.
  String _artwork(Pattern p) => switch(p.topic) {
    'Drag Race' => '👑 EPISODE DISCUSSION',
    'Government' => '🏛️ COMMUNITY BULLETIN',
    'Campus' => '🎓 CAMPUS BOARD',
    'Basketball memorial' => '🏀 COURTSIDE',
    'Shopping' => '🛍️ SHOPPING',
    'Delivery' => '📦 DELIVERY',
    'AI satire' => '🎬 NEW UPLOAD',
    'Weather' => '⛈️ WEATHER WATCH',
    'Health' => '🩺 HEALTH TALK',
    'Jobs' => '💼 OPPORTUNITIES',
    'Entertainment' => '🎤 FANDOM FEED',
    'Election' => '🗳️ ELECTION WATCH',
    _ => '📲 RECENT ACTIVITY',
  };

  int _ageDays(Pattern p) {
    final source = p.secondary.toLowerCase();
    if (RegExp(r'2[- ]day').hasMatch(source)) return 2;
    if (RegExp(r'3[- ]year').hasMatch(source)) return 1095;
    if (RegExp(r'2[- ]year').hasMatch(source)) return 730;
    return choose([2, 18, 130, 530, 1200, 1900]);
  }

  PlayCase build(Pattern p, String id) {
    final fields = <String,String>{
      'who': choose(names),
      'queen': choose(queens),
      'area': choose(places),
      'day': choose(dates),
      'amount': choose(['75','250','399','1,200','2,500']),
      'link': '${choose(['parcel-check','cash-help','verify-account','claim-now','campus-awards'])}.example',
    };
    final age = _ageDays(p);
    final followers = age <= 5 ? between(30, 1800)
        : age < 200 ? between(120, 7000)
        : age < 1000 ? between(400, 22000)
        : between(1000, 85000);
    final author = p.kind == CaseKind.profile
        ? _fill(p.title, fields)
        : p.kind == CaseKind.sms ? choose(['Delivery Desk','Bookshop Service','Text Updates','Class GC','Unknown Number'])
        : p.kind == CaseKind.email ? choose(['Campus Mail','Event Services','Helpdesk','Community Mail'])
        : p.topic == 'Drag Race' ? choose(['Runway Tea PH','Untucked Tambayan','Barkada Feed'])
        : p.topic == 'Basketball memorial' ? choose(['Courtside Community','Hoops and Heart'])
        : choose(groups);
    final normalized = author.toLowerCase().replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9]'), '');
    final handle = '@${normalized.substring(0, min(16,normalized.length))}${choose(['ph','daily','hq',''])}';
    final sourceText = _fill(p.source, fields);
    // Free hints create questions, not answers. A lookup must be purchased
    // to reveal the decisive independent source.
    final clues = <Hotspot>[
      Hotspot('identity', 'ACCOUNT DETAILS',
        p.kind == CaseKind.profile ? 'This page is $age days old with $followers followers. Who confirms its identity?' :
        'Sender: $author. Display names can be copied; independently verify their identity.',
        Icons.account_circle_outlined,
        signal: EvidenceSignal.ambiguous, principle: 'identity_needs_corrob'),
      Hotspot('context', 'READ THE CONTEXT',
        p.kind == CaseKind.report ? 'The queue shows reported content, but the report count cannot establish a violation.' :
        p.kind == CaseKind.sms || p.kind == CaseKind.email ? 'The message claims: ${_fill(p.title,fields)}. Can you corroborate it through a known account?' :
        p.kind == CaseKind.post ? 'The claim concerns ${p.topic.toLowerCase()} and mentions ${fields['day']}. Compare the full original.' :
        p.kind == CaseKind.profile ? 'Look at the account description and history: ${_fill(p.title,fields)}.' :
        'What does the uncropped original clip actually show?',
        Icons.content_paste_search_outlined,
        signal: EvidenceSignal.neutral, principle: 'surface_cues_not_truth'),
      Hotspot('source','VERIFY INDEPENDENTLY', sourceText,
        Icons.fact_check_outlined,
        signal: p.verdict == Verdict.reject ? EvidenceSignal.suspicious : EvidenceSignal.supporting,
        strength: 3, verification: true, principle: 'independent_source'),
    ];
    final isNew = age <= 7;
    final verified = p.kind == CaseKind.profile
        ? (p.verdict == Verdict.approve && !isNew && rng.nextBool())
        : rng.nextInt(5) == 0;
    var message = _fill(p.body, fields);
    // The original audit found verdicts leaked through narration.
    // Remove explicit adjudication labels; keep user-visible suspicious *claims*.
    message = message.replaceAll('fake claim form', 'claim form')
      .replaceAll('fake payout', 'payout')
      .replaceAll('fake screenshot', 'shared screenshot')
      .replaceAll('A page impersonates', 'A page claims to represent')
      .replaceAll('A voiceover was added to the original interview without disclosure.', 'People disagree about what the clip says.')
      .replaceAll('fabricated', 'shared')
      .replaceAll('This old screenshot is proof.', 'A screenshot is being shared.')
      .replaceAll('No action required.', 'You can check your order if needed.');
    final secondary = p.kind == CaseKind.profile
        ? 'Joined ${age <= 7 ? '$age days' : age < 365 ? '${(age/30).round()} months' : '${(age/365).round()} years'} ago'
        : _fill(p.secondary, fields)
          .replaceAll('FAKE', 'RECENT')
          .replaceAll('FALSE', 'REPORTED')
          .replaceAll('Manipulated screenshot', 'Shared screenshot');
    final archetypeId = '${p.kind.index}:${patterns.indexOf(p)}';
    final variant = '$archetypeId|${fields['queen']}|${fields['area']}|${fields['day']}|${fields['amount']}';
    return PlayCase(
      id: id, archetypeId: archetypeId, kind: p.kind, correct: p.verdict,
      topic: p.topic, author: author, handle: handle,
      headline: _neutralHeadline(p,fields), body: message,
      artifact: _artwork(p), secondary: secondary,
      timestamp: choose(['now','8m','21m','1h']), accountAge: '$age days ago',
      ageDays: age, followers: followers, likes: between(3,12000),
      reports: between(0,100), verified: verified,
      clues: clues, rationale: _fill(p.lesson, fields), variant: variant,
      tier: p.kind == CaseKind.post ? 1 : 2,
    );
  }

  ShiftBundle generate(int shift, {
    required int seed,
    bool endless = false,
    int count = 7,
    Set<String> recent = const {},
    List<PlayCase> callbacks = const [],
    int safety = 80,
    int fairness = 80,
  }) {
    final allowedKinds = endless ? CaseKind.values : shifts[(shift-1).clamp(0,5).toInt()].kinds;
    final allowed = patterns.where((p)=>allowedKinds.contains(p.kind)).toList();
    final chosen = <PlayCase>[];
    final picked = <String>{};
    final signatures = <String>[];
    final modifier = choose(modifiers);
    // The source archetype (not cosmetic text) is deduplicated per shift.
    for (final callback in callbacks.take(1)) {
      chosen.add(callback); picked.add(callback.archetypeId);
      signatures.add(callback.archetypeId);
    }
    final firstGenerated = chosen.length;
    for (int i = chosen.length; i < count; i++) {
      final need = i == firstGenerated ? Verdict.approve
          : i == firstGenerated + 1 ? Verdict.reject : null;
      final options = allowed.where((p){
        final sig = '${p.kind.index}:${patterns.indexOf(p)}';
        return !picked.contains(sig) && (need == null || p.verdict == need);
      }).toList();
      if (options.isEmpty) break;
      // A low Safety meter brings more deception reports next shift;
      // low Fairness brings more legitimate posts and appeals to review.
      final biased = safety < 55
          ? options.where((p) => p.verdict == Verdict.reject).toList()
          : fairness < 55
              ? options.where((p) => p.verdict == Verdict.approve).toList()
              : <Pattern>[];
      final categoryPool = biased.isNotEmpty && rng.nextInt(100) < 50 ? biased : options;
      final themed = switch (modifier) {
        'FANDOM FINALE' => categoryPool.where((p) => p.topic == 'Drag Race').toList(),
        'CAMPUS WEEK' => categoryPool.where((p) => p.topic == 'Campus').toList(),
        'COMMUNITY BULLETIN' => categoryPool.where((p) => p.topic == 'Government').toList(),
        _ => <Pattern>[],
      };
      final weighted = themed.isNotEmpty && rng.nextInt(100) < 60 ? themed : categoryPool;
      final fresh = weighted.where((p) => !recent.contains('${p.kind.index}:${patterns.indexOf(p)}')).toList();
      final pool = fresh.isNotEmpty ? fresh : weighted;
      final p = choose(pool);
      final candidate = build(p, '$seed:$i');
      chosen.add(candidate);
      picked.add(candidate.archetypeId);
      signatures.add(candidate.archetypeId);
    }
    chosen.shuffle(rng);
    return ShiftBundle(seed,chosen,modifier,signatures);
  }
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\services\leaderboard_service.dart =====nimport 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';
import 'auth_service.dart';
import 'backend_client.dart';

enum BoardPeriod { weekly, allTime }

class LeaderboardEntry {
  final int rank;
  final String uid;
  final String username;
  final int score;
  const LeaderboardEntry({
    required this.rank,
    required this.uid,
    required this.username,
    required this.score,
  });
}

class MyStanding {
  final int rank;
  final int score;
  const MyStanding({required this.rank, required this.score});
}

/// Global rankings. One board per period x game mode x difficulty:
///
///   leaderboards/{boardId}/entries/{uid} -> { uid, username, score, mode,
///                                             difficulty, period, updatedAt }
///
/// READS come straight from Firestore (public, read-only for clients).
/// WRITES never happen on the device: a ranked run first asks the backend for a
/// one-time ticket ([startRun]); when the run ends, [finishRun] sends the
/// result and the backend validates it against the ticket before touching the
/// leaderboard. Firestore rules must deny all client writes to /leaderboards.
class LeaderboardService {
  LeaderboardService._();
  static final LeaderboardService instance = LeaderboardService._();

  static const int topN = 50;

  FirebaseFirestore get _db => FirebaseFirestore.instance;

  static String modeKey(ArcadeMode? mode) => mode?.name ?? 'mix';

  static String weekKey([DateTime? now]) {
    final ph = (now ?? DateTime.now()).toUtc().add(const Duration(hours: 8));
    final day = DateTime.utc(ph.year, ph.month, ph.day);
    final monday = day.subtract(Duration(days: day.weekday - 1));
    return '${monday.year}-${_two(monday.month)}-${_two(monday.day)}';
  }

  static String boardId(
      BoardPeriod period, String mode, ArcadeDifficulty difficulty) {
    return period == BoardPeriod.weekly
        ? 'week_${weekKey()}_${mode}_${difficulty.name}'
        : 'all_${mode}_${difficulty.name}';
  }

  static String _two(int n) => n.toString().padLeft(2, '0');

  CollectionReference<Map<String, dynamic>> _entries(String id) =>
      _db.collection('leaderboards').doc(id).collection('entries');

  /// Requests a one-time run ticket when a ranked arcade run starts.
  Future<String?> startRun({
    required ArcadeMode? mode,
    required ArcadeDifficulty difficulty,
  }) async {
    final auth = AuthService.instance;
    if (!auth.available || auth.username == null) return null;
    try {
      final data = await BackendClient.instance.post('/api/startRun', {
        'mode': modeKey(mode),
        'difficulty': difficulty.name,
      });
      return data['runId'] as String?;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Run will not be ranked (no ticket): $e');
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// Reports a finished run. Safe to fire-and-forget: it never throws, and it
  /// does nothing when the run has no ticket (signed out, offline, rate
  /// limited) or the score is zero. The backend decides whether the score is
  /// plausible; the client cannot choose the board or the player name.
  Future<void> finishRun({
    required Future<String?> ticket,
    required int score,
    required int rounds,
    required int correct,
    required int bestStreak,
  }) async {
    if (score <= 0) return;
    try {
      final runId = await ticket;
      if (runId == null) return;
      await BackendClient.instance.post('/api/submitRun', {
        'runId': runId,
        'score': score,
        'rounds': rounds,
        'correct': correct,
        'bestStreak': bestStreak,
      });
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Run was not ranked: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<List<LeaderboardEntry>> fetchTop(
      BoardPeriod period, String mode, ArcadeDifficulty difficulty) async {
    final snap = await _entries(boardId(period, mode, difficulty))
        .orderBy('score', descending: true)
        .limit(topN)
        .get();
    final result = <LeaderboardEntry>[];
    for (final doc in snap.docs) {
      final data = doc.data();
      result.add(LeaderboardEntry(
        rank: result.length + 1,
        uid: doc.id,
        username: (data['username'] ?? 'Player').toString(),
        score: (data['score'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,
      ));
    }
    return result;
  }

  Future<MyStanding?> fetchStanding(
      BoardPeriod period, String mode, ArcadeDifficulty difficulty) async {
    final user = AuthService.instance.user;
    if (user == null) return null;
    final col = _entries(boardId(period, mode, difficulty));
    final mine = await col.doc(user.uid).get();
    final score = (mine.data()?['score'] as num?)?.toInt();
    if (score == null) return null;
    final higher = await col.where('score', isGreaterThan: score).count().get();
    return MyStanding(rank: (higher.count ?? 0) + 1, score: score);
  }
}
n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\views\account_screens.dart =====nimport 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';
import '../services/auth_service.dart';
import '../services/leaderboard_service.dart';
import 'theme.dart';

/// Replaces the old "Online features – coming soon" preview card on the
/// Profile screen. Sign-in stays optional; the game works fully offline.
class AccountPanel extends StatefulWidget {
  const AccountPanel({super.key});
  @override
  State<AccountPanel> createState() => _AccountPanelState();
}

class _AccountPanelState extends State<AccountPanel> {
  final _auth = AuthService.instance;
  final _username = TextEditingController();
  final _email = TextEditingController();
  final _password = TextEditingController();
  bool _registerMode = true;
  bool _hidePassword = true;
  String? _error;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _username.dispose();
    _email.dispose();
    _password.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _perform(Future<void> Function() action) async {
    setState(() => _error = null);
    try {
      await action();
    } on AuthException catch (e) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = e.message);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = 'Something went wrong. Please try again.');
    }
  }

  Future<void> _submitForm() => _perform(() {
        final email = _email.text.trim();
        final password = _password.text;
        if (email.isEmpty || password.isEmpty) {
          throw const AuthException('Enter your email and password.');
        }
        if (_registerMode) {
          return _auth.register(
              email: email, password: password, username: _username.text);
        }
        return _auth.signIn(email: email, password: password);
      });

  void _openLeaderboard() => Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder: (_) => const LeaderboardScreen()));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(17),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: deskBlue.withValues(alpha: .80),
          border: Border.all(color: deskLight, width: 1.5),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        ),
        child: ListenableBuilder(
          listenable: _auth,
          builder: (context, _) => Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              _header(),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              _body(),
              if (_error != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Text(_error!, style: captionText(13, color: coral)),
              ],
              const Divider(color: deskLight, height: 25),
              _leaderboardRow(),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );

  Widget _header() {
    final signedIn = _auth.signedIn && _auth.username != null;
    return Row(children: [
      Icon(_auth.available ? Icons.cloud_outlined : Icons.cloud_off_outlined,
          color: parchment),
      const SizedBox(width: 9),
      Expanded(child: Text('ONLINE FEATURES', style: titleText(20, color: paperWhite))),
      InkTag(signedIn ? 'SIGNED IN' : 'OPTIONAL', color: signedIn ? mint : mustard),
    ]);
  }

  Widget _body() {
    if (!_auth.available) {
      return Text(
          'Online features are unavailable right now. The game still works '
          'offline and saves on this device.',
          style: captionText(13, color: parchment));
    }
    if (!_auth.signedIn) return _signedOutForm();
    if (_auth.profileError) {
      return Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: Text('Could not load your profile. Check your connection.',
            style: captionText(13, color: parchment))),
        TextButton(onPressed: _auth.reloadProfile, child: const Text('RETRY')),
      ]);
    }
    if (!_auth.profileLoaded) {
      return const Center(
          child: Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.all(8),
              child: SizedBox(width: 24, height: 24,
                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 3))));
    }
    if (_auth.needsUsername) return _chooseUsername();
    return _signedInCard();
  }

  Widget _signedOutForm() => Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
              _registerMode
                  ? 'Create an optional account to join the global rankings. '
                      'Offline play never needs one.'
                  : 'Welcome back, investigator.',
              style: captionText(13, color: parchment)),
          const SizedBox(height: 13),
          if (_registerMode) ...[
            TextField(
              controller: _username,
              textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.alternate_email_rounded),
                labelText: 'Username',
                helperText: '3–16 letters, numbers or _',
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                isDense: true,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 9),
          ],
          TextField(
            controller: _email,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
            textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.mail_outline_rounded),
              labelText: 'Email address',
              border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              isDense: true,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 9),
          TextField(
            controller: _password,
            obscureText: _hidePassword,
            onSubmitted: (_) => _auth.busy ? null : _submitForm(),
            decoration: InputDecoration(
              prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline_rounded),
              suffixIcon: IconButton(
                icon: Icon(_hidePassword
                    ? Icons.visibility_outlined
                    : Icons.visibility_off_outlined),
                onPressed: () => setState(() => _hidePassword = !_hidePassword),
              ),
              labelText: 'Password',
              border: const OutlineInputBorder(),
              isDense: true,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: _auth.busy ? null : _submitForm,
              icon: Icon(_registerMode ? Icons.person_add_alt_1_rounded : Icons.login_rounded),
              label: Text(_auth.busy
                  ? 'PLEASE WAIT…'
                  : _registerMode ? 'CREATE ACCOUNT' : 'SIGN IN'),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: _auth.busy ? null : () => _perform(_auth.signInWithGoogle),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.account_circle_outlined),
              label: const Text('CONTINUE WITH GOOGLE'),
            ),
          ),
          Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
            child: TextButton(
              onPressed: () => setState(() {
                _registerMode = !_registerMode;
                _error = null;
              }),
              child: Text(_registerMode
                  ? 'Already have an account? Sign in'
                  : 'New here? Create an account'),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      );

  Widget _chooseUsername() => Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text('Pick a username for the leaderboards. Other players will see it.',
              style: captionText(13, color: parchment)),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          TextField(
            controller: _username,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.alternate_email_rounded),
              labelText: 'Username',
              helperText: '3–16 letters, numbers or _',
              border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              isDense: true,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: _auth.busy
                  ? null
                  : () => _perform(() => _auth.chooseUsername(_username.text)),
              icon: const Icon(Icons.check_rounded),
              label: const Text('SAVE USERNAME'),
            ),
          ),
          TextButton(onPressed: _auth.signOut, child: const Text('Sign out')),
        ],
      );

  Widget _signedInCard() => Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(children: [
            const Icon(Icons.verified_user_outlined, color: mint),
            const SizedBox(width: 9),
            Expanded(
                child: Text('@${_auth.username}',
                    style: titleText(20, color: paperWhite),
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          Text('Your best arcade runs are ranked worldwide. Offline play still works.',
              style: captionText(13, color: parchment)),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            onPressed: _auth.busy ? null : _auth.signOut,
            icon: const Icon(Icons.logout_rounded),
            label: const Text('SIGN OUT'),
          ),
        ],
      );

  Widget _leaderboardRow() => Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(children: [
            const Icon(Icons.leaderboard_outlined, color: parchment),
            const SizedBox(width: 9),
            Expanded(
                child: Text('GLOBAL LEADERBOARDS',
                    style: captionText(16, color: paperWhite))),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 5),
          Text(
              _auth.username != null
                  ? 'Weekly and all-time Top 50 for every mode and difficulty.'
                  : 'Anyone can look. Sign in to get ranked.',
              style: captionText(12, color: parchment)),
          const SizedBox(height: 9),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: FilledButton.tonalIcon(
              onPressed: _auth.available ? _openLeaderboard : null,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.emoji_events_outlined),
              label: const Text('VIEW TOP 50'),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      );
}

// ===========================================================================

class _BoardData {
  final List<LeaderboardEntry> top;
  final MyStanding? mine;
  const _BoardData(this.top, this.mine);
}

/// Top 50 for one period x mode x difficulty, plus the player's own rank.
class LeaderboardScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const LeaderboardScreen({super.key});
  @override
  State<LeaderboardScreen> createState() => _LeaderboardScreenState();
}

class _LeaderboardScreenState extends State<LeaderboardScreen> {
  static final List<(String, String)> _modes = [
    ('mix', 'ENDLESS MIX'),
    for (final m in ArcadeMode.values) (m.name, m.title),
  ];

  BoardPeriod _period = BoardPeriod.weekly;
  String _mode = 'mix';
  ArcadeDifficulty _difficulty = ArcadeDifficulty.easy;
  late Future<_BoardData> _future;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _future = _load();
  }

  Future<_BoardData> _load() async {
    final service = LeaderboardService.instance;
    final top = await service.fetchTop(_period, _mode, _difficulty);
    MyStanding? mine;
    try {
      mine = await service.fetchStanding(_period, _mode, _difficulty);
    } catch (_) {
      // The list is still useful without the player's own rank.
    }
    return _BoardData(top, mine);
  }

  void _change(VoidCallback update) {
    setState(() {
      update();
      _future = _load();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: midnight,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: midnight,
          title: Text('LEADERBOARD', style: titleText(24)),
          actions: [
            IconButton(
              tooltip: 'Refresh',
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
              onPressed: () => _change(() {}),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        body: Column(children: [
          _filters(),
          Expanded(child: _results()),
          _yourRank(),
        ]),
      );

  Widget _filters() => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 4, 14, 8),
        child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: SegmentedButton<BoardPeriod>(
              segments: const [
                ButtonSegment<BoardPeriod>(
                    value: BoardPeriod.weekly, label: Text('THIS WEEK')),
                ButtonSegment<BoardPeriod>(
                    value: BoardPeriod.allTime, label: Text('ALL-TIME')),
              ],
              selected: {_period},
              onSelectionChanged: (s) => _change(() => _period = s.first),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: SegmentedButton<ArcadeDifficulty>(
              segments: [
                for (final d in ArcadeDifficulty.values)
                  ButtonSegment<ArcadeDifficulty>(value: d, label: Text(d.title)),
              ],
              selected: {_difficulty},
              onSelectionChanged: (s) => _change(() => _difficulty = s.first),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          SingleChildScrollView(
            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
            child: Row(children: [
              for (final (key, label) in _modes)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 6),
                  child: ChoiceChip(
                    label: Text(label,
                        style: typewriter(11,
                            color: _mode == key ? coal : parchment)),
                    selected: _mode == key,
                    selectedColor: mustard,
                    backgroundColor: deskBlue,
                    onSelected: (_) => _change(() => _mode = key),
                  ),
                ),
            ]),
          ),
        ]),
      );

  Widget _results() {
    if (!AuthService.instance.available) {
      return _message('Online rankings are unavailable right now.');
    }
    return FutureBuilder<_BoardData>(
      future: _future,
      builder: (context, snap) {
        if (snap.connectionState != ConnectionState.done) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }
        if (snap.hasError) {
          return _message(
              'Can’t load rankings. Internet is needed only for online rankings.',
              retry: true);
        }
        final top = snap.data!.top;
        if (top.isEmpty) {
          return _message(_period == BoardPeriod.weekly
              ? 'No scores yet this week. Be the first!'
              : 'No scores yet. Be the first!');
        }
        final me = AuthService.instance.user?.uid;
        return ListView.builder(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 0, 14, 8),
          itemCount: top.length,
          itemBuilder: (context, i) => _row(top[i], top[i].uid == me),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _message(String text, {bool retry = false}) => Center(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
            Text(text, textAlign: TextAlign.center, style: captionText(14)),
            if (retry) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              OutlinedButton(onPressed: () => _change(() {}), child: const Text('TRY AGAIN')),
            ],
          ]),
        ),
      );

  Widget _row(LeaderboardEntry e, bool mine) {
    const medals = ['🥇', '🥈', '🥉'];
    return Container(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: mine ? mustard.withValues(alpha: .18) : deskBlue,
        border: Border.all(color: mine ? mustard : deskLight, width: 1.5),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      ),
      child: Row(children: [
        SizedBox(
          width: 44,
          child: Text(e.rank <= 3 ? medals[e.rank - 1] : '#${e.rank}',
              style: e.rank <= 3 ? const TextStyle(fontSize: 22) : titleText(17)),
        ),
        Expanded(
          child: Text(mine ? '${e.username} (you)' : e.username,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: captionText(15, color: paperWhite, weight: FontWeight.w700)),
        ),
        Text('${e.score} XP', style: typewriter(13, color: mustard)),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _yourRank() => SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: Container(
          width: double.infinity,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
          decoration: const BoxDecoration(
              color: deskBlue, border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: deskLight))),
          child: ListenableBuilder(
            listenable: AuthService.instance,
            builder: (context, _) {
              if (AuthService.instance.username == null) {
                return Text('Sign in on the Profile tab to get ranked.',
                    style: captionText(13));
              }
              return FutureBuilder<_BoardData>(
                future: _future,
                builder: (context, snap) {
                  final mine = snap.data?.mine;
                  return Text(
                    mine == null
                        ? 'YOUR RANK: play a run on this board to get ranked.'
                        : 'YOUR RANK: #${mine.rank} • best ${mine.score} XP',
                    style: captionText(14, color: mustard, weight: FontWeight.w700),
                  );
                },
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      );
}
n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\views\arcade_modes_a.dart =====nimport 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../services/game_audio.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';
import 'arcade_widgets.dart';
import 'case_media.dart';
import 'theme.dart';

/// Shared state for modes that use five inspection chips with a limited
/// budget (Post Autopsy, Clip Check).
mixin _Inspect<T extends StatefulWidget> on State<T> {
  final List<String> opened = [];
  bool get hintOn;
  ArcadeCase get kase;
  ArcadeDifficulty get diff;
  int get left => diff.scanBudget - opened.length;

  void open(String id) {
    if (opened.contains(id)) return;
    if (left <= 0) return;
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.inspect);
    setState(() => opened.add(id));
  }

  bool get usedDecisive => kase.tools.any(
      (t) => t.relevance == Relevance.decisive && opened.contains(t.id));

  Widget chips({bool coachFirst = false}) => Wrap(spacing: 8, runSpacing: 8,
    children: [for (final t in kase.tools)
      ToolChip(emoji: t.emoji, label: t.label,
        opened: opened.contains(t.id), enabled: left > 0,
        glow: (hintOn && t.relevance == Relevance.decisive) ||
            (coachFirst && opened.isEmpty && t == kase.tools.first),
        onTap: () => open(t.id))]);

  Widget notebook() {
    if (opened.isEmpty) {
      return emptyNote('Nothing checked yet. Tap a check above.');
    }
    return Column(children: [for (final id in opened)
      FindingCard(
        emoji: kase.tools.firstWhere((t) => t.id == id).emoji,
        label: kase.tools.firstWhere((t) => t.id == id).label,
        text: kase.tools.firstWhere((t) => t.id == id).text)]);
  }

  Widget budgetTag() => Text(
    left > 0 ? '$left CHECK${left == 1 ? '' : 'S'} LEFT' : 'NO CHECKS LEFT',
    style: typewriter(12, color: left > 0 ? mustard : removeColor));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------ TRUST OR BUST
class TrustView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ArcadeCase c;
  final ArcadeDifficulty d;
  final bool tutorial, hint;
  final SubmitFn onSubmit;
  const TrustView({super.key, required this.c, required this.d,
    required this.tutorial, required this.hint, required this.onSubmit});
  @override
  State<TrustView> createState() => _TrustViewState();
}

class _TrustViewState extends State<TrustView> with _Inspect<TrustView> {
  @override bool get hintOn => widget.hint;
  @override ArcadeCase get kase => widget.c;
  @override ArcadeDifficulty get diff => widget.d;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.c;
    final gate = widget.tutorial && opened.isEmpty;
    return ModeFrame(
      body: [
        if (widget.tutorial) CoachBanner(opened.isEmpty
            ? 'Tap the glowing check to investigate.'
            : 'Now decide: KEEP or REMOVE.'),
        Paper(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(children: [
              CircleAvatar(radius: 19, backgroundColor: choicePink,
                child: Text(c.author.isEmpty ? '?' : c.author[0],
                  style: titleText(18, color: coal))),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(c.author, style: captionText(15, color: coal,
                    weight: FontWeight.w800)),
                  Text(c.handle, style: captionText(12, color: mutedInk)),
                ])),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 11),
            onPaper(c.text, size: 15.5),
            const SizedBox(height: 11),
            CaseMedia(topic: c.imageTopic, caption: c.imageCaption, height: 138),
            const SizedBox(height: 9),
            Text(c.reactions, style: captionText(12, color: mutedInk)),
          ])),
        sectionLabel('CHECKS', trailing: budgetTag()),
        chips(coachFirst: widget.tutorial),
        sectionLabel('NOTEBOOK'),
        notebook(),
      ],
      actions: Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('KEEP', color: keepColor,
          icon: Icons.check_circle_rounded, glow: widget.tutorial && !gate,
          onTap: gate ? null
              : () => widget.onSubmit([0], usedDecisive))),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('REMOVE', color: removeColor,
          icon: Icons.block_rounded, glow: widget.tutorial && !gate,
          onTap: gate ? null
              : () => widget.onSubmit([1], usedDecisive))),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------- IMPOSTOR HUNT
class ImpostorView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ArcadeCase c;
  final ArcadeDifficulty d;
  final bool tutorial, hint;
  final SubmitFn onSubmit;
  const ImpostorView({super.key, required this.c, required this.d,
    required this.tutorial, required this.hint, required this.onSubmit});
  @override
  State<ImpostorView> createState() => _ImpostorViewState();
}

class _ImpostorViewState extends State<ImpostorView> {
  bool a = false, b = false;

  Future<void> _open(ProfileView p, String tag, bool left) async {
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.inspect);
    setState(() { if (left) { a = true; } else { b = true; } });
    await showDetailSheet(context, title: 'PROFILE $tag', children: [
      Text(p.name, style: captionText(18, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800)),
      Text(p.handle, style: captionText(14, color: mutedInk)),
      const SizedBox(height: 10),
      onPaper(p.bio),
      const SizedBox(height: 10),
      _line('Link in bio', p.link),
      _line('Account age', p.ageLabel),
      _line('Followers', '${p.followers}'),
      _line('Badge', p.badge ? 'Has a verified-style badge' : 'No badge'),
      if (p.label != null) _line('Self-label', p.label!),
      const SizedBox(height: 6),
      Text('RECENT POSTS', style: typewriter(11, color: coal)),
      const SizedBox(height: 6),
      for (final r in p.recent)
        Container(width: double.infinity, margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 7),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: chipFill,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
            border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.4)),
          child: onPaper(r, size: 14)),
    ]);
  }

  Widget _line(String k, String v) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
    child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      SizedBox(width: 104, child: Text(k,
        style: captionText(13, color: mutedInk, weight: FontWeight.w700))),
      Expanded(child: Text(v, style: captionText(14, color: coal,
        weight: FontWeight.w700))),
    ]));

  Widget _card(ProfileView p, String tag, bool opened, bool left, Color tint) =>
    Expanded(child: Pulse(active: widget.tutorial && !opened, child: InkWell(
      onTap: () => _open(p, tag, left),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(17),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(11),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: paperWhite,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(17),
          border: Border.all(color: tint, width: 3)),
        child: Column(children: [
          Container(padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 3),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: tint,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
            child: Text(tag, style: typewriter(12, color: stroke))),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          CircleAvatar(radius: 23, backgroundColor: tint.withValues(alpha: .5),
            child: Text(p.name.isEmpty ? '?' : p.name[0],
              style: titleText(22, color: coal))),
          const SizedBox(height: 7),
          Text(p.name, textAlign: TextAlign.center, maxLines: 2,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: captionText(14, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800)),
          Text(p.handle, textAlign: TextAlign.center, maxLines: 1,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: captionText(12, color: mutedInk)),
          const SizedBox(height: 5),
          Text('${p.followers} followers', style: captionText(12, color: mutedInk)),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Container(width: double.infinity,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: opened ? chipOpenFill : chipFill,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.5)),
            child: Text(opened ? 'OPENED ✓' : 'OPEN PROFILE',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: typewriter(11, color: coal))),
        ]),
      ),
    )));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.c;
    final both = a && b;
    final gate = widget.tutorial && !both;
    return ModeFrame(
      body: [
        if (widget.tutorial) CoachBanner(both
            ? 'Now check the directory. Who is copying?'
            : 'Tap both profiles to read them.'),
        Paper(fill: const Color(0xFFE9F1FB), child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text('📖  OFFICIAL DIRECTORY', style: typewriter(12, color: coal)),
            const SizedBox(height: 7),
            for (final line in c.directory) onPaper(line, size: 14.5),
          ])),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        sectionLabel('TWO ACCOUNTS'),
        Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          _card(c.leftProfile!, 'A', a, true, choiceOrange),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          _card(c.rightProfile!, 'B', b, false, choiceBlue),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Text(c.subtext,
          style: captionText(13, color: paperWhite)),
      ],
      actions: Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('FLAG A', color: removeColor,
          icon: Icons.flag_rounded,
          onTap: gate ? null : () => widget.onSubmit([0], a && b))),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('FLAG B', color: removeColor,
          icon: Icons.flag_rounded,
          onTap: gate ? null : () => widget.onSubmit([1], a && b))),
        const SizedBox(width: 8),
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('BOTH OK', color: keepColor,
          icon: Icons.check_rounded,
          onTap: gate ? null : () => widget.onSubmit([2], a && b))),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------- SCAM SLAM
class ChatView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ArcadeCase c;
  final ArcadeDifficulty d;
  final bool tutorial, hint;
  final SubmitFn onSubmit;
  const ChatView({super.key, required this.c, required this.d,
    required this.tutorial, required this.hint, required this.onSubmit});
  @override
  State<ChatView> createState() => _ChatViewState();
}

class _ChatViewState extends State<ChatView> {
  bool phoneOpen = false, linkSeen = false;
  int shown = 0;
  int? picked;
  Timer? _t;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _t = Timer.periodic(const Duration(milliseconds: 650), (t) {
      if (!mounted) { t.cancel(); return; }
      if (shown >= widget.c.chatIn.length) { t.cancel(); return; }
      setState(() => shown++);
    });
    shown = 1;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() { _t?.cancel(); super.dispose(); }

  Widget _bubble(String text, {bool mine = false}) => Align(
    alignment: mine ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
    child: Container(
      constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 310),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: mine ? choiceBlue : const Color(0xFFEFEFF4),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.only(
          topLeft: const Radius.circular(16), topRight: const Radius.circular(16),
          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(mine ? 16 : 4),
          bottomRight: Radius.circular(mine ? 4 : 16)),
        border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.5)),
      child: onPaper(text, size: 15),
    ));

  void _pick(int i) {
    if (picked != null) return;
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
    setState(() => picked = i);
    Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 650), () {
      if (!mounted) return;
      widget.onSubmit([i], phoneOpen || linkSeen);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.c;
    const colors = [choiceOrange, choicePink, choiceBlue];
    return ModeFrame(
      body: [
        if (widget.tutorial) CoachBanner(phoneOpen
            ? 'Does the message match your phone? Pick a reply.'
            : 'Tap YOUR PHONE to check what you really did.'),
        Pulse(active: widget.tutorial && !phoneOpen || widget.hint && !phoneOpen,
          child: InkWell(
            onTap: () {
              GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.inspect);
              setState(() => phoneOpen = !phoneOpen);
            },
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
            child: Container(
              width: double.infinity, padding: const EdgeInsets.all(13),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(color: const Color(0xFFFFF4CF),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 2)),
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(children: [
                    const Text('📱', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18)),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(child: Text('YOUR PHONE: RECENT ACTIVITY',
                      style: typewriter(12, color: coal))),
                    Icon(phoneOpen ? Icons.expand_less_rounded
                        : Icons.expand_more_rounded, color: coal),
                  ]),
                  if (phoneOpen) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    for (final a in c.activity)
                      Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 5),
                        child: onPaper('• $a', size: 14.5)),
                  ] else Text('Tap to see what you did lately',
                    style: captionText(13, color: mutedInk)),
                ]),
            ))),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Paper(fill: Colors.white, child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Row(children: [
              const CircleAvatar(radius: 18, backgroundColor: choiceBlue,
                child: Icon(Icons.person_rounded, color: coal)),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(c.author, style: captionText(15, color: coal,
                    weight: FontWeight.w800)),
                  Text(c.handle, style: captionText(12.5, color: mutedInk)),
                ])),
            ]),
            if (c.subtext.isNotEmpty) Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6, bottom: 4),
              child: Text(c.subtext, style: captionText(12.5, color: mutedInk))),
            const Divider(height: 18),
            for (var i = 0; i < c.chatIn.length && i < shown; i++)
              _bubble(c.chatIn[i]),
            if (shown < c.chatIn.length)
              Text('typing…', style: captionText(12.5, color: mutedInk)),
            if (c.linkDomain.isNotEmpty && shown >= c.chatIn.length)
              Align(alignment: Alignment.centerLeft, child: InkWell(
                onTap: () {
                  setState(() => linkSeen = true);
                  showDetailSheet(context, title: 'LINK PREVIEW', children: [
                    onPaper('This link goes to:'),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    Text(c.linkDomain, style: typewriter(15, color: coal)),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                    onPaper('Only a preview. Nothing was opened.', size: 13.5),
                  ]);
                },
                child: Container(padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 11, vertical: 9),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(color: chipFill,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
                    border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.5)),
                  child: Text('🔗 PREVIEW LINK${linkSeen ? ' ✓' : ''}',
                    style: typewriter(11, color: coal)))) ),
            if (picked != null)
              _bubble(c.choices[picked!].text, mine: true),
          ])),
      ],
      actions: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        for (var i = 0; i < c.choices.length; i++)
          Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 7),
            child: SizedBox(width: double.infinity, child: ArcadeButton(
              c.choices[i].text, color: colors[i % 3],
              icon: Icons.send_rounded,
              onTap: picked == null && shown >= c.chatIn.length
                  ? () => _pick(i) : null))),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------- CLIP CHECK
class ClipView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ArcadeCase c;
  final ArcadeDifficulty d;
  final bool tutorial, hint;
  final SubmitFn onSubmit;
  const ClipView({super.key, required this.c, required this.d,
    required this.tutorial, required this.hint, required this.onSubmit});
  @override
  State<ClipView> createState() => _ClipViewState();
}

class _ClipViewState extends State<ClipView> with _Inspect<ClipView> {
  @override bool get hintOn => widget.hint;
  @override ArcadeCase get kase => widget.c;
  @override ArcadeDifficulty get diff => widget.d;
  int beat = 0;

  Widget _line(String label, String text, Color tint, {bool dim = false}) =>
    Container(width: double.infinity, margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: dim ? deskBlue : paperWhite,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
        border: Border.all(color: tint, width: 2.5)),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Text(label, style: typewriter(11, color: dim ? paperWhite : coal)),
        const SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(text, style: captionText(15, color: dim ? paperWhite : coal,
          weight: FontWeight.w700)),
      ]));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.c;
    final b = c.beats[beat];
    return ModeFrame(
      body: [
        Paper(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(c.author, style: captionText(13, color: mutedInk)),
            const SizedBox(height: 5),
            onPaper(c.text, size: 15.5, weight: FontWeight.w800),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            CaseMedia(topic: c.imageTopic, caption: b.scene, height: 120, badge: 'VIDEO • ${b.second}s'),
          ])),
        sectionLabel('TIMELINE • ${b.second}s',
          trailing: Text('${beat + 1}/${c.beats.length}',
            style: typewriter(12, color: paperWhite))),
        Row(children: [
          for (var i = 0; i < c.beats.length; i++) Expanded(child: InkWell(
            onTap: () {
              GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
              setState(() => beat = i);
            },
            child: Container(height: 34, margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 5),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: i == beat ? mustard : chipFill,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
                border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.6)),
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              child: Text('${c.beats[i].second}s',
                style: typewriter(11, color: coal))))),
        ]),
        Slider(value: beat.toDouble(), min: 0,
          max: (c.beats.length - 1).toDouble(), divisions: c.beats.length - 1,
          activeColor: mustard, inactiveColor: deskLight,
          onChanged: (v) => setState(() => beat = v.round())),
        _line('VIRAL CUT', b.inViral ? b.viralLine : '— this moment is not in the viral cut —',
          choiceOrange, dim: !b.inViral),
        _line('FULL VIDEO', b.fullLine, choiceBlue),
        sectionLabel('CHECKS', trailing: budgetTag()),
        chips(),
        sectionLabel('NOTEBOOK'),
        notebook(),
      ],
      actions: Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('KEEP', color: keepColor,
          icon: Icons.check_circle_rounded,
          onTap: () => widget.onSubmit([0], usedDecisive))),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('REMOVE', color: removeColor,
          icon: Icons.block_rounded,
          onTap: () => widget.onSubmit([1], usedDecisive))),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\views\arcade_modes_b.dart =====nimport 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../models/arcade_models.dart';
