// ------------------------------------------------------------- RUMOR RADAR
class RumorView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ArcadeCase c;
  final ArcadeDifficulty d;
  final bool tutorial, hint;
  final SubmitFn onSubmit;
  const RumorView({super.key, required this.c, required this.d,
    required this.tutorial, required this.hint, required this.onSubmit});
  @override
  State<RumorView> createState() => _RumorViewState();
}

class _RumorViewState extends State<RumorView> {
  late List<int> order = [for (var i = 0; i < widget.c.cards.length; i++) i];
  int? picked;
  final Set<int> words = {};

  Widget _prompt() => Paper(fill: const Color(0xFFE9F1FB), child: Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      Text('🧩  ${widget.c.title.toUpperCase()}', style: typewriter(12, color: coal)),
      const SizedBox(height: 7),
      onPaper(widget.c.text, size: 15.5, weight: FontWeight.w800),
      if (widget.c.subtext.isNotEmpty) ...[
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        onPaper(widget.c.subtext, size: 14),
      ],
    ]));

  Widget _cardBody(RumorCard r) => Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      Text(r.title, style: captionText(13, color: mutedInk,
        weight: FontWeight.w800)),
      const SizedBox(height: 4),
      onPaper(r.body, size: 14.5),
      if (r.meta.isNotEmpty) ...[
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        Text(r.meta, style: captionText(12.5, color: const Color(0xFF3C5F8A),
          weight: FontWeight.w800)),
      ],
    ]);

  void _move(int pos, int delta) {
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
    setState(() {
    final item = order.removeAt(pos);
    order.insert(pos + delta, item);
    });
  }

  Widget _arrow(IconData icon, VoidCallback? onTap) => InkWell(
    onTap: onTap, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
    child: Container(width: 38, height: 34, margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: onTap == null ? deskLight.withValues(alpha: .25) : chipFill,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
        border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.4)),
      child: Icon(icon, color: coal)));

  Widget _orderPuzzle() => Column(children: [
    for (var pos = 0; pos < order.length; pos++)
      Container(key: ValueKey('r${order[pos]}'),
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 9),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: paperWhite,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
          border: Border.all(color: choiceBlue, width: 2.5)),
        child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Container(width: 30, height: 30, alignment: Alignment.center,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: choiceBlue,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9)),
            child: Text('${pos + 1}', style: typewriter(13, color: stroke))),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: _cardBody(widget.c.cards[order[pos]])),
          Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
            _arrow(Icons.keyboard_arrow_up_rounded,
              pos > 0 ? () => _move(pos, -1) : null),
            _arrow(Icons.keyboard_arrow_down_rounded,
              pos < order.length - 1 ? () => _move(pos, 1) : null),
          ]),
        ])),
  ]);

  Widget _pickCards() => Column(children: [
    for (var i = 0; i < widget.c.cards.length; i++)
      Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 9), child: InkWell(
        onTap: () {
          GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
          setState(() => picked = i);
        },
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
        child: Container(width: double.infinity, padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: picked == i ? const Color(0xFFFFF1DA) : paperWhite,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
            border: Border.all(
              color: picked == i ? choiceOrange : stroke,
              width: picked == i ? 3 : 1.8)),
          child: _cardBody(widget.c.cards[i])))),
  ]);

  Widget _wordPuzzle() => Paper(child: Wrap(spacing: 6, runSpacing: 8, children: [
    for (var i = 0; i < widget.c.tokens.length; i++)
      InkWell(onTap: () {
          GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
          setState(() { if (!words.add(i)) words.remove(i); });
        },
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 9),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: words.contains(i) ? choicePink : chipFill,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.6)),
          child: Text(widget.c.tokens[i],
            style: captionText(15, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w700)))),
  ]));

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.c;
    final task = c.rumorTask!;
    final ready = switch (task) {
      RumorTask.order => true,
      RumorTask.wordEdit => words.isNotEmpty,
      _ => picked != null,
    };
    final label = switch (task) {
      RumorTask.order => 'LOCK IN THIS ORDER',
      RumorTask.wordEdit => words.isEmpty ? 'TAP CHANGED WORDS'
          : 'LOCK IN ${words.length} WORD${words.length == 1 ? '' : 'S'}',
      _ => picked == null ? 'TAP AN ITEM' : 'LOCK IN ITEM ${picked! + 1}',
    };
    return ModeFrame(
      body: [
        _prompt(),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        if (task == RumorTask.order) _orderPuzzle()
        else if (task == RumorTask.wordEdit) _wordPuzzle()
        else _pickCards(),
      ],
      actions: ArcadeButton(label, color: choiceOrange,
        icon: Icons.lock_rounded, onTap: !ready ? null : () {
          final answer = switch (task) {
            RumorTask.order => order,
            RumorTask.wordEdit => words.toList(),
            _ => [picked!],
          };
          widget.onSubmit(answer, true);
        }),
    );
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------ INBOX SPRINT
class InboxView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ArcadeCase c;
  final ArcadeDifficulty d;
  final bool tutorial, hint;
  final SubmitFn onSubmit;
  const InboxView({super.key, required this.c, required this.d,
    required this.tutorial, required this.hint, required this.onSubmit});
  @override
  State<InboxView> createState() => _InboxViewState();
}

class _InboxViewState extends State<InboxView>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  int idx = 0;
  bool flipped = false, flippedEver = false, flying = false;
  final List<int> decisions = [];

  late final AnimationController _flipController;
  late final AnimationController _slideController;
  AnimationController? _timer;

  Offset _dragOffset = Offset.zero;
  Offset _slideFrom = Offset.zero;
  Offset _slideTo = Offset.zero;
  Curve _slideCurve = Curves.easeOutCubic;

  int? get seconds => widget.tutorial ? null : widget.d.emailSeconds;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _flipController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 430));
    _slideController = AnimationController(
      vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 320))
      ..addListener(_onSlideTick);
    _startTimer();
  }

  void _onSlideTick() {
    if (!mounted) return;
    final t = _slideCurve.transform(_slideController.value);
    setState(() {
      _dragOffset = Offset.lerp(_slideFrom, _slideTo, t)!;
    });
  }

  void _startTimer() {
    _timer?.dispose();
    _timer = null;
    final s = seconds;
    if (s == null) return;
    final timer = AnimationController(vsync: this,
        duration: Duration(seconds: s));
    timer.addStatusListener((status) {
      if (status == AnimationStatus.completed && !flying) _decide(2);
    });
    _timer = timer;
    timer.forward();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.dispose();
    _flipController.dispose();
    _slideController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _flip() {
    if (flying) return;
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.flip);
    setState(() {
      flipped = !flipped;
      flippedEver = true;
    });
    if (flipped) {
      _flipController.forward();
    } else {
      _flipController.reverse();
    }
  }

  void _slideToOffset(Offset destination, {
    required Duration duration,
    required Curve curve,
    required VoidCallback onFinished,
  }) {
    _slideController.stop();
    _slideFrom = _dragOffset;
    _slideTo = destination;
    _slideCurve = curve;
    _slideController.duration = duration;
    _slideController.forward(from: 0).then((_) {
      if (mounted) onFinished();
    });
  }

  void _release(DragEndDetails details) {
    if (flying) return;
    final width = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width;
    final answer = inboxSwipeVerdict(
      displacement: _dragOffset.dx,
      velocity: details.primaryVelocity ?? details.velocity.pixelsPerSecond.dx,
      viewportWidth: width,
    );
    if (answer != null) {
      _decide(answer);
    } else {
      GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.snap);
      _slideToOffset(Offset.zero,
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 290),
        curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
        onFinished: () {},
      );
    }
  }

  void _decide(int value) {
    if (flying || !mounted) return;
    GameAudio.instance.cue(value == 2 ? GameCue.wrong : GameCue.swipe);
    _timer?.stop();
    setState(() => flying = true);
    final size = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
    // Fully exits the viewport, regardless of the phone's screen width.
    final end = switch (value) {
      0 => Offset(-size.width - 230, -22),
      1 => Offset(size.width + 230, -22),
      _ => Offset(0, -size.height - 150), // Timer expired.
    };
    _slideToOffset(end,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 325),
      curve: Curves.easeInCubic,
      onFinished: () => _finishEmail(value),
    );
  }

  void _finishEmail(int decision) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    decisions.add(decision);
    if (decisions.length >= widget.c.emails.length) {
      _timer?.dispose();
      _timer = null;
      widget.onSubmit(decisions, flippedEver);
      return;
    }
    _flipController.value = 0;
    setState(() {
      idx++;
      flipped = false;
      flying = false;
      _dragOffset = Offset.zero;
    });
    _startTimer();
  }

  Widget _front(EmailItem e) => Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      Text(e.fromName,
        style: captionText(15, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800)),
      const SizedBox(height: 3),
      Text(e.subject,
        style: captionText(16, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800)),
      const SizedBox(height: 10),
      Text(e.body, style: captionText(14.5, color: coal)),
      if (e.attachment.isNotEmpty) ...[
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Text('📎 ${e.attachment}', style: typewriter(12, color: coal)),
      ],
      if (e.linkLabel.isNotEmpty) ...[
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 9),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: choiceBlue,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.6)),
          child: Text(e.linkLabel,
            style: captionText(13.5, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800))),
      ],
    ]);

  Widget _back(EmailItem e) {
    Widget row(String k, String v) => Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 9),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Text(k, style: typewriter(11, color: mutedInk)),
        Text(v, style: captionText(14.5, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800)),
      ]));
    return Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      Text('DETAILS', style: typewriter(12, color: coal)),
      const SizedBox(height: 10),
      row('FROM', '${e.fromName} <${e.fromAddress}>'),
      row('REPLIES GO TO', e.replyTo.isEmpty ? 'Same as sender' : e.replyTo),
      row('LINK REALLY GOES TO',
        e.linkTarget.isEmpty ? 'No link' : e.linkTarget),
      row('ATTACHMENT', e.attachment.isEmpty ? 'None' : e.attachment),
    ]);
  }

  Widget _face(EmailItem e, {
    required bool back,
    required double viewportWidth,
  }) {
    final base = back ? const Color(0xFFE9F1FB) : paperWhite;
    final tint = inboxSwipeTint(
      base: base,
      displacement: _dragOffset.dx,
      viewportWidth: viewportWidth);
    final outline = inboxSwipeBorder(
      base: stroke,
      displacement: _dragOffset.dx,
      viewportWidth: viewportWidth);
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 270),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: tint,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        border: Border.all(color: outline, width: 2.2),
        boxShadow: const [BoxShadow(color: stroke, offset: Offset(0, 4))]),
      child: back ? _back(e) : _front(e),
    );
  }

  /// Both faces participate in layout so the card stays the same height
  /// throughout the 3D turn, including with long email addresses on phones.
  Widget _sizedFace(EmailItem e, bool back, double viewportWidth) => Stack(
    children: [
      IgnorePointer(child: ExcludeSemantics(child: Opacity(opacity: 0,
        child: _face(e, back: false, viewportWidth: viewportWidth)))),
      IgnorePointer(child: ExcludeSemantics(child: Opacity(opacity: 0,
        child: _face(e, back: true, viewportWidth: viewportWidth)))),
      Positioned.fill(child: KeyedSubtree(
        key: ValueKey(back ? 'inbox-visible-back' : 'inbox-visible-front'),
        child: _face(e, back: back, viewportWidth: viewportWidth))),
    ],
  );

  Widget _animatedCard(EmailItem e, double viewportWidth) =>
      AnimatedBuilder(animation: _flipController, builder: (context, _) {
    // A half-turn around the Y axis, with the reverse face un-mirrored.
    // This looks like a physical flashcard rather than a content swap.
    final turn = Curves.easeInOutCubic.transform(_flipController.value);
    final back = turn >= .5;
    return Transform(
      alignment: Alignment.center,
      transform: Matrix4.identity()
        ..setEntry(3, 2, .0011)
        ..rotateY(math.pi * turn),
      child: Transform(
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        transform: Matrix4.rotationY(back ? math.pi : 0),
        child: _sizedFace(e, back, viewportWidth),
      ),
    );
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.c;
    final e = c.emails[idx];
    final width = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width;
    final tilt = (_dragOffset.dx / (width * .75)).clamp(-1.0, 1.0) * .14;
    return ModeFrame(
      body: [
        if (widget.tutorial) CoachBanner(!flippedEver
            ? 'Tap the card to see who really sent it.'
            : 'Swipe left = SPAM, right = KEEP. Or use the buttons.'),
        Row(children: [
          for (var i = 0; i < c.emails.length; i++) Expanded(child: Container(
            height: 7, margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 5),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: i < idx ? mustard : i == idx ? paperWhite : deskLight,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4)))),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        Row(children: [
          Text('EMAIL ${idx + 1} OF ${c.emails.length}',
            style: typewriter(12, color: blue)),
          const Spacer(),
          if (_timer != null) SizedBox(width: 120, child: AnimatedBuilder(
            animation: _timer!, builder: (context, _) =>
              LinearProgressIndicator(value: 1 - _timer!.value, minHeight: 7,
                color: _timer!.value > .7 ? removeColor : mustard,
                backgroundColor: deskLight,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4)))),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Pulse(active: widget.tutorial && !flippedEver, radius: 20,
          child: GestureDetector(
            key: const ValueKey('inbox-swipe-card'),
            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
            onTap: _flip,
            onHorizontalDragStart: flying ? null : (_) {
              // Interrupt a snap-back and let the card follow the new gesture.
              _slideController.stop();
            },
            onHorizontalDragUpdate: flying ? null : (details) {
              final next = (_dragOffset.dx + details.delta.dx)
                  .clamp(-width * 1.2, width * 1.2).toDouble();
              setState(() => _dragOffset = Offset(next, 0));
            },
            onHorizontalDragEnd: flying ? null : _release,
            onHorizontalDragCancel: flying ? null : () {
              _slideToOffset(Offset.zero,
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 290),
                curve: Curves.easeOutCubic, onFinished: () {});
            },
            child: Transform.translate(
              offset: _dragOffset,
              child: Transform.rotate(angle: tilt,
                child: RepaintBoundary(child: _animatedCard(e, width))),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Center(child: Text(
          flipped ? 'Tap to flip back • Swipe to sort' :
              'Tap to inspect • Swipe left or right to sort',
          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
          style: captionText(13, color: paperWhite))),
      ],
      actions: Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('SPAM', color: removeColor,
          icon: Icons.arrow_back_rounded,
          onTap: flying ? null : () => _decide(0))),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('KEEP', color: keepColor,
          icon: Icons.arrow_forward_rounded,
          onTap: flying ? null : () => _decide(1))),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------- COMMENT CHAOS
class ThreadView extends StatefulWidget {
  final ArcadeCase c;
  final ArcadeDifficulty d;
  final bool tutorial, hint;
  final SubmitFn onSubmit;
  const ThreadView({super.key, required this.c, required this.d,
    required this.tutorial, required this.hint, required this.onSubmit});
  @override
  State<ThreadView> createState() => _ThreadViewState();
}

class _ThreadViewState extends State<ThreadView> {
  final Set<int> flagged = {};

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final c = widget.c;
    return ModeFrame(
      body: [
        if (widget.tutorial) CoachBanner(flagged.isEmpty
            ? 'Tap the comment that crosses the line.'
            : 'Tap REMOVE to take it down.'),
        Paper(fill: const Color(0xFFE9F1FB), child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text('📌  THREAD', style: typewriter(12, color: coal)),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            onPaper(c.title, size: 15.5, weight: FontWeight.w800),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Text(c.subtext, style: captionText(13, color: mutedInk)),
          ])),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        for (var i = 0; i < c.comments.length; i++) Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 9), child: InkWell(
            onTap: () {
              GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
              setState(() { if (!flagged.add(i)) flagged.remove(i); });
            },
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
            child: Container(width: double.infinity, padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: flagged.contains(i) ? const Color(0xFFFFE5E5) : paperWhite,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: flagged.contains(i) ? removeColor : stroke,
                  width: flagged.contains(i) ? 3 : 1.8)),
              child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(children: [
                    Expanded(child: Text(c.comments[i].handle,
                      style: captionText(13, color: mutedInk,
                        weight: FontWeight.w800))),
                    if (flagged.contains(i)) Text('🚩 FLAGGED',
                      style: typewriter(11, color: coal)),
                  ]),
                  if (c.comments[i].context.isNotEmpty) Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 3),
                    child: Text('↳ ${c.comments[i].context}',
                      style: captionText(12.5, color: const Color(0xFF3C5F8A)))),
                  const SizedBox(height: 5),
                  onPaper(c.comments[i].body, size: 15),
                ])))),
      ],
      actions: Row(children: [
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton(
          flagged.isEmpty ? 'REMOVE' : 'REMOVE (${flagged.length})',
          color: removeColor, icon: Icons.delete_outline_rounded,
          glow: widget.tutorial && flagged.isNotEmpty,
          onTap: flagged.isEmpty ? null
              : () => widget.onSubmit(flagged.toList()..sort(), flagged.isNotEmpty))),
        const SizedBox(width: 10),
        Expanded(child: ArcadeButton('ALLOW ALL', color: keepColor,
          icon: Icons.check_rounded,
          onTap: flagged.isNotEmpty || widget.tutorial ? null
              : () => widget.onSubmit(const [], true))),
      ]),
    );
  }
}

/// The Arcade: seven minigames, each with its own mechanic. All people,
/// brands and links are fictional. See arcade_modes_a/b.dart for the games.
class ArcadeHub extends StatefulWidget {
  final GameEngine game;
  final bool embedded;
  final VoidCallback? onBack;
  final VoidCallback? onOpenProfile;
  final ValueChanged<bool>? onPlayingChanged;
  const ArcadeHub({super.key, required this.game, this.embedded = false,
    this.onBack, this.onOpenProfile, this.onPlayingChanged});

  @override
  State<ArcadeHub> createState() => _ArcadeHubState();
}

class _ArcadeHubState extends State<ArcadeHub> {
  ArcadeDifficulty difficulty = ArcadeDifficulty.easy;
  ArcadeSession? session;
  bool introJustCompleted = false;
  bool hintOn = false;
  String? newestCareerReward;

  /// One-time server ticket for the current ranked run. It is requested when
  /// the run starts and redeemed by [_submitScore] when the run ends.
  Future<String?>? runTicket;

  /// The last session already reported, so a run is never counted twice.
  ArcadeSession? _reportedSession;

  static const modeColors = [mustard, blue, mint, coral, pink, parchment,
    Color(0xFFBCAAFF)];
  Color get accent => modeColors[session?.current.mode.index ?? 0];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    GameAudio.instance.setScene(MusicScene.menu);
    super.dispose();
  }

  MusicScene _sceneFor(ArcadeMode mode) => switch (mode) {
    ArcadeMode.trust => MusicScene.trust,
    ArcadeMode.impostor => MusicScene.impostor,
    ArcadeMode.scamSlam => MusicScene.scamSlam,
    ArcadeMode.rumor => MusicScene.rumor,
    ArcadeMode.inbox => MusicScene.inbox,
    ArcadeMode.comments => MusicScene.comments,
    ArcadeMode.clip => MusicScene.clip,
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => PopScope(
    canPop: session == null,
    onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, result) {
      if (!didPop && session != null) _confirmExit();
    },
    child: Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: midnight,
      body: SafeArea(child: DeskBackdrop(child: session == null
          ? (widget.game.arcadeUnlocked ? _hub() : _firstDayStart())
          : _gameplay())),
    ),
  );

  Widget _scroll(List<Widget> children) => SingleChildScrollView(
    physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(17, 14, 17, 26),
    child: Center(child: ConstrainedBox(constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 540),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: children))),
  );

  Widget _header(String title) => Row(children: [
    IconButton(onPressed: widget.onBack ?? () => Navigator.pop(context),
      icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_ios_new_rounded, color: paperWhite),
      tooltip: 'Back to main menu'),
    const SizedBox(width: 4),
    Expanded(child: Text(title, style: titleText(23, color: mustard),
      maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)),
    IconButton(
      tooltip: 'Investigator profile and unlockables',
      icon: const Icon(Icons.workspace_premium_rounded, color: mint),
      onPressed: () {
        GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
        if (widget.onOpenProfile != null) {
          widget.onOpenProfile!();
        } else {
          Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute<void>(
            builder: (_) => CareerIdentityScreen(game: widget.game)));
        }
      },
    ),
    IconButton(
      tooltip: 'Music and sound settings',
      icon: const Icon(Icons.volume_up_rounded, color: paperWhite),
      onPressed: _openAudioSettings,
    ),
    if (!MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width.isFinite ||
        MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width > 395)
      InkTag('${widget.game.careerXp} XP', color: mustard, icon: Icons.bolt_rounded),
  ]);

  Widget _firstDayStart() => _scroll([
    _header('FIRST DAY'),
    const SizedBox(height: 22),
    GestureDetector(onTap: _startIntro, child: Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(22),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: const LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFF514079), Color(0xFF245B65)]),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
        border: Border.all(color: mustard, width: 2.5),
      ),
      child: Column(children: [
        MascotAvatar(game: widget.game, size: 155),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text('WELCOME TO\nBANTAY TAMBAYAN!',
          style: titleText(31, color: paperWhite), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        Text('5 quick tries. Tap the glowing things. That\'s it!',
          style: captionText(15, color: paperWhite), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
        const SizedBox(height: 15),
        const InkTag('TAP ANYWHERE TO START', color: mustard,
          icon: Icons.touch_app_rounded),
      ]),
    )),
    const SizedBox(height: 22),
    SizedBox(width: double.infinity, child: GameButton('PLAY MY FIRST DAY',
      onTap: _startIntro, icon: Icons.play_arrow_rounded, color: mustard)),
    const SizedBox(height: 8),
    Center(child: TextButton(
      onPressed: _skipIntro,
      child: Text('ALREADY PLAYED? SKIP INTRO',
        style: typewriter(11, color: paperWhite)))),
    const SizedBox(height: 10),
    Text('All examples are fictional. Never enter real passwords or OTPs.',
      style: captionText(12, color: paperWhite), textAlign: TextAlign.center),
  ]);

  void _startIntro() {
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.unlock);
    _begin(null, firstDay: true);
  }

  void _skipIntro() {
    GameAudio.instance.enter(MusicScene.menu);
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.unlock);
    widget.game.finishArcadeIntro();
    setState(() { introJustCompleted = true; session = null; });
  }

  Widget _hub() => _scroll([
    _header('SCAMOUFLAGE ARCADE'),
    const SizedBox(height: 16),
    Material(color: Colors.transparent, child: InkWell(
      onTap: () => _begin(null),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(23),
      child: Container(
        width: double.infinity,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: const LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFF544472), Color(0xFF255B69)]),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(23),
          border: Border.all(color: mustard, width: 2),
        ),
        child: Row(children: [
          MascotAvatar(game: widget.game, size: 104),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text('ENDLESS MIX!', style: titleText(28, color: paperWhite)),
              const SizedBox(height: 7),
              Text('All your unlocked minigames, shuffled. How long can you last?',
                style: captionText(13, color: paperWhite)),
              const SizedBox(height: 9),
              const InkTag('TAP TO PLAY', color: mustard, icon: Icons.shuffle_rounded),
            ])),
        ]),
      ),
    )),
    if (introJustCompleted) ...[
      const SizedBox(height: 12),
      Container(width: double.infinity, padding: const EdgeInsets.all(13),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: const Color(0xFFE5F3EB),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13)),
        child: Text('🎉 Arcade unlocked! Earn XP to open more games.',
          style: captionText(14, color: coal))),
    ],
    const SizedBox(height: 22),
    Text('HOW TRICKY?', style: typewriter(12, color: blue)),
    const SizedBox(height: 9),
    Row(children: ArcadeDifficulty.values.map((level) {
      final active = difficulty == level;
      return Expanded(child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 7),
        child: GameButton(level.title, compact: true,
          color: active ? mustard : blue, outlined: !active,
          icon: active ? Icons.check_rounded : Icons.adjust_rounded,
          onTap: () => setState(() => difficulty = level)),
      ));
    }).toList()),
    const SizedBox(height: 8),
    Row(children: [
      const Icon(Icons.favorite_rounded, color: coral, size: 18),
      const SizedBox(width: 7),
      Expanded(child: Text('${difficulty.startingHearts} hearts • ${_difficultyNote()}',
        style: captionText(12.5, color: paperWhite))),
    ]),
    const SizedBox(height: 23),
    Text('ARCADE MINIGAMES • FREE PLAY', style: typewriter(12, color: blue)),
    const SizedBox(height: 10),
    ...([...ArcadeMode.values]..sort((a, b) => a.unlockXp.compareTo(b.unlockXp))).map((mode) {
      final unlocked = widget.game.careerXp >= mode.unlockXp;
      final color = modeColors[mode.index];
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
        child: Material(color: Colors.transparent, child: InkWell(
          onTap: () => unlocked ? _begin(mode) : _showLocked(mode),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(13),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: deskBlue, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
              border: Border.all(color: unlocked ? color : deskLight,
                width: unlocked ? 1.8 : 1),
            ),
            child: Row(children: [
              Container(height: 52, width: 52, alignment: Alignment.center,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: color.withValues(alpha: unlocked ? .22 : .08),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14)),
                child: Text(unlocked ? mode.emoji : '🔒',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 27))),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(mode.title, style: titleText(19,
                    color: unlocked ? paperWhite : parchment)),
                  const SizedBox(height: 2),
                  Text(unlocked ? mode.subtitle : 'UNLOCK AT ${mode.unlockXp} CAREER XP',
                    style: captionText(12, color: unlocked ? paperWhite : mustard)),
                ])),
              Icon(unlocked ? Icons.play_circle_rounded : Icons.lock_rounded,
                size: 25, color: unlocked ? color : parchment),
            ]),
          ),
        )),
      );
    }),
    const SizedBox(height: 14),
    Center(child: Text('XP IS PERMANENT • UNLOCKS COST NOTHING',
      style: typewriter(11, color: paperWhite), textAlign: TextAlign.center)),
    const SizedBox(height: 12),
    Center(child: TextButton.icon(onPressed: () => _begin(null, firstDay: true),
      icon: const Icon(Icons.school_rounded, color: blue),
      label: Text('REPLAY FIRST DAY', style: captionText(13, color: paperWhite)))),
  ]);

  String _difficultyNote() => switch (difficulty) {
    ArcadeDifficulty.easy => 'all checks open, no timers',
    ArcadeDifficulty.medium => 'fewer checks, timed inbox',
    ArcadeDifficulty.hard => 'few checks, noisy replies, fast inbox',
  };

  void _openAudioSettings() {
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.tap);
    showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context, isScrollControlled: true, backgroundColor: deskBlue,
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24))),
      builder: (ctx) => SafeArea(child: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 16, 20, 22),
        child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Text('AUDIO SETTINGS', style: titleText(24, color: mustard)),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          const ScamouflageAudioControls(),
        ]),
      )),
    );
  }

  void _showLocked(ArcadeMode mode) {
    final remaining = mode.unlockXp - widget.game.careerXp;
    _showMessage('${mode.title} unlocks in $remaining more XP. Play a free mode or Endless Mix!');
  }

  void _begin(ArcadeMode? selected, {bool firstDay = false}) {
    if (!firstDay && !widget.game.arcadeUnlocked) {
      _startIntro();
      return;
    }
    if (!firstDay && selected != null && widget.game.careerXp < selected.unlockXp) {
      _showLocked(selected);
      return;
    }
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
    setState(() {
      session = ArcadeSession(
        difficulty: firstDay ? ArcadeDifficulty.easy : difficulty,
        selectedMode: selected,
        firstDay: firstDay,
        awardCareerXp: widget.game.awardArcadeXp,
        careerXp: () => widget.game.careerXp,
        recentHistory: widget.game.arcadeHistory.toSet(),
        onCaseGenerated: widget.game.recordArcadeCase,
      );
      hintOn = false;
    });
    // Ranked runs get a one-time server ticket; the tutorial day is unranked.
    runTicket = firstDay
        ? null
        : LeaderboardService.instance.startRun(
            mode: selected, difficulty: difficulty);
    final started = session;
    if (started != null) {
      widget.onPlayingChanged?.call(true);
      GameAudio.instance.enter(_sceneFor(started.current.mode));
    }
  }

  void _useHint() {
    final s = session;
    if (s == null || s.waitingForNext || hintOn) return;
    // Hints are free during the first-day tutorial.
    if (!s.firstDay && !widget.game.spendHint()) {
      _showMessage('Earn 50 Career XP to get another hint.');
      return;
    }
    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.hint);
    setState(() => hintOn = true);
  }

  void _submitScore(ArcadeSession s) {
    if (identical(_reportedSession, s)) return;
    _reportedSession = s;

    // Lifetime stats are part of the synced player profile.
    widget.game.recordRun(
      rounds: s.round,
      correct: s.successful,
      bestStreak: s.bestStreak,
      score: s.score,
    );

    // The tutorial day never reaches the leaderboard.
    if (s.firstDay) return;
    final ticket = runTicket ?? Future<String?>.value(null);
    runTicket = null;
    LeaderboardService.instance.finishRun(
      ticket: ticket,
      score: s.score,
      rounds: s.round,
      correct: s.successful,
      bestStreak: s.bestStreak,
    );
  }

  void _submit(List<int> answer, bool usedDecisive) {
    final s = session;
    if (s == null || s.waitingForNext) return;
    final xpBefore = widget.game.careerXp;
    setState(() => s.submit(answer, usedDecisive: usedDecisive));
    if (s.finished) _submitScore(s);
    final xpAfter = widget.game.careerXp;
    final unlocked = ArcadeMode.values.any((m) =>
        m.unlockXp > xpBefore && m.unlockXp <= xpAfter);
    final gainedRank = CareerProgression.rankAt(xpAfter);
    final newRank = CareerProgression.rankAt(xpBefore).level < gainedRank.level;
    final newBadge = CareerProgression.badges.where((b) =>
        b.xp > xpBefore && b.xp <= xpAfter).toList();
    final newFrame = CareerProgression.frames.where((f) =>
        f.xp > xpBefore && f.xp <= xpAfter).toList();
    newestCareerReward = newRank
        ? 'LEVEL ${gainedRank.level} • ${gainedRank.title} unlocked!'
        : newBadge.isNotEmpty
            ? 'NEW BADGE • ${newBadge.last.name}!'
            : newFrame.isNotEmpty
                ? 'NEW FRAME • ${newFrame.last.name}!'
                : null;
    GameAudio.instance.cue(unlocked || newestCareerReward != null ? GameCue.unlock
        : s.finished ? (s.firstDay ? GameCue.victory : GameCue.defeat)
        : s.lastCorrect == true ? GameCue.correct : GameCue.wrong);
    if (s.firstDay && s.finished) {
      widget.game.finishArcadeIntro();
      introJustCompleted = true;
    }
    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
  }

  void _next() {
    final s = session;
    if (s == null) return;
    GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.tap);
    if (s.finished) {
      newestCareerReward = null;
      setState(() => session = null);
      widget.onPlayingChanged?.call(false);
      GameAudio.instance.enter(MusicScene.menu);
      final level = widget.game.takeLevelUp();
      if (level != null) {
        WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
          if (mounted) showLevelUpCelebration(context, widget.game, level);
        });
      }
    } else {
      setState(() { s.next(); hintOn = false; newestCareerReward = null; });
      GameAudio.instance.setScene(_sceneFor(s.current.mode));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _confirmExit() async {
    final shouldExit = await showDialog<bool>(context: context, builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(
      backgroundColor: paperWhite,
      title: Text('LEAVE THIS RUN?', style: titleText(24, color: coal)),
      content: Text('Your earned Career XP stays saved.',
        style: captionText(15, color: coal)),
      actions: [
        TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
          child: Text('STAY', style: captionText(15, color: coal))),
        TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
          child: Text('LEAVE', style: captionText(15, color: coal))),
      ],
    ));
    if (!mounted || shouldExit != true) return;
    final leaving = session;
    if (leaving != null && !leaving.finished) _submitScore(leaving);
    setState(() => session = null);
    widget.onPlayingChanged?.call(false);
    GameAudio.instance.enter(MusicScene.menu);
    // XP remains earned if the player leaves early; celebrate any new rank.
    final level = widget.game.takeLevelUp();
    if (level != null) {
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted) showLevelUpCelebration(context, widget.game, level);
      });
    }
  }

  void _showMessage(String message) {
    final messenger = ScaffoldMessenger.of(context);
    messenger.hideCurrentSnackBar();
    messenger.showSnackBar(SnackBar(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF293D5B),
      behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 0, 12, 82),
      duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),
      content: Text(message, style: captionText(14, color: paperWhite)),
    ));
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------- gameplay
  Widget _mobileGameHeader(ArcadeSession s, ArcadeCase c) {
    final name = s.firstDay
        ? 'FIRST DAY • ${s.round + (s.waitingForNext ? 0 : 1)}/5'
        : c.mode.title;
    final subtitle = s.firstDay ? c.mode.title
        : '${s.selectedMode == null ? 'ENDLESS MIX' : difficulty.title} • ROUND ${s.round + (s.waitingForNext ? 0 : 1)}';
    final compact = PhoneLayout.compact(context);
    final title = Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
        Text(name, style: titleText(compact ? 16 : 18, color: accent),
          maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
        Text(subtitle, style: typewriter(11, color: paperWhite),
          maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
      ]);
    final audioButton = IconButton(
      tooltip: 'Audio settings',
      visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
      onPressed: _openAudioSettings,
      icon: const Icon(Icons.volume_up_rounded, size: 21, color: paperWhite),
    );
    final hint = Tooltip(message: 'Hint • +1 every 50 Career XP', child:
      TextButton.icon(
        onPressed: _useHint,
        style: TextButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: mustard,
          minimumSize: const Size(49, 42),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 9, vertical: 6)),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.lightbulb_rounded, size: 19, color: coal),
        label: Text(s.firstDay ? 'FREE' : '${widget.game.hintsAvailable}',
          style: typewriter(11, color: coal)),
      ));
    final status = Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
      const Icon(Icons.favorite_rounded, color: coal, size: 0),
      for (var i = 0; i < difficulty.startingHearts && !s.firstDay; i++)
        Icon(i < s.hearts ? Icons.favorite_rounded : Icons.favorite_border_rounded,
          color: coral, size: 16),
      if (s.firstDay) ...[
        const Icon(Icons.favorite_rounded, color: coral, size: 17),
        Text(' ${s.hearts}', style: captionText(13, color: paperWhite)),
      ],
      const SizedBox(width: 9),
      Text('${s.score} XP', style: typewriter(11, color: mustard)),
      if (s.streak >= 2) Text('  🔥${s.streak}',
        style: captionText(13, color: paperWhite)),
    ]);
    return Container(
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(5, 6, 12, compact ? 8 : 9),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: deskBlue,
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: accent, width: 2))),
      child: compact
        ? Column(children: [
            Row(children: [
              IconButton(onPressed: _confirmExit,
                tooltip: 'Leave run', icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, color: paperWhite)),
              Expanded(child: title),
              audioButton,
              hint,
            ]),
            Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 48, top: 1),
              child: Align(alignment: Alignment.centerRight, child: status)),
          ])
        : Row(children: [
            IconButton(onPressed: _confirmExit,
              tooltip: 'Leave run', icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, color: paperWhite)),
            Expanded(child: title),
            audioButton,
            hint,
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            status,
          ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _gameplay() {
    final s = session!;
    final c = s.current;
    return Column(children: [
      _mobileGameHeader(s, c),
      if (!s.firstDay || hintOn) Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 9, 14, 0),
        child: hintOn ? _hintPanel(c.hint) : _howTo(c.mode)),
      Expanded(child: Stack(children: [
        Positioned.fill(child: KeyedSubtree(
          key: ValueKey('${c.signature}:${s.round}'),
          child: _surface(s, c))),
        if (s.waitingForNext) Positioned.fill(child: _resultOverlay(s, c)),
      ])),
    ]);
  }

  Widget _howTo(ArcadeMode mode) => Row(children: [
    Text(mode.emoji, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18)),
    const SizedBox(width: 8),
    Expanded(child: Text(mode.how, style: captionText(13.5, color: paperWhite,
      weight: FontWeight.w700))),
  ]);

  Widget _hintPanel(String message) => Container(
    width: double.infinity,
    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: const Color(0xFFFFF1CC),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
      border: Border.all(color: mustard, width: 2)),
    child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      const Icon(Icons.lightbulb_rounded, color: coal, size: 22),
      const SizedBox(width: 9),
      Expanded(child: Text(message, style: captionText(14, color: coal))),
    ]),
  );

  Widget _surface(ArcadeSession s, ArcadeCase c) {
    final tut = s.firstDay;
    final d = s.firstDay ? ArcadeDifficulty.easy : difficulty;
    return switch (c.mode) {
      ArcadeMode.trust => TrustView(c: c, d: d, tutorial: tut, hint: hintOn, onSubmit: _submit),
      ArcadeMode.impostor => ImpostorView(c: c, d: d, tutorial: tut, hint: hintOn, onSubmit: _submit),
      ArcadeMode.scamSlam => ChatView(c: c, d: d, tutorial: tut, hint: hintOn, onSubmit: _submit),
      ArcadeMode.rumor => RumorView(c: c, d: d, tutorial: tut, hint: hintOn, onSubmit: _submit),
      ArcadeMode.inbox => InboxView(c: c, d: d, tutorial: tut, hint: hintOn, onSubmit: _submit),
      ArcadeMode.comments => ThreadView(c: c, d: d, tutorial: tut, hint: hintOn, onSubmit: _submit),
      ArcadeMode.clip => ClipView(c: c, d: d, tutorial: tut, hint: hintOn, onSubmit: _submit),
    };
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------- result
  Widget _resultOverlay(ArcadeSession s, ArcadeCase c) {
    final correct = s.lastCorrect == true;
    final partial = c.isBatch && !correct && s.lastParts > 0;
    final maxH = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height * .72;
    final bg = correct ? const Color(0xFFE5F3E9) : const Color(0xFFFFEDEA);
    final edge = correct ? const Color(0xFF397E61) : const Color(0xFFB7515E);
    final headline = correct ? 'NICE! +${s.lastEarnedXp} XP'
        : partial ? '${s.lastParts}/${c.totalParts} RIGHT • −1 ♥'
        : s.firstDay ? 'GOOD TRY! LET\'S LEARN' : 'NOT QUITE • −1 ♥';
    return Container(
      color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: .5),
      alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
      child: Container(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: maxH, maxWidth: 560),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(color: bg,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
          border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: edge, width: 4))),
        child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
          Flexible(child: SingleChildScrollView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 16, 18, 8),
            child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
              Row(children: [
                Icon(correct ? Icons.verified_rounded : Icons.lightbulb_outline_rounded,
                  color: coal, size: 30),
                const SizedBox(width: 9),
                Expanded(child: Text(headline, style: titleText(23, color: coal))),
              ]),
              if (correct && s.lastUsedDecisive) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text('Includes +15 XP for checking decisive evidence!',
                    style: captionText(12, color: coal)),
              ],
              if (newestCareerReward != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 9),
                Container(
                  width: double.infinity,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0xFFFFE8A6),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                  child: Row(children: [
                    const Icon(Icons.workspace_premium_rounded, color: coal),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Expanded(child: Text(
                      '$newestCareerReward Visit My Investigator to customize.',
                      style: captionText(13, color: coal))),
                  ]),
                ),
              ],
              if (partial && s.lastEarnedXp > 0) Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                child: Text('+${s.lastEarnedXp} XP for the right ones',
                  style: captionText(14, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w700))),
              if (s.streak >= 2) Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                child: Text('🔥 ${s.streak} in a row',
                  style: captionText(14, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800))),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              ..._recap(c, s),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text('WHY IT MATTERS', style: typewriter(11, color: coal)),
              const SizedBox(height: 4),
              Text(c.lesson, style: captionText(15, color: coal)),
              if (s.finished) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(s.firstDay ? 'DAY COMPLETE • ARCADE UNLOCKED 🎉'
                    : 'RUN OVER • ${s.successful}/${s.round} correct • ${s.score} XP',
                  style: captionText(14, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800)),
              ],
            ]),
          )),
          Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 4, 16, 12),
            child: SafeArea(top: false, child: SizedBox(width: double.infinity,
              child: ArcadeButton(s.finished
                  ? (s.firstDay ? 'OPEN ARCADE!' : 'BACK TO ARCADE')
                  : 'NEXT CHALLENGE', color: mustard, onTap: _next)))),
        ]),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _recapLine(String label, String text) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
    child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      Text(label, style: typewriter(11, color: coal)),
      const SizedBox(height: 3),
      Text(text, style: captionText(14.5, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w700)),
    ]));

  Widget _bubble(String text) => Container(
    margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 7),
    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(11),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: paperWhite,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
      border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.5)),
    child: Text(text, style: captionText(14.5, color: coal)));

  List<Widget> _recap(ArcadeCase c, ArcadeSession s) {
    final a = s.lastAnswer;
    switch (c.mode) {
      case ArcadeMode.trust:
      case ArcadeMode.clip:
        return [for (final t in c.tools)
          if (t.relevance == Relevance.decisive)
            _recapLine('KEY CLUE • ${t.emoji} ${t.label}', t.text)];
      case ArcadeMode.impostor:
        return [_recapLine('ANSWER', c.correctChoice == 2
            ? 'Both accounts are OK.'
            : 'Flag account ${c.correctChoice == 0 ? 'A' : 'B'}.')];
      case ArcadeMode.scamSlam:
        final chosen = a.isEmpty ? 0 : a.first;
        return [
          if (s.lastCorrect != true)
            _recapLine('BEST MOVE', c.choices[c.correctChoice].text),
          Text('WHAT HAPPENS NEXT', style: typewriter(11, color: coal)),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          for (final f in c.followUps[chosen] ?? const <String>[]) _bubble(f),
        ];
      case ArcadeMode.rumor:
        return switch (c.rumorTask!) {
          RumorTask.order => [_recapLine('CORRECT ORDER',
              [for (var i = 0; i < c.correct.length; i++)
                '${i + 1}. ${c.cards[c.correct[i]].title}'].join('\n'))],
          RumorTask.wordEdit => [_recapLine('CHANGED WORDS',
              c.correct.map((i) => c.tokens[i]).join(' '))],
          _ => [_recapLine('RIGHT ANSWER',
              '${c.cards[c.correctChoice].title}: ${c.cards[c.correctChoice].body}')],
        };
      case ArcadeMode.inbox:
        return [for (var i = 0; i < c.emails.length; i++) Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 9),
          child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Icon(i < a.length && a[i] == c.correct[i]
                ? Icons.check_circle_rounded : Icons.cancel_rounded,
              color: coal, size: 20),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text('${c.emails[i].spam ? 'SPAM' : 'KEEP'} • ${c.emails[i].subject}',
                  style: captionText(14, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800)),
                Text(c.emails[i].why, style: captionText(13.5, color: coal)),
              ])),
          ]))];
      case ArcadeMode.comments:
        final wrong = [for (final i in a) if (!c.comments[i].violates) i];
        return [
          if (c.correct.isEmpty) _recapLine('ANSWER',
              'None of these comments break the rules.'),
          for (final i in c.correct) _recapLine('REMOVE • ${c.comments[i].handle}',
              c.comments[i].reason),
          for (final i in wrong) _recapLine('ALLOWED • ${c.comments[i].handle}',
              'Harsh or annoying, but not a rule break.'),
        ];
    }
  }
}

/// Colour rules for every arcade decision.
///  green  = KEEP / ALLOW / BOTH OK      red = REMOVE / FLAG / SPAM
///  orange, pink and blue = all other (neutral) choices.
const keepColor = Color(0xFF7FE3A6);
const removeColor = Color(0xFFFF7D7D);
const choiceOrange = Color(0xFFFFB36D);
const choicePink = Color(0xFFFFB1D3);
const choiceBlue = Color(0xFFA3D6F6);
const chipFill = Color(0xFFE3ECF8);
const chipOpenFill = Color(0xFFC6D9F1);

/// White "paper" card used for every case surface.
class Paper extends StatelessWidget {

  const Paper({super.key, required this.child, this.fill = paperWhite,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(14)});

Widget onPaper(String text, {double size = 15, FontWeight? weight}) => Text(
  text, style: captionText(size, color: coal,
    weight: weight ?? FontWeight.w600));

/// Draws a soft pulsing glow around [child] while [active].
class Pulse extends StatefulWidget {
  final bool active;
  final Widget child;
  final double radius;
  const Pulse({super.key, required this.active, required this.child,
    this.radius = 14});
  @override
  State<Pulse> createState() => _PulseState();
}

class _PulseState extends State<Pulse> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _c = AnimationController(
    vsync: this, duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 900));
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    if (widget.active) _c.repeat(reverse: true);
  }
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(Pulse old) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(old);
    if (widget.active && !_c.isAnimating) _c.repeat(reverse: true);
    if (!widget.active && _c.isAnimating) { _c.stop(); _c.value = 0; }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() { _c.dispose(); super.dispose(); }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (!widget.active) return widget.child;
    return AnimatedBuilder(animation: _c, child: widget.child,
      builder: (context, child) => DecoratedBox(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(widget.radius + 3),
          boxShadow: [BoxShadow(
            color: mustard.withValues(alpha: .35 + .45 * _c.value),
            blurRadius: 6 + 12 * _c.value, spreadRadius: 1 + 3 * _c.value)]),
        child: child));
  }
}

/// Chief's short coaching line for the first-day tutorial.

      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
      border: Border.all(color: mustard, width: 2)),

      const Text('🦎', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24)),
      const SizedBox(width: 10),
      Expanded(child: Text(text,
        style: captionText(15, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800))),
    ]),
  );
}

class ArcadeButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;
  final bool glow;
  const ArcadeButton(this.label, {super.key, required this.color,
    required this.onTap, this.icon = Icons.arrow_forward_rounded,
    this.glow = false});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Pulse(active: glow && onTap != null,
    child: Opacity(opacity: onTap == null ? .45 : 1,
      child: Material(color: Colors.transparent, child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
        child: Container(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 52),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
            border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 2),
            boxShadow: const [BoxShadow(color: stroke, offset: Offset(0, 3))]),
          child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [
            Flexible(child: Text(label, textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              maxLines: 3, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: captionText(14, color: coal, weight: FontWeight.w800))),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Icon(icon, size: 20, color: coal),
          ]),
        ),
      ))));
}

/// Neutral inspection button. Looks identical whatever the result is.
class ToolChip extends StatelessWidget {
  final String emoji, label;
  final bool opened, enabled, glow;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  const ToolChip({super.key, required this.emoji, required this.label,
    required this.opened, required this.enabled, required this.onTap,
    this.glow = false});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Pulse(active: glow && !opened,
    radius: 12,
    child: Opacity(opacity: enabled || opened ? 1 : .4,
      child: Material(color: Colors.transparent, child: InkWell(
        onTap: enabled || opened ? onTap : null,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 11, vertical: 11),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: opened ? chipOpenFill : chipFill,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(color: stroke, width: 1.8)),
          child: Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
            Text(emoji, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16)),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(label, style: captionText(12.5, color: coal,
              weight: FontWeight.w800)),
            if (opened) ...[const SizedBox(width: 5),
              const Icon(Icons.check_rounded, size: 15, color: coal)],
          ]),
        ),
      ))));
}

/// Readable bottom sheet for details that do not fit on a card.
Future<void> showDetailSheet(BuildContext context,
    {required String title, required List<Widget> children}) {
  return showModalBottomSheet<void>(
    context: context, isScrollControlled: true,
    backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
    builder: (ctx) {
      final h = MediaQuery.sizeOf(ctx).height;
      return Container(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: h * .78),
        decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: paperWhite,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
          border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: stroke, width: 3))),
        child: SafeArea(top: false, child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 16, 18, 18),
          child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
              Row(children: [
                Expanded(child: Text(title,
                  style: titleText(22, color: coal))),
                IconButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.close_rounded, color: coal)),
              ]),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              ...children,
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              SizedBox(width: double.infinity, child: ArcadeButton('GOT IT',
                color: mustard, icon: Icons.check_rounded,
                onTap: () => Navigator.pop(ctx))),
            ]),
        )),
      );
    });
}

/// previews, not working authentication or cloud storage.
class CareerIdentityScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  final GameEngine game;
  final VoidCallback? onBack;
  const CareerIdentityScreen({super.key, required this.game, this.onBack});

  static const _framePalette = <String, Color>{
    'classic': blue,
    'sunny': mustard,
    'bubblegum': pink,
    'mint': mint,
    'violet': Color(0xFFBCA5FF),
  };

  void _equip(bool Function() action) {
    if (action()) GameAudio.instance.cue(GameCue.choose);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Scaffold(
    backgroundColor: midnight,
    body: SafeArea(child: DeskBackdrop(child: AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: game,
      builder: (context, _) => SingleChildScrollView(
        physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 32),
        child: Center(child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 560),

                tooltip: 'Back to the game',

                icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back_rounded, color: paperWhite),
              ),
              Expanded(child: Text('MY INVESTIGATOR',
                style: titleText(23, color: mustard))),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            _identityCard(),
            const SizedBox(height: 22),
            _sectionTitle("CHAMELEO'S EVOLUTIONS", '10 outfits • each rank gets a new look.'),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            _evolutionPreview(),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _sectionTitle('MASCOT NICKNAMES', 'Choose a new nickname for your companion.'),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Wrap(spacing: 10, runSpacing: 10, children: [
              for (final item in CareerProgression.mascotNames)
                _cosmeticTile(item.name, item.symbol, item.xp,
                  game.equippedMascotNameId == item.id,
                  () => _equip(() => game.equipMascotName(item.id)), context: context),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _sectionTitle('MASCOT COLORS', 'Unlocked colors change the mascot, not your XP.'),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Wrap(spacing: 10, runSpacing: 10, children: [
              for (final item in CareerProgression.mascotColors)
                _cosmeticTile(item.name, item.symbol, item.xp,
                  game.equippedMascotColorId == item.id,
                  () => _equip(() => game.equipMascotColor(item.id)),
                  accent: MascotPalette.forId(item.id), context: context),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _sectionTitle('EARNED TITLES', 'Choose the title under your name.'),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            ...CareerProgression.ranks.map((rank) => _choice(
              rank.title, 'LEVEL ${rank.level} • ${rank.xp} XP',
              rank.xp <= game.careerXp, game.equippedTitleId == rank.id,
              Icons.verified_outlined,
              () => _equip(() => game.equipCareerTitle(rank.id)))),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _sectionTitle('BADGE COLLECTION', 'Your chosen badge appears on your profile.'),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Wrap(spacing: 10, runSpacing: 10, children: [
              for (final badge in CareerProgression.badges)
                _cosmeticTile(
                  badge.name, badge.symbol, badge.xp,
                  game.equippedBadgeId == badge.id,
                  () => _equip(() => game.equipBadge(badge.id)), context: context),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            _sectionTitle('PROFILE FRAMES', 'Earn a color frame through Career XP.'),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Wrap(spacing: 10, runSpacing: 10, children: [
              for (final frame in CareerProgression.frames)
                _cosmeticTile(
                  frame.name, frame.symbol, frame.xp,
                  game.equippedFrameId == frame.id,
                  () => _equip(() => game.equipFrame(frame.id)),
                  accent: _framePalette[frame.id], context: context,),
            ]),
            const SizedBox(height: 24),
            const AccountPanel(),
          ]),
        )),
      ),
    ))),
  );

  Widget _identityCard() {
    final current = game.careerRank;
    final next = game.nextCareerRank;
    final frame = _framePalette[game.equippedFrameId] ?? blue;
    final badge = CareerProgression.badgeById(game.equippedBadgeId)?.symbol ?? '🦎';
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
        border: Border.all(color: frame, width: 3),
        gradient: LinearGradient(colors: [deskBlue, frame.withValues(alpha: .24)]),
      ),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center, children: [
          SizedBox(width: 128, height: 128, child: Stack(
            alignment: Alignment.center, clipBehavior: Clip.none, children: [
              SizedBox(width: 120, height: 120, child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                value: game.careerLevelProgress, strokeWidth: 8,
                strokeCap: StrokeCap.round, color: frame,
                backgroundColor: midnight)),
              MascotAvatar(game: game, size: 105),
              Positioned(bottom: -3, right: 2, child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 5),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: midnight,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                  border: Border.all(color: frame, width: 2)),
                child: Text('LV ${current.level}',
                  style: typewriter(10, color: mustard)))),
              Positioned(bottom: -5, left: 0, child: Text(badge,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 24))),
            ])),
          const SizedBox(width: 17),
          Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text(game.mascotName.toUpperCase(), style: typewriter(11, color: frame)),
            const SizedBox(height: 5),
            Text('LEVEL ${current.level} / 10', style: typewriter(12, color: mustard)),
            const SizedBox(height: 7),
            Text(game.equippedTitle, style: titleText(22, color: paperWhite)),
            const SizedBox(height: 7),
            Text('${game.careerXp} CAREER XP', style: captionText(14, color: mustard)),
            const SizedBox(height: 5),
            Text('Local investigator profile',
              style: captionText(12, color: parchment)),
          ])),
        ]),
        const SizedBox(height: 19),
        ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          child: LinearProgressIndicator(
            value: game.careerLevelProgress,
            backgroundColor: midnight,
            color: frame,
            minHeight: 9)),
        const SizedBox(height: 7),
        Text(next == null
            ? 'MAX LEVEL REACHED • All investigator ranks earned!'
            : '${next.xp - game.careerXp} XP TO LEVEL ${next.level} • ${next.title}',
          style: captionText(12, color: paperWhite)),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        Text('XP unlocks titles and cosmetics automatically. Equipping is free.',
          style: captionText(12, color: parchment)),
      ]),
    );
  }

  Widget _evolutionPreview() => SizedBox(height: 154,
    child: ListView.separated(
      scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
      itemCount: CareerProgression.ranks.length,
      separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(width: 8),
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final rank = CareerProgression.ranks[index];
        final unlocked = game.careerXp >= rank.xp;
        return Container(width: 112,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(6),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: deskBlue, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            border: Border.all(color: unlocked ? mustard : deskLight)),
          child: Column(children: [
            Opacity(opacity: unlocked ? 1 : .45,
              child: MascotAvatar(game: game, size: 91, level: rank.level)),
            const SizedBox(height: 3),
            Text('LV ${rank.level}', style: typewriter(10, color: unlocked ? mustard : parchment)),
            Text(unlocked ? rank.title : '${rank.xp} XP',
              style: captionText(10, color: parchment),
              maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis),
          ]),
        );
      },
    ),
  );

  Widget _sectionTitle(String title, String caption) => Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
      Text(title, style: titleText(21, color: paperWhite)),
      const SizedBox(height: 3),
      Text(caption, style: captionText(12, color: parchment)),
    ]);

  Widget _choice(String title, String threshold, bool unlocked,
      bool selected, IconData icon, VoidCallback select) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 7),
    child: Material(color: Colors.transparent, child: InkWell(
      onTap: unlocked ? select : null,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 13, vertical: 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? deskLight : deskBlue,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
          border: Border.all(color: selected ? mint : deskLight, width: selected ? 2 : 1),
        ),
        child: Row(children: [
          Icon(unlocked ? icon : Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
            color: unlocked ? mustard : parchment, size: 24),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
            Text(title, style: captionText(15,
              color: unlocked ? paperWhite : parchment)),
            Text(unlocked ? threshold : 'LOCKED • $threshold',
              style: typewriter(10, color: unlocked ? blue : parchment)),
          ])),
          if (selected) const Icon(Icons.check_circle_rounded, color: mint)
          else if (unlocked) const Icon(Icons.radio_button_unchecked_rounded,
            color: parchment),
        ]),
      ),
    )),
  );

  Widget _cosmeticTile(String title, String symbol, int xp, bool selected,
    VoidCallback select, {Color? accent, required BuildContext context}) {
    final unlocked = xp <= game.careerXp;
    return Material(color: Colors.transparent, child: InkWell(
      onTap: unlocked ? select : null,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
      child: Container(
        width: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width < 380 ? 132 : 153,
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 116),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected ? deskLight : deskBlue,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
          border: Border.all(color: selected ? (accent ?? mint) : deskLight,
            width: selected ? 2.5 : 1),
        ),
        child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
          Row(children: [
            Opacity(opacity: unlocked ? 1 : .4,
              child: accent == null
                ? Text(symbol, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 27))
                : Container(width: 29, height: 29,
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: accent, shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      border: Border.all(color: paperWhite, width: 2)))),
            const Spacer(),
            Icon(selected ? Icons.check_circle_rounded :
              unlocked ? Icons.radio_button_unchecked_rounded : Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
              size: 17, color: selected ? mint : parchment),
          ]),
          const SizedBox(height: 5),
          Text(title, maxLines: 2, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: captionText(13, color: unlocked ? paperWhite : parchment)),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(unlocked ? (selected ? 'EQUIPPED' : 'TAP TO EQUIP') : '$xp XP TO UNLOCK',
            style: typewriter(10, color: unlocked ? blue : parchment)),
        ]),
      ),
    ));
  }
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\views\case_media.dart =====nimport 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'theme.dart';

/// Offline, original, topic-aware editorial art. No external assets, no
/// misleading photographs, no network dependency. Changes when comparing the
/// repost with its supposed source so the comparison is visually actionable.
class CaseMedia extends StatelessWidget {
  final String topic;
  final String caption;
  final String sourceCaption;
  final bool original;
  final double height;
  /// Optional corner label, e.g. 'VIDEO'. Defaults to SHARED POST / ORIGINAL VIEW.
  final String? badge;
  const CaseMedia({super.key,required this.topic,required this.caption,this.sourceCaption='SOURCE VIEW',this.original=false,this.height=196,this.badge});

  @override Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final icon = switch(topic){
      'Drag Race' => Icons.auto_awesome_rounded,
      'Government' => Icons.account_balance_rounded,
      'Campus' => Icons.school_rounded,
      'Basketball memorial' => Icons.sports_basketball_rounded,
      'Shopping' => Icons.local_shipping_rounded,
      'Delivery' => Icons.inventory_2_rounded,
      'Weather' => Icons.thunderstorm_rounded,
      'Music' => Icons.music_note_rounded,
      'Health' => Icons.medical_services_rounded,
      'AI' => Icons.smart_toy_rounded,
      'Election' => Icons.how_to_vote_rounded,
      _ => Icons.newspaper_rounded,
    };
    final colors = switch(topic){
      'Drag Race' => [const Color(0xFF602C90),const Color(0xFFF87AB6)],
      'Government' => [const Color(0xFF125D79),const Color(0xFF65C2D9)],
      'Campus' => [const Color(0xFF4262A3),const Color(0xFF9ED9DE)],
      'Basketball memorial' => [const Color(0xFF8D4D39),const Color(0xFFE9B36F)],
      'Weather' => [const Color(0xFF29557A),const Color(0xFF8CC8E8)],
      'Music' => [const Color(0xFFB03A7C),const Color(0xFFFFB86B)],
      'Health' => [const Color(0xFF1F7A6D),const Color(0xFFA6E6C9)],
      'AI' => [const Color(0xFF3B3B8F),const Color(0xFF7FE0E6)],
      'Election' => [const Color(0xFF8A3A3A),const Color(0xFFF2C46B)],
      _ => [const Color(0xFF515895),const Color(0xFFB18DC9)],
    };
    return SizedBox(height:height,width:double.infinity,child:ClipRRect(
      borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(15),
      child:Stack(children:[
        Positioned.fill(child:CustomPaint(painter:_PosterPainter(colors[0],colors[1],topic,original))),
        Align(alignment:Alignment.center,child:Container(
          width:124,height:124,
          decoration:BoxDecoration(color:paperWhite.withValues(alpha:.18),shape:BoxShape.circle,border:Border.all(color:paperWhite.withValues(alpha:.38),width:3)),
          child:Icon(icon,size:76,color:paperWhite),
        )),
        Positioned(top:11,left:11,child:Container(
          padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:10,vertical:7),
          decoration:BoxDecoration(color:midnight.withValues(alpha:.82),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(8)),
          child:Text(badge??(original?'ORIGINAL VIEW':'SHARED POST'),style:typewriter(11,color:paperWhite)),
        )),
        Positioned(left:9,right:9,bottom:9,child:Container(
          padding:const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal:9,vertical:8),
          decoration:BoxDecoration(color:midnight.withValues(alpha:.84),borderRadius:BorderRadius.circular(9)),
          child:Text(original?_shorten(sourceCaption):_shorten(caption),textAlign:TextAlign.center,maxLines:2,overflow:TextOverflow.ellipsis,style:captionText(13,color:paperWhite,weight:FontWeight.bold)),
        )),
      ]),
    ));
  }

  static String _shorten(String s) {
    final cleaned=s.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s+'),' ').trim();
    return cleaned.length>73?'${cleaned.substring(0,73)}…':cleaned;
  }
}

class _PosterPainter extends CustomPainter {
  final Color first,second;
  final String topic;
  final bool original;
  const _PosterPainter(this.first,this.second,this.topic,this.original);
  @override void paint(Canvas canvas,Size size){
    final rectangle=Offset.zero & size;
    canvas.drawRect(rectangle,Paint()..shader=LinearGradient(begin:Alignment.topLeft,end:Alignment.bottomRight,colors:original?[second,first]:[first,second]).createShader(rectangle));
    final white=Paint()..color=Colors.white.withValues(alpha:.19);
    final line=Paint()..color=Colors.white.withValues(alpha:.25)..strokeWidth=1.6;
    for(var x=0.0;x<size.width;x+=25){canvas.drawLine(Offset(x,0),Offset(x+85,size.height),line);}
    final orb=Offset(size.width*.78,size.height*.22);
    canvas.drawCircle(orb,size.height*.37,white);
    canvas.drawCircle(Offset(size.width*.14,size.height*.89),size.height*.4,white);
    for(var i=0;i<13;i++){
      final x=((i*67.0)%math.max(1,size.width));
      final y=((i*37.0)%math.max(1,size.height));
      canvas.drawCircle(Offset(x,y),i%3==0?3.2:1.6,Paint()..color=paperWhite.withValues(alpha:.62));
    }
    // A stylized scene silhouette is more recognizably visual than a flat text card.
    final shadow=Paint()..color=midnight.withValues(alpha:.21);
    switch(topic){
      case 'Government':
      case 'Campus':
      case 'Election':
        final roof=Path()..moveTo(size.width*.19,size.height*.73)..lineTo(size.width*.49,size.height*.36)..lineTo(size.width*.79,size.height*.73)..close();
        canvas.drawPath(roof,shadow);
        for(var j=0;j<5;j++){
          final x=size.width*(.28+j*.105);
          canvas.drawRRect(RRect.fromRectAndRadius(Rect.fromLTWH(x,size.height*.69,size.width*.05,size.height*.2),const Radius.circular(3)),shadow);
        }
        break;
      case 'Drag Race':
        for(var i=0;i<4;i++){
          final p=Path()..moveTo(size.width*(i*.30),0)..lineTo(size.width*(.25+i*.23),size.height*.95)..lineTo(size.width*(.39+i*.22),size.height*.95)..close();
          canvas.drawPath(p,white);
        }
        break;
      case 'Basketball memorial':
        canvas.drawCircle(Offset(size.width*.5,size.height*.55),size.height*.52,Paint()..color=const Color(0xFFEA8B4B).withValues(alpha:.18));
        canvas.drawLine(Offset(0,size.height*.5),Offset(size.width,size.height*.5),line..strokeWidth=4);
        break;
      default:
        canvas.drawRRect(RRect.fromRectAndRadius(Rect.fromLTWH(size.width*.07,size.height*.55,size.width*.23,size.height*.4),const Radius.circular(9)),shadow);
        canvas.drawRRect(RRect.fromRectAndRadius(Rect.fromLTWH(size.width*.7,size.height*.38,size.width*.24,size.height*.52),const Radius.circular(9)),shadow);
    }
  }
  @override bool shouldRepaint(covariant _PosterPainter old)=>first!=old.first||second!=old.second||topic!=old.topic||original!=old.original;
}

n// ===== C:\FlutterProjects\Scamouflage_v1.3_MVC\lib\views\mascot.dart =====nimport 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../controllers/engine.dart';
